948__((PTM_1.1))

$$948

__&&948__$$1.1.1##__

vāṭiṉēṉ vāṭivaruntiṉēṉ maṉattāl
peruntuyariṭumpiyil piṟantu,
kūṭiṉēṉ kūṭiyiḷiyavarttammōṭu
avarttarum kalaviyēkaruti,
ōṭiṉēṉ ōṭiyuyvatōrp poruḷāl
uṇarveṉum perum patamf tirintu,
nāṭiṉēṉ nāṭi nāṉ kaṇṭukoṇṭēṉ
nārāyaṇā veṉṉum nāmam. (2)
[[((1.1.1))]]

$$949

__&&949__$$1.1.2##__

āviyē. amutē. eṉaniṉinturuki
avaravarppaṇimulituṇiyā,
pāviyēṉuṇarā tettaṉipakalum
paḻutupōyoḻintaṉanāḷkaḷ,
tūvicēraṉṉam tuṇiyoṭumpuṇarum
cūḻpuṉaṟkuṭantiyētoḻutu, eṉ
nāviṉāluyyanāṉ kaṇṭukoṇṭēṉ
nārāyaṇāveṉṉum nāmam. (2)
[[((1.1.2))]]

$$950

__&&950__$$1.1.3##__

cēmamēvēṇṭit tīviṉiperukkit
terivimāruruvamēmaruvi,
ūmaṉār kaṇṭakaṉavilumpaḻutāy
oḻintaṉakaḻintavannāḷkaḷ,
kāmaṉār tātinammuṭiyaṭikaḷ
tammaṭintārmaṉattiruppār,
nāmamnāṉuyya nāṉkaṇṭu koṇṭēṉ
nārāyaṇāveṉṉumnāmam.
[[((1.1.3))]]

$$951

__&&951__$$1.1.4##__

veṉṟiyē vēṇṭi vīḻporuṭ kiraṅki
vēṟkaṇār kalaviyē karuti,
niṉṟavā nillā neñciṉiyuṭiyēṉ
eṉceykēṉ neṭuvicumpaṇavum,
paṉṟiyā yaṉṟupārakaṅkīṇṭa
pāḻiyā ṉāḻiyāṉaruḷē,
naṉṟu nāṉuyya nāṉkaṇṭu koṇṭēṉ
nārāyaṇāveṉṉumnāmam.
[[((1.1.4))]]

$$952

__&&952__$$1.1.5##__

kaḷvaṉēṉāṉēṉpaṭiṟuceytiruppēṉ
kaṇṭavātiritantēṉēlum,
teḷḷiyēṉāṉēṉcelkatikkamintēṉ
cikkeṉattiruvaruḷpeṟṟēṉ,
uḷḷelāmurukikkural taḻuttoḻintēṉ
uṭampelāmkaṇṇanīrcōra,
naḷḷiruḷaḷavum pakalum nāṉaḻippaṉ
nārāyaṇāveṉṉumnāmam.
[[((1.1.5))]]

$$953

__&&953__$$1.1.6##__

empirāṉ enti eṉṉuṭiccuṟṟam
eṉakkaracu eṉṉuṭivāṇāḷ,
ampiṉāl arakkarverukkoḷanerukki
avaruyircekuttavemmaṇṇal,
vampulāñfcōlimāmatiḷ tañci
māmaṇikkōyilēvaṇaṅki,
nampikāḷ uyyanāṉ kaṇṭu koṇṭēṉ
nārāyaṇāveṉṉumnāmam. (2)
[[((1.1.6))]]

$$954

__&&954__$$1.1.7##__

iṟpiṟappaṟiyīr ivaravareṉṉīr
iṉṉatōrttaṉmiyeṉṟuṇarīr,
kaṟpakampulavarkaḷikaṇeṉṟūlakil
kaṇṭavātoṇṭarippāṭum,
coṟpuruḷāḷīrcollukēṉvammiṉ
cūḻpuṉaṟkuṭantiyētoḻumiṉ,
naṟporuḷkāṇmiṉ pāṭi nīruymmiṉ
nārāyaṇāveṉṉumnāmam.
[[((1.1.7))]]

$$955

__&&955__$$1.1.8##__

kaṟṟilēṉ kalikaḷ aimpulaṉ karutum
karuttuḷē tiruttiṉēṉ maṉatti,
peṟṟilēṉ ataṉāl pētiyēṉ naṉmi
perunilattāruyirkkellām,
ceṟṟamēvēṇṭittiritaruvēṉ tavirntēṉ
celkatikkuyyumāṟeṇṇi,
naṟṟuṇiyākappaṟṟiṉēṉ aṭiyēṉ
nārāyaṇāveṉṉumnāmam.
[[((1.1.8))]]

$$956

__&&956__$$1.1.9##__

kulamtarum celvam tantiṭum aṭiyār
paṭutuyarāyiṉavellam,
nilantarañceyyum nīḷvicumparuḷum
aruḷoṭuperunilamaḷikkum,
valantarummaṟṟuntantiṭum peṟṟa
tāyiṉu māyiṉaceyyum,
nalantaruñcolli nāṉ kaṇṭukoṇṭēṉ
nārāyaṇāveṉṉumnāmam. (2)
[[((1.1.9))]]

$$957

__&&957__$$1.1.10##__

mañculāñ cōli vaṇṭaṟi mānīr
maṅkiyārvāḷff kalikaṉṟi,
ceñcolāleṭutta teyva naṉfmāli
ivikoṇṭu cikkeṉattoṇṭīr.,
tuñcumpōtu aḻimiṉ tuyarvaril niṉimiṉ
tuyarilīr collilum naṉṟām,
nañcutāṉ kaṇṭīr nammuṭiviṉikku
nārāyaṇāveṉṉum nāmam. (2)
[[((1.1.10))]]

$$958

__&&958__$$1.2.1##__

vālimāvalattoruvaṉatuṭalkeṭa
varicilivaḷivittu,aṉṟu
ēlanāṟutaṇtaṭampoḻiliṭampeṟa
iruntanalimayyattuḷ,
ālimāmukilatirtara aruvari
akaṭuṟamukaṭēṟi,
pīlimāmayilnaṭañceyumtaṭañcuṉip
piriticeṉṟaṭineñcē. (2)
[[((1.2.1))]]

$$959

__&&959__$$1.2.2##__

kalaṅkamākkaṭalarikulampaṇiceyya
aruvariyaṇikaṭṭi,
ilaṅkimānakarppoṭiceytavaṭikaḷtām
iruntanallimayattu,
vilaṅkalpōlvaṉaviṟaliruñciṉattaṉa
vēḻaṅkaḷtuyarkkūra,
pilaṅkoḷvāḷeyiṟṟariyavitiritaru
piriticeṉṟaṭineñcē.
[[((1.2.2))]]

$$960

__&&960__$$1.2.3##__

ṟiḷaṅkoṭitiṟattu, āyar
iṭikoḷveṅkuraliṉaviṭiyaṭarttavaṉ
iruntanallimayattu,
kaṭikoḷvēṅkiyiṉnaṟumalaramaḷiyiṉ

$$961

__&&961__$$1.2.4##__

maṟaṅkoḷāḷariyuruveṉaveruvara

tiṟantu,vāṉavarmaṇimuṭipaṇitara
iruntanallimayattuḷ,
iṟaṅkiyēṉaṅkaḷvaḷimaruppiṭantiṭak
kiṭantarukerivīcum,

$$962

__&&962__$$1.2.5##__

kariceymākkaṭalkiṭantavaṉ kaṉikaḻal
amararkaḷtoḻutētta,

amarntanallimayattu,

$$963

__&&963__$$1.2.6##__

iruntanallimayattu,

$$964

__&&964__$$1.2.7##__

eṭṭumiṭṭimiyōrkaḷ,

$$965

__&&965__$$1.2.8##__

aruvariyimayattu,

piriticeṉṟaṭineñcē.
[[((1.2.8))]]

$$966

__&&966__$$1.2.9##__

iruntanallimayattu,

$$967

__&&967__$$1.2.10##__

avimuḻaṅkiṭa,kaḷiṟeṉṟu

__&&1029__$$1.9.2##__

māṉēykaṇmaṭavār mayakkilpaṭṭu mānilattu,
nāṉēnāṉāvita narakampukumpāvamceytēṉ,
tēṉēypūmpoḻilcūḻ tiruvēṅkaṭamāmali, eṉ
āṉāy vantaṭintēṉ aṭiyēṉiyāṭkoṇṭaruḷē.
[[((1.9.2))]]

$$1030

__&&1030__$$1.9.3##__

koṉṟēṉpalluyirik kuṟikkōḷoṉṟilāmiyiṉāl,
eṉṟēṉumirantārkku iṉitākavurittaṟiyēṉ,
kuṉṟēymēkamatir kuḷirmāmalivēṅkaṭavā.,
aṉṟēvantaṭintēṉ aṭiyēṉiyāṭkoṇṭaruḷē.
[[((1.9.3))]]

$$1031

__&&1031__$$1.9.4##__

kulantāṉettaṉiyum piṟantēyiṟanteyttoḻintēṉ,
nalantāṉoṉṟumilēṉ nallatōraṟamceytumilēṉ,
nilamtōynīḷmukilcēr neṟiyārttiruvēṅkaṭavā.,
alantēṉvantaṭintēṉ aṭiyēṉiyāṭkoṇṭaruḷē.
[[((1.9.4))]]

$$1032

__&&1032__$$1.9.5##__

eppāvampalavum iviyēceytiḷittoḻintēṉ,
tuppā. niṉṉaṭiyē toṭarntēttavumkiṟkiṉṟilēṉ,
ceppārttiṇvaricūḻ tiruvēṅkaṭamāmali, eṉ
appā. vantaṭintēṉ aṭiyēṉiyāṭkoṇṭaruḷē.
[[((1.9.5))]]

$$1033

__&&1033__$$1.9.6##__

maṉṉāynīrerikāl mañculāvumākācamumām,
puṇṇārākkitaṉṉuḷ pulampittaḷarnteyttoḻintēṉ,
viṇṇārnīḷcikara viriyārttiruvēṅkaṭavā.,
aṇṇā. vantaṭintēṉ aṭiyēṉiyāṭkoṇṭaruḷē.
[[((1.9.6))]]

$$1034

__&&1034__$$1.9.7##__

teriyeṉpālakaṉāyp palatīmikaḷceytumiṭṭēṉ,
periyēṉāyiṉa piṉ piṟarkkēyuḻittēḻiyāṉēṉ,
karicērppūmpoḻilcūḻ kaṉamāmalivēṅkaṭavā.,
ariyē. vantaṭintēṉ aṭiyēṉiyāṭkoṇṭaruḷē.
[[((1.9.7))]]

$$1035

__&&1035__$$1.9.8##__

nōṟṟēṉpalpiṟavi uṉṉikkāṇpatōrāciyiṉāl,
ēṟṟēṉippiṟappē yiṭaruṟṟaṉaṉemperumāṉ.,
kōltēṉ pāyntoḻukum kuḷircōlicūḻvēṅkaṭavā.,
āṟṟēṉvantaṭintēṉ aṭiyēṉiyāṭkoṇṭaruḷē.
[[((1.9.8))]]

$$1036

__&&1036__$$1.9.9##__

paṟṟēloṉṟumilēṉ pāvamēceytupāviyāṉēṉ,
maṟṟēloṉṟaṟiyēṉ māyaṉē. eṅkaḷmātavaṉē.,
kaltēṉpāyntoḻukum kamalaccuṉivēṅkaṭavā.,
aṟṟēṉvantaṭintēṉ aṭiyēṉiyāṭkoṇṭaruḷē.
[[((1.9.9))]]

$$1037

__&&1037__$$1.9.10##__

kaṇṇāyēḻulakukku uyirāyaveṅkārvaṇṇaṉi,
viṇṇōrttāmparavum poḻilvēṅkaṭavētiyaṉi,
tiṇṇārmāṭaṅkaḷ cūḻ tirumaṅkiyarkkōṉkaliyaṉ,
paṇṇārppāṭalpattum payilvārkkillipāvaṅkaḷē.
[[((1.9.10))]]

1038__((PTM_1.10))

$$1038

__&&1038__$$1.10.1##__

kaṇṇārkkaṭalcūḻ ilaṅkikkiṟivantaṉ,
tiṇṇākampiḷakkac caramcelavuyttāy.,
viṇṇōrttoḻum vēṅkaṭamāmalimēya,
aṇṇā. aṭiyēṉ iṭarikkaḷiyāyē.
[[((1.10.1))]]

$$1039

__&&1039__$$1.10.2##__

ilaṅkippatikku aṉṟīṟiyāya, arakkar
kulamkeṭṭavarmāḷak koṭippuḷtirittāy.,
vilaṅkalkuṭumit tiruvēṅkaṭammēya,
alaṅkaltuḷapamuṭiyāy. aruḷāyē.
[[((1.10.2))]]

$$1040

__&&1040__$$1.10.3##__

nīrārkkaṭalum nilaṉummuḻutuṇṭu,
ērālamiḷantaḷirmēl tuyilentāy.,
cīrār tiruvēṅkaṭamāmalimēya,
ārāvamutē. aṭiyēṟkaruḷāyē.
[[((1.10.3))]]

$$1041

__&&1041__$$1.10.4##__

uṇṭāyuṟimēl naṟuṉeyyamutāka,
koṇṭāykuṟaḷāy nilamīraṭiyālē,
viṇtōycikarat tiruvēṅkaṭammēya,
aṇṭā. aṭiyēṉukku aruḷpuriyāyē.
[[((1.10.4))]]

$$1042

__&&1042__$$1.10.5##__

tūṇāyataṉūṭu ariyāyvantutōṉṟi,
pēṇāvavuṇaṉuṭalam piḷantiṭṭāy.,
cēṇār tiruvēṅkaṭamāmalimēya,
kōṇākaṇiyāy. kuṟikkoḷḷeṉinīyē.
[[((1.10.5))]]

$$1043

__&&1043__$$1.10.6##__

maṉṉā immaṉicappiṟaviyinīkki,
taṉṉākit taṉṉiṉaruḷceyyumtalivaṉ,
miṉṉārmukilcēr tiruvēṅkaṭammēya,
eṉṉāṉiyeṉṉappaṉ eṉṉeñciluḷāṉē.
[[((1.10.6))]]

$$1044

__&&1044__$$1.10.7##__

māṉēymaṭanōkkitiṟattu etirvanta,
āṉēḻviṭiceṟṟa aṇivarittōḷā.,
tēṉē. tiruvēṅkaṭamāmalimēya,
kōṉē. eṉmaṉam kuṭikoṇṭiruntāyē.
[[((1.10.7))]]

$$1045

__&&1045__$$1.10.8##__

cēyaṉaṇiyaṉ eṉacintiyuḷniṉṟa
māyaṉ, maṇivāḷoḷi veṇṭaraḷaṅkaḷ,
vēyviṇṭutir vēṅkaṭamāmalimēya,
āyaṉaṭiyallatu maṟṟaṟiyēṉē.
[[((1.10.8))]]

$$1046

__&&1046__$$1.10.9##__

vantāyeṉmaṉampukuntāy maṉṉiniṉṟāy,
nantātakoḻuñcuṭarē yeṅkaḷ nampī.,
cintāmaṇiyē tiruvēṅkaṭammēya
entāy., iṉiyāṉuṉṉi yeṉṟum viṭēṉē.
[[((1.10.9))]]

$$1047

__&&1047__$$1.10.10##__

villārmali vēṅkaṭamāmalimēya,
mallārttiraṭōḷ maṇivaṇṇaṉammāṉi,
kallārttiraṭōḷ kaliyaṉcoṉṉamāli,
vallāravar vāṉavarākuvarttāmē.
[[((1.10.10))]]

1048__((PTM_2.1))

$$1048

__&&1048__$$2.1.1##__

vāṉavar taṅkaḷ cinti pōlēṉ
neñcamē. iṉituvantu, mātava
māṉavar taṅkaḷ cinti yamarntuṟi kiṉṟaventi,
kāṉavariṭu kārakiṟpuki yōṅku vēṅkaṭam mēvi, māṇkuṟaḷ
āṉa antaṇaṟ kiṉṟaṭi mittoḻil pūṇṭāyē. (2)
[[((2.1.1))]]

$$1049

__&&1049__$$2.1.2##__

uṟavu cuṟṟameṉ ṟoṉṟilā voruvaṉ
ukantavar tammi, maṇmicip
piṟavi yēkeṭup pāṉatu kaṇṭeṉ neñcameṉpāy,
kuṟavar mātarka ḷōṭu vaṇṭu
kuṟiñci maruḷici pāṭum vēṅkaṭattu,
aṟava nāyakaṟ kiṉṟaṭi mittoḻil pūṇṭāyē.
[[((2.1.2))]]

$$1050

__&&1050__$$2.1.3##__

iṇṭi yāyiṉa koṇṭu toṇṭarkaḷ
ēttu vāruṟa vōṭum, vāṉiṭik
koṇṭu pōyiṭavu matukaṇṭeṉ neñcameṉpāy,
vaṇṭu vāḻvaṭa vēṅkaṭamali
kōyil koṇṭata ṉōṭum, mīmici
aṇṭa māṇṭirup pāṟkaṭi mittoḻil pūṇṭāyē.
[[((2.1.3))]]

$$1051

__&&1051__$$2.1.4##__

pāvi yātucey tāyeṉ ṉeñcamē.
paṇṭu toṇṭucey tāri, maṇmici
mēvi yāṭkoṇṭu pōyvi cumpēṟa vikkumenti,
kōvi nāyakaṉ koṇṭa luntuyar
vēṅka ṭamali yāṇṭu, vāṉavar
āvi yāyirup pāṟkaṭi mittoḻil pūṇṭāyē.
[[((2.1.4))]]

$$1052

__&&1052__$$2.1.5##__

poṅku pōtiyum piṇṭi yumuṭip
puttar nōṉpiyar paḷḷi yuḷḷuṟi,
taṅkaḷ tēvarum tāṅkaḷu mēyāka eṉneñcameṉpāy
eṅkum vāṉavar tāṉa varniṟin
tēttum vēṅkaṭam mēvi niṉṟaruḷ,
aṅka ṇāyakaṟ kiṉṟaṭi mittoḻil pūṇṭāyē.
[[((2.1.5))]]

$$1053

__&&1053__$$2.1.6##__

tuvari yāṭiyar maṭṭi yarcamaṇ
toṇṭar kaḷmaṇṭi yuṇṭu piṉṉarum,
tamarum tāṅkaḷumē taṭikkeṉ neñcameṉpāy,
kavari mākkaṇam cērum vēṅkaṭam
kōyil koṇṭakaṇ ṇārvi cumpiṭi,
amara nāyakaṟaku iṉṟaṭimittoḻil pūṇṭāyē.
[[((2.1.6))]]

$$1054

__&&1054__$$2.1.7##__

tarukki ṉālcamaṇ ceytu cōṟutaṇ
tayiriṉāltiraḷi,mi ṭaṟṟiṭi
nerukku vāralak kaṇatu kaṇṭeṉ neñcameṉpāy,
maruṭkaḷ vaṇṭukaḷ pāṭum vēṅkaṭam
kōyil koṇṭata ṉōṭum, vāṉiṭi
arukkaṉ mēviniṟ pāṟkaṭi mittoḻil pūṇṭāyē.
[[((2.1.7))]]

$$1055

__&&1055__$$2.1.8##__

cēyaṉaṇiyaṉ ciṟiyaṉ periya ṉeṉpatu
cilarppēcak kēṭṭirun
tē,eṉ ṉeñcameṉ pāy,.eṉak koṉṟu collātē,
vēykaḷ niṉṟuveṇ mutta mēcori
vēṅka ṭamali kōyil mēviya,
āyar nāyakaṟ kiṉṟaṭi mittoḻil pūṇṭāyē.
[[((2.1.8))]]

$$1056

__&&1056__$$2.1.9##__

kūṭi yāṭi yurittatē yurittāyeṉ
neñcameṉ pāy. tuṇintukēḷ,
pāṭi yāṭip palarum paṇintēttik kāṇkilā,
āṭu tāmari yōṉu mīcaṉum
amar kōṉumniṉ ṟēttum,vēṅkaṭattu
āṭu kūttaṉuk kiṉṟaṭi mittoḻil pūṇṭāyē.
[[((2.1.9))]]

$$1057

__&&1057__$$2.1.10##__

miṉṉu māmukil mēvu taṇtiru vēṅka
ṭamali kōyil mēviya,
aṉṉamāy nikaḻnta amarar perumāṉi,
kaṉṉi māmatiḷ maṅki yarkkali
kaṉṟi yintami ḻālu ritta, im
maṉṉu pāṭalval lārkkiṭa mākum vāṉulakē. (2)
[[((2.1.10))]]

1058__((PTM_2.2))

$$1058

__&&1058__$$2.2.1##__

kāci yāṭi mūṭiyōṭik kātalcey tāṉavaṉūr,
nāca māka nampavalla nampi namperumāṉ,
vēyi ṉaṉṉa tōḷfmaṭavār veṇṇeyuṇ ṭāṉivaṉeṉṟu
ēca niṉṟa vemperumā ṉevvuḷ kiṭantāṉē. (2)
[[((2.2.1))]]

$$1059

__&&1059__$$2.2.2##__

tiya lāḷmēl kātalceyta tāṉavaṉ vāḷarakkaṉ,
poyyi lāta poṉmuṭika ḷoṉpatō ṭoṉṟum,aṉṟu
ceyta vempōr taṉṉi laṅkōr ceñcarat tāluruḷa,
eyta venti yemperumā ṉevvuḷ kiṭantāṉē.
[[((2.2.2))]]

$$1060

__&&1060__$$2.2.3##__

muṉṉōr tūtu vāṉarattiṉ vāyil moḻintu,arakkaṉ
maṉṉūr taṉṉi vāḷiyiṉāl māḷa muṉintu avaṉē
piṉṉōr tūta ṉātimaṉṉark kākip perunilattār,
iṉṉār tūta ṉeṉaniṉṟā ṉevvuḷ kiṭantāṉē.
[[((2.2.3))]]

$$1061

__&&1061__$$2.2.4##__

panta ṇinta melviralāḷ pāvitaṉ kāraṇattāl,
venti ṟalē ṟēḻumveṉṟa vēntaṉ viripukaḻcēr,
nantaṉ minta ṉākavākum nampi namperumāṉ,
enti tanti tamperumāṉevvuḷ kiṭantāṉē.
[[((2.2.4))]]

$$1062

__&&1062__$$2.2.5##__

pāla nāki ñālamēḻu
muṇṭupaṇ ṭālilimēl,
cāla nāḷum paḷḷikoḷḷum
tāmarik kaṇṇaṉeṇṇil,
nīla mārvaṇ ṭuṇṭuvāḻum
neytalan taṇkaḻaṉi,
ēla nāṟum pimpuṟavi
levvuḷ kiṭantāṉē.
[[((2.2.5))]]

$$1063

__&&1063__$$2.2.6##__

cōtta nampi yeṉṟutoṇṭar miṇṭit toṭarntaḻikkum,
āttaṉamfpi ceṅkaṇampi yākilum tēvarkkellām,
mūtta nampi mukkaṇampi yeṉṟu muṉivarttoḻu-
tēttum, nampi yemperumā ṉevvuḷ kiṭantāṉē.
[[((2.2.6))]]

$$1064

__&&1064__$$2.2.7##__

tiṅka ḷappu vāṉerikālāki, ticimukaṉār
taṅka ḷappaṉ cāmiyappaṉ pākat tirunta,vaṇṭuṇ
toṅka lappu nīṇmuṭiyāṉ cūḻkaḻal cūṭaniṉṟa,
eṅka ḷappa ṉemperumāṉ evvuḷ kiṭantāṉē.
[[((2.2.7))]]

$$1065

__&&1065__$$2.2.8##__

muṉivaṉ mūrtti mūvarāki
vētam viritturitta
puṉitaṉ, pūvi vaṇṇaṉaṇṇal
puṇṇiyaṉ viṇṇavarkōṉ,
taṉiyaṉ cēyaṉ tāṉoruvaṉ
ākilum taṉṉaṭiyārkku
iṉiyaṉ, enti yemperumāṉ
evvuḷ kiṭantāṉē.
[[((2.2.8))]]

$$1066

__&&1066__$$2.2.9##__

panti rukkum melviralāḷ
pāvi paṉimalarāḷ,
vanti rukkum mārvaṉnīla
mēṉi maṇivaṇṇaṉ,
anta rattil vāḻum vāṉōr
nāyaka ṉāyaminta,
inti raṟkum tamperumā
ṉevvuḷ kiṭantāṉē.
[[((2.2.9))]]

$$1067

__&&1067__$$2.2.10##__

iṇṭi koṇṭu toṇṭarētta
evvuḷ kiṭantāṉi,
vaṇṭu pāṭum pipuṟavil
maṅkiyar kōṉkaliyaṉ,
koṇṭa cīrāl taṇṭamiḻcey
māliyī rintumvallār,
aṇṭa māḷva tāṇiyaṉṟē
lāḷva rarulakē. (2)
[[((2.2.10))]]

1068__((PTM_2.3))

$$1068

__&&1068__$$2.3.1##__

viṟperu viḻavum kañcaṉum mallum
vēḻamum pākaṉum vīḻa,
ceṟṟavaṉ ṟaṉṉi, purameri ceyta
civaṉuṟu tuyarkaḷi tēvi,
paṟṟalar vīyak kōlkiyil koṇṭu
pārttaṉṟaṉ tērmuṉniṉ ṟāṉi,
ciṟṟavi paṇiyāl muṭituṟan tāṉit
tiruvallik kēṇikkaṇ ṭēṉē. (2)
[[((2.3.1))]]

$$1069

__&&1069__$$2.3.2##__

vētatti vētat tiṉcuvip payaṉi
viḻumiya muṉivarkaḷ viḻuṅkum,
kōtiliṉ kaṉiyi nantaṉār kaḷiṟṟik
kuvalayat tōrtoḻu tēttum,
ātiyi yamuti yeṉṉi yāḷuṭi
appaṉi oppava rillā
mātarkaḷ vāḻum, māṭamā mayilit
tiruvallik kēṇikkaṇ ṭēṉē. (2)
[[((2.3.2))]]

$$1070

__&&1070__$$2.3.3##__

vañcaṉi ceyyat tāyuru vāki
vantapē yalaṟimaṇ cēra,
nañcamar muliyū ṭuyirceka vuṇṭa
nātaṉit tāṉavar kūṟṟi,
viñcivā ṉavarcā raṇarcittar viyantu
tuticeyyap peṇṇuru vāki,
añcuvi yamuta maṉṟaḷit tāṉit
tiruvallik kēṇikkaṇ ṭēṉē.
[[((2.3.3))]]

$$1071

__&&1071__$$2.3.4##__

intira ṉukkeṉ ṟāyarka ḷeṭutta
eḻilviḻa vilpaḻa naṭicey,
mantira vitiyil pūcaṉi peṟātu
maḻipoḻin tiṭattaḷarntu, āyar
antamō ṭiṉavā niritaḷa rāmal
emperu māṉaru ḷeṉṉa,
antamil variyāl maḻitaṭut tāṉit
tiruvallik kēṇikkaṇ ṭēṉē.
[[((2.3.4))]]

$$1072

__&&1072__$$2.3.5##__

intuṇip patumat talarmakaḷ taṉakkum
iṉpaṉnaṟ puvitaṉak kiṟivaṉ,
tantuṇi yāyar pāvinap piṉṉi
taṉakkiṟi maṟṟiyōrk kellām
vaṉtuṇi, pañca pāṇṭavark kāki
vāyuri tūtuceṉ ṟiyaṅkum
eṉtuṇi, enti tantitam māṉit
tiruvallik kēṇikkaṇ ṭēṉē.
[[((2.3.5))]]

$$1073

__&&1073__$$2.3.6##__

antakaṉ ciṟuva ṉaracartta maracaṟ
kiḷiyava ṉaṇiyiḻi yicceṉṟu,
entamak kurimi cey eṉat tariyātu
emperu māṉaruḷ eṉṉa,
cantamal kuḻalāḷ alakkaṇnūṟ ṟuvarttam
peṇṭiru meytinū liḻappa,
intiraṉ ciṟuvaṉ tērmuṉniṉ ṟāṉit
tiruvallik kēṇikkaṇ ṭēṉē.
[[((2.3.6))]]

$$1074

__&&1074__$$2.3.7##__

parataṉum tampi catturuk kaṉaṉṉum
ilakkuma ṉōṭumi tiliyum
iravunaṉ pakalum tuticeyya niṉṟa
irāvaṇān takaṉiyem māṉi,
kuravamē kamaḻum kuḷirppoḻi lūṭu
kuyiloṭu mayilkaḷniṉ ṟāla,
iraviyiṉ katirkaḷ nuḻitalcey taṟiyāt
tiruvallik kēṇikkaṇ ṭēṉē. (2)
[[((2.3.7))]]

$$1075

__&&1075__$$2.3.8##__

paḷḷiyi lōti vantataṉ ciṟuvaṉ
vāyilō rāyira nāmam,
oḷḷiya vākip pōtavāṅ kataṉuk
koṉṟumōr poṟuppila ṉāki,
piḷḷiyic cīṟi vekuṇṭutūṇ puṭippap
piṟiyeyiṟ ṟaṉalviḻip pēḻvāy,
teḷḷiya ciṅka mākiya tēvit
tiruvallik kēṇikkaṇ ṭēṉē. (2)
[[((2.3.8))]]

$$1076

__&&1076__$$2.3.9##__

mīṉamar poyki nāṇmalar koyvāṉ
vēṭkiyi ṉōṭuceṉ ṟiḻinta,
kāṉamar vēḻam kiyeṭut talaṟak
karāvataṉ kāliṉik katuva,
āṉiyiṉ tuyaram tīrappuḷ ḷūrntu
ceṉṟuniṉ ṟāḻitoṭ ṭāṉi,
tēṉamar cōli māṭamā mayilit
tiruvallik kēṇikkaṇ ṭēṉē. (2)
[[((2.3.9))]]

$$1077

__&&1077__$$2.3.10##__

maṉṉutaṇ poḻilum vāviyum matiḷum
māṭamā ḷikiyummaṇ ṭapamum,
teṉṉaṉtoṇ ṭiyarkkōṉ ceytanaṉmayilit
tiruvallik kēṇiniṉ ṟāṉi,
kaṉṉinaṉ māṭa maṅkiyar talivaṉ
kāmaru cīrkkali kaṉṟi,
coṉṉacoṉ māli pattuṭaṉ vallār
cukamiṉi tāḷvarvā ṉulakē. (2)
[[((2.3.10))]]

1078__((PTM_2.4))

$$1078

__&&1078__$$2.4.1##__

aṉṟāyarku lakkoṭi yōṭaṇimā
malarmaṅkiyo ṭaṉpaḷavi,avuṇark
keṉṟāṉu mirakkami lātavaṉukkuk
kuṟiyumiṭa māvatu,irumpoḻilcūḻ
naṉṟāyapu ṉalnaṟi yūrttiruvā
likuṭanti taṭantikaḻ kōvalnakar,
niṉṟāṉirun tāṉkiṭan tāṉnaṭantāṟ
kiṭammāmali yāvatu nīrmaliyē. (2)
[[((2.4.1))]]

$$1079

__&&1079__$$2.4.2##__

kāṇṭāvaṉa meṉpator kāṭamarark
kariyaṉatu kaṇṭavaṉ niṟka,muṉē
mūṇṭāraḻa luṇṇamu ṉintatuvum
atuvaṉṟiyum muṉṉula kampoṟitīrt
tāṇṭāṉ,avuṇaṉavaṉ mārvakalam
ukirālvaki rākamu ṉintu, ariyāy
nīṇṭāṉkuṟa ḷākini mirntavaṉuk
kiṭammāmali yāvatu nīrmaliyē.
[[((2.4.2))]]

$$1080

__&&1080__$$2.4.3##__

alamaṉṉu maṭalcuri caṅkameṭut
taṭalāḻiyi ṉālaṇi yāruruvilf,
pulamaṉṉu vaṭampuṉi koṅkiyiṉāḷ
poṟitīramu ṉāḷaṭu vāḷamaril,
palamaṉṉar paṭaccuṭa rāḻiyiṉip
pakalōṉmaṟi yappaṇi koṇṭu,aṇicēr
nilamaṉṉaṉu māyula kāṇṭavaṉuk
kiṭammāmali yāvatu nīrmaliyē.
[[((2.4.3))]]

$$1081

__&&1081__$$2.4.4##__

tāṅkātatō rāḷari yāyavuṇaṉ -
ṟaṉivīṭa muṉintava ṉālamarum,
pūṅkōtiyar poṅkeri mūḻkaviḷit
tatuvaṉṟiyum veṉṟikoḷ vāḷamaril,
pāṅkākamuṉ aivaro ṭaṉpaḷavip
patiṟṟintiraṭ ṭippaṭi vēntarpaṭa,
nīṅkācceru vilniṟi kāttavaṉuk
kiṭammāmali yāvatu nīrmaliyē.
[[((2.4.4))]]

$$1082

__&&1082__$$2.4.5##__

māluṅkaṭa lārama likkuvaṭiṭ
ṭaṇikaṭṭi varampuruvamaticēr
kōlamati ḷāyavi laṅkikeṭap
paṭitoṭṭoru kālama rilatira,
kālamitu veṉṟayaṉ vāḷiyiṉāl
katirnīṇmuṭipattu maṟuttamarum,
nīlamukil vaṇṇaṉe makkiṟivaṟ
kiṭammāmali yāvatu nīrmaliyē.
[[((2.4.5))]]

$$1083

__&&1083__$$2.4.6##__

pārārula kumpaṉi mālvariyum
kaṭalumcuṭa rumivi yuṇṭum, eṉak
kārā teṉa niṉṟava ṉemperumāṉ
alinīrula kukkaracākiya,ap-
pērāṉimuṉintamuṉikkariyaṉ
piṟarilli nuṉakkeṉu melliyiṉāṉ,
nīrārppē rāṉneṭu mālavaṉuk
kiṭammāmali yāvatu nīrmaliyē.
[[((2.4.6))]]

$$1084

__&&1084__$$2.4.7##__

pukarāruru vākimuṉintavaṉip
pukaḻvīṭa muṉintuyi ruṇṭu,acuraṉ
nakarāyiṉa pāḻpaṭa nāmameṟin-
tatuvaṉṟiyum veṉṟikoḷ vāḷavuṇaṉ,
pakarātava ṉāyira nāmamaṭip
paṇiyātava ṉippaṇi yāmalaril,
nikarāyavaṉ neñciṭan tāṉavaṉuk
kiṭammāmali yāvatu nīrmaliyē.
[[((2.4.7))]]

$$1085

__&&1085__$$2.4.8##__

piccacciṟu pīlipi ṭittulakil
piṇantiṉmaṭavāravar pōl,aṅṅaṉē
accamilar nāṇila rātaṉmiyāl
avarceyki veṟuttaṇi māmalarttūy,
naccinama ṉāraṭi yāminamak
karuḷcey eṉavuḷkuḻin tārvamoṭu,
niccamniṉivārkkaruḷ ceyyumavaṟ
kiṭammāmali yāvatu nīrmaliyē.
[[((2.4.8))]]

$$1086

__&&1086__$$2.4.9##__

pēcumaḷa vaṉṟitu vammiṉnamar.
piṟarkkēṭpataṉ muṉpaṇi vārviṉikaḷ,
nācamatu ceytiṭum ātaṉmiyāl
atuvēnama tuyviṭam nāṇmalarmēl
vācamaṇi vaṇṭaṟi pimpuṟavil
maṉamintoṭu nintuḻal vār,matiyil
nīcaravar ceṉṟaṭi yātavaṉuk
kiṭammāmali yāvatu nīrmaliyē.
[[((2.4.9))]]

$$1087

__&&1087__$$2.4.10##__

neṭumālavaṉ mēviya nīrmalimēl
nilavumpukaḻ maṅkiyar kōṉ,amaril
kaṭamākaḷi yāṉivallāṉ kaliyaṉ
oliceytamiḻ mālival lārkku,uṭaṉē
viṭumālviṉi vēṇṭiṭil mēlulakum
eḷitāyiṭu maṉṟiyi laṅkolicēr,
koṭumākaṭal viyaka māṇṭumatik
kuṭimaṉṉava rāyaṭi kūṭuvarē. (2)
[[((2.4.10))]]

1088__((PTM_2.5))

$$1088

__&&1088__$$2.5.1##__

pārāya tuṇṭumiḻnta pavaḷattūṇip
pāṭukaṭali lamutattip parivāykīṇṭa
cīrāṉi, emmāṉit toṇṭartaṅkaḷ
cintiyuḷḷē muḷitteḻunta tīṅkarumpiṉi,
pōrāṉik kompocitta pōrēṟṟiṉip
puṇarmaruta miṟanaṭanta poṟkuṉṟiṉi,
kārāṉi yiṭarkkaṭinta kaṟpakattik
kaṇṭatunāṉ kaṭalmallit talacayaṉattē. (2)
[[((2.5.1))]]

$$1089

__&&1089__$$2.5.2##__

pūṇṭavattam piṟarkkaṭintu toṇṭupaṭṭup
poynñūli meynñūleṉ ṟeṉṟumōti
māṇṭu,avattam pōkātē vammiṉenti
eṉvaṇaṅkap paṭuvāṉi, kaṇaṅkaḷēttum
nīṇṭavattik karumukili emmāṉtaṉṉi
niṉṟavūr nittilattit tottārcōli,
kāṇṭavattik kaṉalerivāyp peyvittāṉik
kaṇṭatunāṉ kaṭalmallit talacayaṉattē. (2)
[[((2.5.2))]]

$$1090

__&&1090__$$2.5.3##__

uṭampuruvil mūṉṟoṟāy mūrttivēṟāy
ulakuyya niṉṟāṉi,aṉṟupēycci
viṭamparuku vittakaṉik kaṉṟumēyttu
viḷiyāṭa vallāṉi varimīkāṉil,
taṭamparuku karumukilit tañcikkōyil
tavaneṟikkōr peruneṟiyi viyaṅkākkum,
kaṭumparimēl kaṟkiyināṉkaṇṭukoṇṭēṉ
kaṭipoḻilcūḻ kaṭaṉfmallit talacayaṉattē.
[[((2.5.3))]]

$$1091

__&&1091__$$2.5.4##__

pēyttāyi muliyuṇṭa piḷḷitaṉṉip
piṇimaruppil karuṅkaḷiṟṟip piṇimāṉṉōkkiṉ,
āyttāyar tayirveṇṇe yamarntakōvi
antaṇartam amutattik kuravimuṉṉē
kōttāṉi, kuṭamāṭu kūttaṉṟaṉṉik
kōkulaṅkaḷ taḷarāmal kuṉṟamēntik
kāttāṉi, emmāṉik kaṇṭukoṇṭēṉ
kaṭipoḻilcūḻ kaṭalmallit talacayaṉattē.
[[((2.5.4))]]

$$1092

__&&1092__$$2.5.5##__

pāyntāṉit tiricakaṭam pāṟivīḻap
pālakaṉā yāliliyil paḷḷiyiṉpam
ēyntāṉi, ilaṅkoḷicēr maṇikkuṉṟaṉṉa
īriraṇṭu mālvarittō ḷemmāṉṟaṉṉi,
tōyntāṉi nilamakaḷtōḷ tūtiṟceṉṟap
poyyaṟivāyp pukappeyta mallarmaṅkak
kāyttāṉi, emmāṉik kaṇṭukoṇṭēṉ
kaṭipoḻilcūḻ kaṭalmallit talacayaṉattē.
[[((2.5.5))]]

$$1093

__&&1093__$$2.5.6##__

kiṭantāṉit taṭaṅkaṭaluḷ paṇaṅkaḷmēvik
kiḷarppoṟiya maṟitiriya vataṉiṉpiṉṉē
paṭarntāṉip, paṭumatatta kaḷiṟṟiṉkompu
paṟittāṉip pāriṭatti eyiṟukīṟa
iṭantāṉi, vaḷimaruppi ṉēṉamāki
irunilaṉum peruvicumpu meytāvaṇṇam
kaṭantāṉi, emmāṉik kaṇṭukoṇṭēṉ
kaṭipoḻilcūḻ kaṭalmallit talacayaṉattē.
[[((2.5.6))]]

$$1094

__&&1094__$$2.5.7##__

pēṇāta valiyarakkar meliyavaṉṟu
peruvarittō ḷiṟanerittaṉ ṟavuṇarkkōṉi,
pūṇākam piḷaveṭutta pōrvallōṉip
porukaṭaluḷ tuyilamarnta puḷḷūrtiyi
ūṇākap pēymulinañ cuṇṭāṉ taṉṉi
uḷḷuvā ruḷḷattē yuṟikiṉṟāṉi,
kāṇātu tiritaruvēṉ kaṇṭukoṇṭēṉ
kaṭipoḻilcūḻ kaṭaṉfmallit talacayaṉattē.
[[((2.5.7))]]

$$1095

__&&1095__$$2.5.8##__

peṇṇāki yiṉṉamutam vañcittāṉip
piṟiyeyiṟṟaṉ ṟaṭalariyāyp perukiṉāṉi,
taṇṇārnta vārppuṉalcūḻ meyyameṉṉum
taṭavarimēl kiṭantāṉip paṇaṅkaḷmēvi,
eṉṇāṉi yeṇṇiṟanta pukaḻiṉāṉi
ilaṅkoḷicē raravintam pōṉṟunīṇṭa
kaṇṇāṉi, kaṇṇārak kaṇṭukoṇṭēṉ
kaṭipoḻilcūḻ kaṭalmallit talacayaṉattē.
[[((2.5.8))]]

$$1096

__&&1096__$$2.5.9##__

toṇṭāyar tāmparavu maṭiyiṉāṉip
paṭikaṭanta tāḷāḷaṟ kāḷāyuytal
viṇṭāṉi, teṉṉilaṅki yarakkarvēnti
vilaṅkuṇṇa valaṅkivāyc caraṅkaḷāṇṭu,
paṇṭāya vētaṅkaḷ nāṉkumintu
vēḷvikaḷum kēḷviyō ṭaṅkamāṟum
kaṇṭāṉi, toṇṭaṉēṉ kaṇṭukoṇṭēṉ
kaṭipoḻilcūḻ kaṭalmallit talacayaṉattē.
[[((2.5.9))]]

$$1097

__&&1097__$$2.5.10##__

paṭanākat taṇikkiṭantaṉ ṟavuṇarkōṉip
paṭavekuṇṭu marutiṭippōyp paḻaṉavēli,
taṭamārnta kaṭalmallit talacayaṉattut
tāmarikkaṇ tuyilamarnta talivaṉtaṉṉi,
kaṭamārum karuṅkaḷiṟu vallāṉvelpōrk
kalikaṉṟi yoliceyta iṉpappāṭal,
tiṭamāka viviyintu mintumvallār
tīviṉiyi mutalariya vallārtāme. (2)
[[((2.5.10))]]

1098__((PTM_2.6))

$$1098

__&&1098__$$2.6.1##__

naṇṇāta vāḷavuṇa
riṭippukku, vāṉavarip
peṇṇāki yamutūṭṭum
perumāṉār, maruviṉiya
taṇṇārnta kaṭaṉfmallit
talacayaṉat tuṟivāri,
eṇṇātē yiruppāri
yiṟippoḻutu meṇṇōmē. (2)
[[((2.6.1))]]

$$1099

__&&1099__$$2.6.2##__

pārvaṇṇa maṭamaṅki
paṉinaṉmā malarkkiḻatti,
nīrvaṇṇaṉ mārvakatti
lirukkiyimuṉ niṉintavaṉūr,
kārvaṇṇa mutumunnīrk
kaṭalmallit talacayaṉam,
āreṇṇum neñcuṭiyā
ravaremmi yāḷvārē.
[[((2.6.2))]]

$$1100

__&&1100__$$2.6.3##__

ēṉatti ṉuruvāki
nilamaṅki yeḻilkoṇṭāṉ,
vāṉatti lavarmuṟiyāl
makiḻntētti valaṅkoḷḷa,
kāṉattiṉ kaṭalmallit
talacayaṉat tuṟikiṉṟa,
ñāṉatti ṉoḷiyuruvi
niṉivāreṉ nāyakarē.
[[((2.6.3))]]

$$1101

__&&1101__$$2.6.4##__

viṇṭāri veṉṟāvi
vilaṅkuṇṇa, melliyalār
koṇṭāṭum mallakalam
aḻalēṟa veñcamattuk
kaṇṭāri, kaṭalmallit
talacayaṉat tuṟivāri,
koṇṭāṭum neñcuṭiyā
ravareṅkaḷ kulateyvamē.
[[((2.6.4))]]

$$1102

__&&1102__$$2.6.5##__

piccac ciṟupīlic
camaṇkuṇṭar mutalāyōr,
viccik kiṟiyeṉṉu
mavviṟiyip paṇiyātē,
kaccik kiṭantavaṉūr
kaṭaṉfmallit talacayaṉam,
naccit toḻuvāri
nacceṉṟaṉ naṉṉeñcē.
[[((2.6.5))]]

$$1103

__&&1103__$$2.6.6##__

pulaṉkoḷnitik kuviyōṭu
puḻikkimā kaḷiṟṟiṉamum
nalaṅkoḷnava maṇikkuviyum
cumantekkum nāṉṟocintu,
kalaṅkaḷiyaṅ kummallik
kaṭalmallit talacayaṉam,
valaṅkoḷmaṉat tāravari
valaṅkoḷḷeṉ maṭaneñcē.
[[((2.6.6))]]

$$1104

__&&1104__$$2.6.7##__

pañcic ciṟukūḻi
yuruvāki, maruvāta
vañcappeṇ nañcuṇṭa
aṇṇalmuṉ naṇṇāta,
kañcik kaṭantavaṉūr
kaṭaṉfmallit talacayaṉam,
neñcil toḻuvārit
toḻuvāyeṉ tūyneñcē.
[[((2.6.7))]]

$$1105

__&&1105__$$2.6.8##__

ceḻunīr malarkkamalam
tiriyunta vaṉpakaṭṭāl,
uḻunīr vayaluḻava
ruḻappiṉmuṉ piḻitteḻunta,
kaḻunīr kaṭikamaḻum
kaṭaṉfmallit talacayaṉam,
toḻunīr maṉattavarit
toḻuvāyeṉ tūyneñcē.
[[((2.6.8))]]

$$1106

__&&1106__$$2.6.9##__

piṇaṅkaḷiṭu kāṭataṉuḷ
naṭamāṭu piññakaṉōṭu,
iṇaṅkutiruc cakkarattem
perumāṉārk kiṭam,vicumpil
kaṇaṅkaḷiyaṅ kummallik
kaṭaṉfmallit talacayaṉam,
vaṇaṅkumaṉat tāravari
vaṇaṅkeṉṟaṉ maṭaneñcē.
[[((2.6.9))]]

$$1107

__&&1107__$$2.6.10##__

kaṭikamaḻu neṭumaṟukil
kaṭalmallit talacayaṉattu,
aṭikaḷaṭi yēniṉiyu
maṭiyavarkaḷ tammaṭiyāṉ,
vaṭikoḷneṭu vēlvalavaṉ
kalikaṉṟi yolivallār,
muṭikoḷneṭu maṉṉavartam
mutalvarmuta lāvārē. (2)
[[((2.6.10))]]

1108__((PTM_2.7))

$$1108

__&&1108__$$2.7.1##__

tivaḷumveṇ matipōl tirumukat tarivi
ceḻuṅkaṭa lamutiṉiṟ piṟanta
avaḷum,niṉ ṉākat tiruppatu maṟintum
ākilu mācivi ṭāḷāl,
kuvaḷiyaṅ kaṇṇi kolliyam pāvi
colluniṉ tāḷnayan tirunta
ivaḷi,uṉ maṉattā leṉṉiṉin tiruntāy
iṭaventi yenti pirāṉē. (2)
[[((2.7.1))]]

$$1109

__&&1109__$$2.7.2##__

tuḷampaṭu muṟuval tōḻiyark karuḷāḷ
tuṇimuli cāntukoṇ ṭaṇiyāḷ,
kuḷampaṭu kuvaḷik kaṇṇiṇi yeḻutāḷ
kōlanaṉ malarkkuḻaṟ kaṇiyāḷ,
vaḷampaṭu munnīr viyamuṉ ṉaḷanta,
māleṉṉum māliṉa moḻiyāḷ,
iḷampaṭi yivaḷuk keṉṉiṉin tiruntāy
iṭaventi yenti pirāṉē.
[[((2.7.2))]]

$$1110

__&&1110__$$2.7.3##__

cāntamum pūṇum cantaṉak kuḻampum
taṭamulik kaṇiyilum taḻalām,
pōntaveṇ tiṅkaḷ katircuṭa meliyum
porukaṭal pulampilum pulampum,
māntaḷir mēṉi vaṇṇamum poṉṉām
vaḷikaḷum iṟinillā, eṉtaṉ
ēntiḻi yivaḷuk keṉṉiṉin tiruntāy
iṭaventi yenti pirāṉē.
[[((2.7.3))]]

$$1111

__&&1111__$$2.7.4##__

ūḻiyil peritāl nāḻiki. eṉṉum
oṇcuṭar tuyiṉṟatāl eṉṉum,
āḻiyum pulampum. aṉṟilu muṟaṅkā
teṉṟalum tīyiṉiṟ koṭitām,
tōḻiyō. eṉṉum tuṇimuli yarakkum
collumi ṉeṉceykēṉ eṉṉum,
ēḻiyeṉ poṉṉuk keṉṉiṉin tiruntāy
iṭaventi yenti pirāṉē.
[[((2.7.4))]]

$$1112

__&&1112__$$2.7.5##__

ōtilum uṉpē raṉṟimaṟ ṟōtāḷ
urukumniṉ tiruvuru niṉintu,
kātaṉmi peritu kiyaṟa vuṭiyaḷ
kayalneṭuṅ kaṇtuyil maṟantāḷ,
pētiyēṉ pēti piḷḷimi peritu
teḷḷiyaḷ vaḷḷinuṇ maruṅkul,
ētalar muṉṉā eṉṉiṉin tiruntāy
iṭaventi yenti pirāṉē.
[[((2.7.5))]]

$$1113

__&&1113__$$2.7.6##__

taṉkuṭik kētum takkavā niṉiyāḷ
taṭaṅkaṭal nuṭaṅkeyi lilaṅki,
vaṉkuṭi maṭaṅka vāḷamar tolitta
vārttikēṭ ṭiṉpuṟum mayaṅkum,
miṉkoṭi maruṅkul curuṅkamēl neruṅki
meṉmuli poṉpayan tirunta,
eṉkoṭi yivaḷuk keṉṉiṉin tiruntāy
iṭaventi yenti pirāṉē.
[[((2.7.6))]]

$$1114

__&&1114__$$2.7.7##__

uḷaṅkaṉin tirukkum uṉṉiyē pitaṟṟum
uṉakkaṉṟi yeṉakkaṉpoṉ ṟilaḷāl,
vaḷaṅkaṉi poḻilcūḻ māliruñ cōli
māyaṉē. eṉṟuvāy veruvum,
kaḷaṅkaṉi muṟuval kāriki peritu
kavaliyō ṭavalamcērn tirunta,
iḷaṅkaṉi yivaḷuk keṉṉiṉin tiruntāy
iṭaventi yenti pirāṉē.
[[((2.7.7))]]

$$1115

__&&1115__$$2.7.8##__

alaṅkeḻu taṭakki yāyaṉvā yāmpaṟ
kaḻiyumā leṉṉuḷḷam. eṉṉum,
pulaṅkeḻu porunīrp puṭkuḻi pāṭum
pōtumō nīrmalik ke ṉṉum,
kulaṅkeḻu kolli kōmaḷa vallik
koṭiyiṭi neṭumaḻik kaṇṇi,
ilaṅkeḻil tōḷik keṉṉiṉin tiruntāy
iṭaventi yenti pirāṉē.
[[((2.7.8))]]

$$1116

__&&1116__$$2.7.9##__

poṉkulām payali pūttaṉa mentōḷ
porukayal kaṇtuyil maṟantāḷ,
aṉpiṉā luṉmē lātaram peritu ivva
ṇaṅkiṉuk kuṟṟanō yaṟiyēṉ,
miṉkulā maruṅkul curuṅkamēl neruṅki
vīṅkiya vaṉamuli yāḷukku,
eṉkolām kuṟippi leṉṉiṉin tiruntāy
iṭaventi yenti pirāṉē.
[[((2.7.9))]]

$$1117

__&&1117__$$2.7.10##__

aṉṉamum mīṉum āmiyum ariyum
āyem māyaṉē. aruḷāy,
eṉṉumiṉ toṇṭark kiṉṉaruḷ puriyum
iṭaventi yenti pirāṉi,
maṉṉumā māṭa maṅkiyar talivaṉ
māṉavēl kaliyaṉvā yolikaḷ,
paṉṉiya paṉuval pāṭuvār nāḷum
paḻaviṉi paṟṟaṟup pārē. (2)
[[((2.7.10))]]

1118__((PTM_2.8))

$$1118

__&&1118__$$2.8.1##__

tiripura mūṉṟerit tāṉummaṟṟi
malarmici mēlaya ṉumviyappa,
muritiri mākaṭal pōlmuḻaṅki
mūvula kummuṟi yālvaṇaṅka,
eriyaṉa kēcara vāḷeyiṟṟō
ṭiraṇiya ṉāka miraṇṭukūṟā,
ariyuru vāmiva rārkkoleṉṉa
aṭṭa puyakarat tēṉeṉṟārē. (2)
[[((2.8.1))]]

$$1119

__&&1119__$$2.8.2##__

ventiṟal vīraril vīraroppār
vēta murittimi yōrvaṇaṅkum,
centamiḻ pāṭuvār tāmvaṇaṅkum
tēva rivarkol terikkamāṭṭēṉ,
vantu kuṟaḷaru vāynimirntu
māvali vēḷviyil maṇṇaḷanta,
antaṇar pōṉṟiva rārkkoleṉṉa
aṭṭa puyakarat tēṉeṉṟārē.
[[((2.8.2))]]

$$1120

__&&1120__$$2.8.3##__

cempo ṉilaṅku valaṅkivāḷi
tiṇcili taṇṭoṭu caṅkamoḷvāḷ,
umpa rirucuṭa rāḻiyōṭu
kēṭaka moṇmalar paṟṟiyeṟṟē,
vempu ciṉattaṭal vēḻamvīḻa
veṇmarup poṉṟu paṟittu,iruṇṭa
amputam pōṉṟiva rārkkoleṉṉa
aṭṭa puyakarat tēṉeṉṟārē.
[[((2.8.3))]]

$$1121

__&&1121__$$2.8.4##__

mañcuyar māmaṇik kuṉṟamēnti
māmaḻi kāttoru māyavāṉi
yañca,ataṉmarup poṉṟuvāṅkum
āyarkol māya maṟiyamāṭṭēṉ,
veñcuṭa rāḻiyum caṅkumēnti
vētamu ṉōtuvar nītivāṉattu,
añcuṭar pōṉṟiva rārkkoleṉṉa
aṭṭa puyakarat tēṉeṉṟārē.
[[((2.8.4))]]

$$1122

__&&1122__$$2.8.5##__

kalikaḷum vētamum nītinūlum
kaṟpamum coṟporuḷ tāṉum,maṟṟi
nilikaḷum vāṉavark kumpiṟarkkum
nīrmiyi nālaruḷ ceytu,nīṇṭa
malikaḷum māmaṇi yummalarmēl
maṅkiyum caṅkamum taṅkukiṉṟa,
alikaṭal pōṉṟiva rārkkoleṉṉa
aṭṭa puyakarat tēṉeṉṟārē.
[[((2.8.5))]]

$$1123

__&&1123__$$2.8.6##__

eṅṅaṉum nāmivar vaṇṇameṇṇil
ētu maṟikilam, ēntiḻiyār
caṅkum maṉamum niṟivumellām
tammaṉa vākap pukuntu,tāmum
poṅku karuṅkaṭal pūvikāyāp
pōtaviḻ nīlam puṉintamēkam,
aṅṅaṉam pōṉṟiva rārkkoleṉṉa
aṭṭa puyakarat tēṉeṉṟārē.
[[((2.8.6))]]

$$1124

__&&1124__$$2.8.7##__

muḻucivaṇ ṭāṭiya taṇṭuḻāyiṉ
moymmalark kaṇṇiyum,mēṉiyañcān-
tiḻiciya kōla miruntavāṟum
eṅṅaṉañ collukēṉ. ōvinallār,
eḻutiya tāmari yaṉṉakaṇṇum
ēnteḻi lākamum tōḷumvāyum,
aḻakiya tāmiva rārkkoleṉṉa
aṭṭa puyakarat tēṉeṉṟārē.
[[((2.8.7))]]

$$1125

__&&1125__$$2.8.8##__

mēviyep pālumviṇ ṇōrvaṇaṅka
vēta muripparmun nīrmaṭanti
tēvi,ap pālatir caṅkamippāl
cakkaram maṟṟivar vaṇṇameṇṇil,
kāviyop pārkkaṭa lēyumoppār
kaṇṇum vaṭivum neṭiyarāy,eṉ
āviyop pāriva rārkkoleṉṉa
aṭṭa puyakarat tēṉeṉṟārē.
[[((2.8.8))]]

$$1126

__&&1126__$$2.8.9##__

tañca mivarkkeṉ vaḷiyumnillā
neñcamum tammatē cintittēṟku,
vañci maruṅkul neruṅkanōkki
vāytiṟan toṉṟu paṇittatuṇṭu,
nañca muṭittivar nōkkumnōkkam
nāṉivar tammi yaṟiyamāṭṭēṉ
añcuvaṉ maṟṟiva rārkkoleṉṉa
aṭṭa puyakarat tēṉeṉṟārē.
[[((2.8.9))]]

$$1127

__&&1127__$$2.8.10##__

maṉṉavaṉ toṇṭiyar kōṉvaṇaṅkum
nīḷmuṭi māli vayiramēkaṉ,
taṉvali taṉpukaḻ cūḻntakacci
aṭṭa puyakarat tātitaṉṉi,
kaṉṉinaṉ māmatiḷ maṅkivēntaṉ
kāmaru cīrkkali kaṉṟi,kuṉṟā
iṉṉici yālcoṉṉa ceñcolmāli
yēttaval lārkkiṭam vikuntamē. (2)
[[((2.8.10))]]

1128__((PTM_2.9))

$$1128

__&&1128__$$2.9.1##__

colluvaṉ coṟporuḷ tāṉavi yāyccuvi
yūṟoli nāṟṟamum tōṟṟamumāy,
nallaraṉ nāṉmukaṉ nāraṇa ṉukkiṭan
tāṉtaṭañ cūḻntaḻa kāyakacci,
pallavaṉ villava ṉeṉṟula kilpala
rāyppala vēntar vaṇaṅkukaḻaṟf
pallavaṉ, malliyar kōṉpaṇinata para
mēccura viṇṇaka ramatuvē. (2)
[[((2.9.1))]]

$$1129

__&&1129__$$2.9.2##__

kārmaṉṉu nīḷvicum pumkaṭa lumcuṭa
rumnila ṉummali yum,taṉṉuntit
tārmaṉṉu tāmarik kaṇṇaṉi ṭamtaṭa
māmatiḷ cūḻntaḻa kāyakacci,
tērmaṉṉu teṉṉava ṉimuṉi yilceru
viltiṟal vāṭṭiya tiṇciliyōṉ,
pārmaṉṉu pallavar kōṉpaṇinta para
mēccura viṇṇaka ramatuvē.
[[((2.9.2))]]

$$1130

__&&1130__$$2.9.3##__

urantaru mellaṇip paḷḷikoṇ ṭāṉoru
kālmuṉṉam māvuru vāykkaṭaluḷ,
varantarum māmaṇi vaṇṇaṉi ṭammaṇi
māṭaṅkaḷ cūḻntaḻa kāyakacci,
nirantavar maṇṇiyil puṇṇukar vēlneṭu
vāyi lukacceru vilmuṉanāḷ,
parantavaṉ pallavar kōṉpaṇin tapara
mēccura viṇṇaka ramatuvē.
[[((2.9.3))]]

$$1131

__&&1131__$$2.9.4##__

aṇṭamu meṇṭici yumnila ṉumali
nīroṭu vāṉeri kālmutalā
uṇṭavaṉ, entipi rāṉati ṭamoḷi
māṭaṅkaḷ cūntaḻa kāyakacci,
viṇṭava riṇṭikku ḻāmuṭa ṉēvirin
tāriri yacceru vilmuṉintu,
paṇṭoru kālvaḷit tāṉpaṇin tapara
mēccura viṇṇaka ramatuvē.
[[((2.9.4))]]

$$1132

__&&1132__$$2.9.5##__

tūmpuṭit tiṇkivaṉ tāḷkaḷiṟ ṟiṉtuyar
tīrttara vamveruva,muṉanāḷ
pūmpuṉal poykipuk kāṉava ṉukkiṭan
tāṉtaṭañ cūḻntaḻa kāyakacci,
tēmpoḻil kuṉṟeyil teṉṉava ṉitticip
pacceru mēlviyan taṉṟuceṉṟa,
pāmpuṭip pallavar kōṉpaṇin tapara
mēccura viṇṇaka ramatuvē.
[[((2.9.5))]]

$$1133

__&&1133__$$2.9.6##__

tiṇpaṭik kōḷari yiṉuru vāyttiṟa
lōṉaka lamceru vilmuṉanāḷ,
puṇpaṭap pōḻnta pirāṉati ṭamporu
māṭaṅkaḷ cūḻntaḻa kāyakacci,
veṇkuṭi nīḻalceṅ kōlnaṭap paviṭi
velkoṭi vēṟfpaṭi muṉṉuyartta,
paṇpuṭip pallavar kōṉpaṇin tapara
mēccura viṇṇaka ramatuvē.
[[((2.9.6))]]

$$1134

__&&1134__$$2.9.7##__

ilakiya nīṇmuṭi māvali taṉperu
vēḷviyil māṇuru vāymuṉanāḷ,
calamoṭu mānilaṅ koṇṭava ṉukkiṭan
tāṉtaṭañ cūḻntaḻa kāyakacci,
ulakuṭi maṉṉavaṉ teṉṉava ṉikkaṉṉi
māmatiḷ cūḻkaru vūrveruva,
palapaṭi cāyaveṉ ṟāṉpaṇin tapara
mēccura viṇṇaka ramatuvē.
[[((2.9.7))]]

$$1135

__&&1135__$$2.9.8##__

kuṭittiṟal maṉṉava ṉāyoru kālkuraṅ
kippaṭi yā,mali yālkaṭali
aṭittava ṉentipi rāṉati ṭammaṇi
māṭaṅkaḷ cūḻntaḻa kāyakacci,
viṭittiṟal villavaṉ neṉmeli yilveru
vacceru vēlvalaṅ kippiṭitta,
paṭittiṟal pallavar kōṉpaṇin tapara
mēccura viṇṇaka ramatuvē.
[[((2.9.8))]]

$$1136

__&&1136__$$2.9.9##__

piṟiyuṭi vāṇutal piṉṉi tiṟattu
muṉṉoru kālceru villurumiṉ,
maṟiyuṭi mālviṭi yēḻaṭart tāṟkiṭan
tāṉtaṭañ cūḻntaḻa kāyakacci,
kaṟiyuṭi vāḷmaṟa maṉṉarkke ṭakkaṭal
pōla muḻaṅkum kuralkaṭuvāy,
paṟiyuṭip pallavar kōṉpaṇin tapara
mēccura viṇṇaka ramatuvē.
[[((2.9.9))]]

$$1137

__&&1137__$$2.9.10##__

pārmaṉṉu tolpukaḻp pallavar kōṉpaṇin
tapara mēccura viṇṇakarmēl,
kārmaṉṉu nīḷvayal maṅkiyar tantali
vaṉkali kaṉṟikuṉ ṟāturitta,
cīrmaṉṉu centamiḻ mālival lārttiru
māmakaḷ taṉṉaru ḷāl,ulakil
tērmaṉṉa rāyoli mākaṭal cūḻceḻu
nīrula kāṇṭu tikaḻvarkaḷē. (2)
[[((2.9.10))]]

1138__((PTM_2.10))

$$1138

__&&1138__$$2.10.1##__

mañcāṭu variyēḻum kaṭalka ḷēḻum
vāṉakamum maṇṇakamum maṟṟu mellām,
eñcāmal vayiṟṟaṭakki yāliṉ mēlōr
iḷantaḷiril kaṇvaḷarnta īcaṉ ṟaṉṉi,
tuñcānīr vaḷañcurakkum peṇṇit teṉpāl
tūyanāṉ maṟiyāḷar cōmuc ceyya,
ceñcāli viḷivayaluḷ tikaḻntu tōṉṟum
tirukkōva lūrataṉuḷ kaṇṭēṉ nāṉē. (2)
[[((2.10.1))]]

$$1139

__&&1139__$$2.10.2##__

kontalarnta naṟuntuḻāy cāntam tūpam
tīpamkoṇ ṭamararttoḻap paṇaṅkoḷpāmpil,
cantaṇimeṉ mulimalarāḷ taraṇi maṅki
tāmiruva raṭivaruṭum taṉmi yāṉi,
vantaṉiceytu iciyēḻā ṟaṅkam aintu
vaḷarvēḷvi nāṉmaṟikaḷ mūṉṟu tīyum,
cintaṉicey tirupoḻutu moṉṟum celvat
tirukkōva lūrataṉuḷ kaṇṭēṉ nāṉē.
[[((2.10.2))]]

$$1140

__&&1140__$$2.10.3##__

koḻuntalaru malarccōlik kuḻāṅkoḷ poykik
kōḷmutali vāḷeyiṟṟuk koṇṭaṟkeḷki,
aḻuntiyamā kaḷiṟṟiṉukkaṉ ṟāḻi yēnti
antaramē varattōṉṟi yaruḷcey tāṉi,
eḻuntamalark karunīla miruntil kāṭṭa
irumpuṉṉi muttarumpic cempoṉkāṭṭa,
ceḻuntaṭanīrk kamalamtī vikipōl kāṭṭum
tirukkōva lūrataṉuḷ kaṇṭēṉ nāṉē.
[[((2.10.3))]]

$$1141

__&&1141__$$2.10.4##__

tāṅkarumpōr mālipaṭap paṟavi yūrntu
tarātalattōr kuṟimuṭitta taṉmiyāṉi,
āṅkarumpik kaṇṇīrcōrn taṉpu kūrum
aṭiyavarkaṭ kāramuta māṉāṉ ṟaṉṉi,
kōṅkarumpu curapuṉṉi kuravār cōlik
kuḻāvarivaṇ ṭicipāṭum pāṭal kēṭṭu
tīṅkarumpu kaṇfvaḷarum kaḻaṉi cūḻnta
tirukkōva lūrataṉuḷ kaṇṭēṉ nāṉē.
[[((2.10.4))]]

$$1142

__&&1142__$$2.10.5##__

kaṟivaḷarvēl karaṉmutalāk kavantaṉ vāli
kaṇiyoṉṟi nālmaṭiya ilaṅkitaṉṉuḷ,
piṟiyeyiṟṟu vāḷarakkar cēṉi yellām
peruntakiyō ṭuṭantuṇitta pemmāṉṟaṉṉi,
maṟivaḷarap pukaḻvaḷara māṭan tōṟum
maṇṭapamoṇ toḷiyaṉittum vāramōta,
ciṟiyaṇinta poḻilaṇinta teṉṟal vīcum
tirukkōva lūrataṉuḷ kaṇṭēṉ nāṉē.
[[((2.10.5))]]

$$1143

__&&1143__$$2.10.6##__

uṟiyārnta naṟuveṇṇe yoḷiyāl ceṉṟaṅ
kuṇṭāṉik kaṇṭāycci yuralō ṭārkka,
taṟiyārnta karuṅkaḷiṟē pōla niṉṟu
taṭaṅkaṇkaḷ paṉimalkum taṉmi yāṉi,
veṟiyārnta malarmakaḷnā maṅki yōṭu,
viyaṉkaliyeṇ tōḷiṉāḷ viḷaṅku, celvac
ceṟiyārnta maṇimāṭam tikaḻntu tōṉṟum
tirukkōva lūrataṉuḷ kaṇṭēṉ nāṉē.
[[((2.10.6))]]

$$1144

__&&1144__$$2.10.7##__

iruṅkimmā karimuṉintu pariyik kīṟi
iṉaviṭika ḷēḻaṭarttu marutam cāyttu,
varumcakaṭa miṟavutittu malli yaṭṭu
vañcakañcey kañcaṉukku nañcā ṉāṉi,
karuṅkamuku pacumpāḷi veṇmut tīṉṟu
kāyellām marakatamāyp pavaḷaṅ kāṭṭa,
ceruntimika moṭṭalarttum tēṉkoḷcōlit
tirukkōva lūrataṉuḷ kaṇṭēṉ nāṉē.
[[((2.10.7))]]

$$1145

__&&1145__$$2.10.8##__

pārēṟu perumpāran tīrap paṇṭu
pāratattut tūtiyaṅki, pārttaṉ celvat
tērēṟu cāratiyā yetirntār cēṉi
cerukkaḷattut tiṟalaḻiyac ceṟṟāṉṟaṉṉi,
pōrēṟoṉ ṟuṭiyāṉu maḷakik kōṉum
purantaraṉum nāṉmukaṉum poruntu mūrppōl,
cīrēṟu maṟiyāḷar niṟinta celvat
tirukkōva lūrataṉuḷ kaṇṭēṉ nāṉē.
[[((2.10.8))]]

$$1146

__&&1146__$$2.10.9##__

tūvaṭiviṉ pārmakaḷpū maṅki yōṭu
cuṭarāḻi caṅkirupāl polintu tōṉṟa,
kāvaṭiviṉ kaṟpakamē pōla niṉṟu
kalantavarkaṭ karuḷpuriyuṅ karutti ṉāṉi,
cēvaṭiki tiruvāykaṇ civanta vāṭi
cempoṉcey tiruvuruva māṉāṉ ṟaṉṉi,
tīvaṭiviṉ civaṉayaṉē pōlvār maṉṉu
tirukkōva lūrataṉuḷ kaṇṭēṉ nāṉē.
[[((2.10.9))]]

$$1147

__&&1147__$$2.10.10##__

vāraṇaṅko ḷiṭarkkaṭinta māli nīla
maratakatti maḻimukilē pōlvāṉṟaṉṉi,
cīraṇaṅku maṟiyāḷar niṟinta celvat
tirukkōva lūrataṉuḷ kaṇṭēṉ, eṉṟu
vāraṇaṅku mulimaṭavār maṅki vēntaṉ
vāṭkaliya ṉoliyintu mintum vallār,
kāraṇaṅka lālukaṅ kalantaṅk kēttak
karanteṅkum parantāṉik kāṇpar tāmē. (2)
[[((2.10.10))]]

periya tirumoḻi mūṉṟām pattu

1148__((PTM_3.1))

$$1148

__&&1148__$$3.1.1##__

iruntaṇ mānila mēṉama tāyvaḷi
maruppiṉi lakattoṭukki,
karuntaṇ mākaṭal kaṇṭuyiṉ ṟavaṉiṭam
kamalanaṉ malarttēṟal
arunti, iṉṉici muraṉṟeḻum aḷikulam
potuḷiyam poḻilūṭē,
cerunti nāṇmalar ceṉṟaṇin tuḻitaru
tiruvayin tirapuramē. (2)
[[((3.1.1))]]

$$1149

__&&1149__$$3.1.2##__

miṉṉu māḻiyaṅ kiyavaṉ ceyyavaḷ
uṟitaru tirumārpaṉ,
paṉṉu nāṉmaṟip palaporu ḷākiya
paraṉiṭam variccāral,
piṉṉu mātavip pantalil peṭivarap
piṇiyaviḻ kamalattu,
teṉṉa veṉṟuvaṇ ṭiṉṉici muraltaru
tiruvayin tirapuramē.
[[((3.1.2))]]

$$1150

__&&1150__$$3.1.3##__

viya mēḻumuṇ ṭālili vikiya
māyavaṉ, aṭiyavarkku
meyya ṉākiya teyvanā yakaṉiṭam
meytaku variccāral,
moykoḷ mātavi caṇpakam muyaṅkiya
mulliyaṅ koṭiyāṭa,
ceyya tāmaric ceḻumpaṇi tikaḻtaru
tiruvayin tirapuramē.
[[((3.1.3))]]

$$1151

__&&1151__$$3.1.4##__

māṟu koṇṭuṭaṉ ṟetirntaval lavuṇaṉṟaṉ
mārpaka mirupiḷavā,
kūṟu koṇṭavaṉ kulamakaṟ kiṉṉaruḷ
koṭuttava ṉiṭam,miṭintu
cāṟu koṇṭameṉ karumpiḷaṅ kaḻitaki
vicumpuṟa maṇinīḻal,
cēṟu koṇṭataṇ paḻaṉama teḻiltikaḻ
tiruvayin tirapuramē.
[[((3.1.4))]]

$$1152

__&&1152__$$3.1.5##__

āṅku māvali vēḷviyi lirantuceṉ
ṟakaliṭa maḷantu āyar,
pūṅko ṭikkiṉa viṭiporu tavaṉiṭam
poṉmalar tikaḻ,vēṅki
kōṅku ceṇpakak kompiṉil kutikoṭu
kurakkiṉam irittōṭi
tēṉka lantaṇ palaṅkaṉi nukarttaru
tiruvayin tirapuramē.
[[((3.1.5))]]

$$1153

__&&1153__$$3.1.6##__

kūṉu lāviya maṭantitaṉ koṭuñcoliṉ
tiṟattiḷaṅ koṭiyōṭum,
kāṉu lāviya karumukil tiruniṟat
tavaṉiṭam kaviṉārum,
vāṉu lāviya matitavaḻ mālvari
māmatiḷ puṭicūḻa,
tēṉu lāviya ceḻumpoḻil taḻuviya
tiruvayin tirapuramē.
[[((3.1.6))]]

$$1154

__&&1154__$$3.1.7##__

miṉṉiṉ nuṇṇiṭi maṭakkoṭi kāraṇam
vilaṅkaliṉ miciyilaṅki
maṉṉaṉ, nīṇmuṭi poṭiceyta mintaṉa
tiṭammaṇi varinīḻal,
aṉṉa māmala raravintat tamaḷiyil
peṭiyoṭu miṉitamara,
cenne lārkkava rikkuli vīcutaṇ
tiruvayin tirapuramē.
[[((3.1.7))]]

$$1155

__&&1155__$$3.1.8##__

virika maḻntameṉ karuṅkuḻal kāraṇam
villiṟuttu aṭalmaḻikku,
nirika laṅkiṭa varikuṭi yeṭuttavaṉ
nilaviya iṭamtaṭamār,
variva ḷantikaḻ matakari maruppoṭu
malivaḷa rakilunti,
tiriko ṇarntaṇi ceḻunati vayalpuku
tiruvayin tirapuramē.
[[((3.1.8))]]

$$1156

__&&1156__$$3.1.9##__

vēlkoḷ kittalat taracarvem pōriṉil
vicayaṉuk kāy,maṇittērk
kōlkoḷ kittalat tentipem māṉiṭam
kulavutaṇ variccāral,
kālkoḷ kaṇkoṭik kiyeḻak kamukiḷam
pāḷikaḷ kamaḻcāral,
cēlkaḷ pāytaru ceḻunati vayalpuku
tiruvayin tirapuramē.
[[((3.1.9))]]

$$1157

__&&1157__$$3.1.10##__

mūva rākiya voruvaṉi mūvula
kuṇṭumiḻn taḷantāṉi,
tēvar tāṉavar ceṉṟuceṉ ṟiṟiñcattaṇ
tiruvayin tirapurattu,
mēvu cōtiyi vēlvala vaṉkali
kaṉṟi viritturitta,
pāvu taṇṭamiḻ pattivi pāṭiṭap
pāvaṅkaḷ payilāvē. (2)
[[((3.1.10))]]

1158__((PTM_3.2))

$$1158

__&&1158__$$3.2.1##__

ūṉvāṭa vuṇṇā tuyirkkāva liṭṭu
uṭaliṟ piriyāp pulaṉintum nontu,
tāmvāṭa vāṭat tavamceyya vēṇṭā
tamatā imiyō rulakāḷa kiṟpīr
kāṉāṭa maññik kaṇamāṭa māṭē
kayalāṭu kāṉīrp paḻaṉam puṭipōy,
tēṉāṭa māṭak koṭiyāṭu tillit
tiruccitra kūṭam ceṉṟucēr miṉkaḷē. (2)
[[((3.2.1))]]

$$1159

__&&1159__$$3.2.2##__

kāyōṭu nīṭu kaṉiyuṇṭu vīcu
kaṭuṅkāl ñukarntu neṭuṅkālam, aintu
tīyoṭu niṉṟu tavañceyya vēṇṭā
tirumārpaṉiccinti yuḷvittu meṉpīr,
vāyōtu vētam malkiṉṟa tolcīr
maṟiyāḷar nāḷum muṟiyāl vaḷartta,
tīyōṅka vōṅkap pukaḻōṅku tillit
tiruccitra kūṭam ceṉṟucēr miṉkaḷē.
[[((3.2.2))]]

$$1160

__&&1160__$$3.2.3##__

vempum ciṉattup puṉakkēḻa loṉṟāy
virinīr mutuveḷḷa muḷpuk kaḻunta,
vampuṇ poḻilcū ḻulakaṉ ṟeṭuttāṉ
aṭippō taṇivāṉ viruppō ṭiruppīr,
pimpoṉṉu muttum maṇiyum koṇarntu
paṭimaṉṉavaṉpal lavarkkōṉ paṇinta,
cempoṉ maṇimāṭaṅ kaḷcūḻnta tillit
tiruccitra kūṭam ceṉṟucēr miṉkaḷē.
[[((3.2.3))]]

$$1161

__&&1161__$$3.2.4##__

arumā nilamaṉ ṟaḷappāṉ kuṟaḷāy
avuṇaṉ peruvēḷ viyilceṉ ṟiranta,
perumāṉ tirunā mampitaṟ ṟinuntam
piṟavit tuyarnīṅku tumeṉṉa kiṟpīr.
karumā kaṭaluḷ kiṭantā ṉuvantu
kavinā varavi ṉaṇippaḷḷi yiṉmēl,
tirumāl tirumaṅ kiyoṭāṭu tillit
tiruccitra kūṭam ceṉṟucēr miṉkaḷē.
[[((3.2.4))]]

$$1162

__&&1162__$$3.2.5##__

kōmaṅka vaṅkak kaṭalviya muyyak
kulamaṉṉa raṅkam maḻuvil tuṇiya,
tāmaṅ kamaruḷ paṭitoṭṭa veṉṟit
tavamā muṉiyit tamakkākka kiṟpīr,
pūmaṅki taṅkip pulamaṅki maṉṉip
pukaḻmaṅki yeṅkum tikaḻappukaḻcēr
cēmaṅkoḷ pimpūm poḻilcūḻnta tillit
tiruccitra kūṭam ceṉṟucēr miṉkaḷē.
[[((3.2.5))]]

$$1163

__&&1163__$$3.2.6##__

neyvā yaḻalam puturantu munnīr
tuṇiyap paṇikoṇ ṭaṇiyārntu,ilaṅku
miyār vaṇivaṇ ṇaṉiyeṇṇi nuntam
maṉattē yiruttum paṭivāḻa vallīr,
avvāyiḷamaṅ kiyarppēca vuntāṉ
arumā maṟiyan taṇarcin tipuka,
cevvāyk kiḷināṉ maṟipāṭu tillit
tiruccitra kūṭam ceṉṟucēr miṉkaḷē.
[[((3.2.6))]]

$$1164

__&&1164__$$3.2.7##__

muval kuḻalāycci mentōḷ nayantu
makaram cuḻalac cuḻalnīr payanta,
teyvat tirumā malarmaṅki taṅku
tirumār paṉiccinti yuḷvittu meṉpīr
kuvik kaḷiṟṟiṉ maruppum poruppil
kamaḻcantu munti nivāva laṅkoḷ,
teyvap puṉalcūḻn taḻakāya tillit
tiruccitra kūṭam ceṉṟucēr miṉkaḷē.
[[((3.2.7))]]

$$1165

__&&1165__$$3.2.8##__

māvāyi ṉaṅkam matiyātu kīṟi
maḻimā mutukuṉ ṟeṭuttu,āyar taṅkaḷ
kōvāy nirimēyt tulakuṇṭa māyaṉ
kurimā kaḻalkū ṭumkuṟip puṭiyīr,
mūvā yiranāṉ maṟiyāḷar nāḷum
muṟiyāl vaṇaṅka aṇaṅkāya cōti,
tēvāti tēvaṉ tikaḻkiṉṟa tillit
tiruccitra kūṭam ceṉṟucēr miṉkaḷē.
[[((3.2.8))]]

$$1166

__&&1166__$$3.2.9##__

cerunīla vēṟkaṇ maṭavār tiṟattuc
ciṉattōṭu niṉṟu maṉattāl vaḷarkkum,
arunīla pāva makalap pukaḻcēr
amararkku meytāta aṇṭatti ruppīr,
perunīr nivāvunti muttaṅko ṇarnteṅkum
vittum vayaluḷ kayalpāyn tukaḷa,
tirunīla niṉṟu tikaḻkiṉṟa tillit
tiruccitra kūṭam ceṉṟucēr miṉkaḷē.
[[((3.2.9))]]

$$1167

__&&1167__$$3.2.10##__

cīrār poḻilcūḻn taḻakāya tillit
tirucitra kūṭat tuṟiceṅkaṇ mālukku,
ārāta vuḷḷat tavarkkēṭ ṭuvappa
alinī rulakuk karuḷē puriyum,
kārār puyaṟkik kalikaṉṟi kuṉṟā
olimāli yoṟoṉpa tōṭoṉṟum vallār,
pārā rulaka maḷantā ṉaṭikkīḻp
palakālam niṟkum paṭivāḻvar tāmē. (2)
[[((3.2.10))]]

1168__((PTM_3.3))

$$1168

__&&1168__$$3.3.1##__

vāṭa marutiṭi pōki
mallarik koṉṟokka liṭṭiṭṭu,
āṭalnal māvuṭit tāyar
ānirik kaṉṟiṭar tīrppāṉ,
kūṭiya māmaḻi kātta
kūtta ṉeṉevaru kiṉṟāṉ,
cēṭuyar pūmpoḻil tillic
cittira kūṭattuḷ ḷāṉē. (2)
[[((3.3.1))]]

$$1169

__&&1169__$$3.3.2##__

pēymakaḷ koṅkinañ cuṇṭa
piḷḷi paricitu veṉṟāl,
mānila māmakaḷ mātar
kēḷva ṉivaṉeṉṟum, vaṇṭuṇ
pūmakaḷ nāyaka ṉeṉṟum
pulaṅkeḻu kōviyar pāṭi,
tēmalar tūva varuvāṉ
cittira kūṭattuḷ ḷāṉē.
[[((3.3.2))]]

$$1170

__&&1170__$$3.3.3##__

paṇṭivaṉ veṇṇeyuṇ ṭāṉeṉ
ṟāycciyar kūṭi yiḻippa
eṇṭici yōrumva ṇaṅka
iṇimaru tūṭu naṭantiṭṭu,
aṇṭarum vāṉat tavaru
māyira nāmaṅka ḷōṭu,
tiṇṭiṟal pāṭa varuvāṉ
cittira kūṭattuḷ ḷāṉē.
[[((3.3.3))]]

$$1171

__&&1171__$$3.3.4##__

vaḷikki neṭuṅkaṇ maṭavā
rāycciya rañci yaḻippa,
taḷittaviḻ tāmarip poykit
taṇtaṭam pukkaṇṭar kāṇa,
muḷitta eyiṟṟaḻal nākat
tucciyil niṉṟatu vāṭa,
tilittamar ceytu varuvāṉ
cittira kūṭattuḷ ḷāṉē.
[[((3.3.4))]]

$$1172

__&&1172__$$3.3.5##__

paruvak karumuki lottu
muṭṭuṭi mākaṭa lottu,
aruvit tiraḷtikaḻ kiṉṟa
vāyiram poṉmali yottu,
uruvak karuṅkuḻa lāycci
tiṟattiṉa mālviṭi ceṟṟu,
teruvil tiḷittu varuvāṉ
cittira kūṭattuḷ ḷāṉē.
[[((3.3.5))]]

$$1173

__&&1173__$$3.3.6##__

eyyac citinta tilaṅki
malaṅka varumaḻi kāppāṉ,
uyyap paruvari tāṅki
āniri kāttāṉeṉ ṟētti,
viyat tevarum vaṇaṅka
aṇaṅkeḻu māmali pōlē,
teyvappuḷ ḷēṟi varuvāṉ
cittira kūṭattuḷ ḷāṉē. (2)
[[((3.3.6))]]

$$1174

__&&1174__$$3.3.7##__

āva rivicey taṟivār?
añcaṉa māmali pōlē,
mēvu ciṉattaṭal vēḻam
vīḻa muṉintu,aḻa kāya
kāvi malarneṭuṅ kaṇṇār
kitiḻa vīti varuvāṉ,
tēvar vaṇaṅkutaṇ tillic
cittira kūṭattuḷ ḷāṉē.
[[((3.3.7))]]

$$1175

__&&1175__$$3.3.8##__

poṅki yamari lorukāl
poṉpeya rōṉi veruva,
aṅkavaṉāka maḷintiṭ
ṭāyiran tōḷeḻun tāṭa,
piṅka ṇiraṇṭeri kāṉṟa
nīṇṭa eyiṟṟoṭu pēḻvāy,
ciṅka vuruvil varuvāṉ
cittira kūṭattuḷ ḷāṉē.
[[((3.3.8))]]

$$1176

__&&1176__$$3.3.9##__

karumukil pōlvatōr mēṉi
kiyaṉa vāḻiyum caṅkum,
peruviṟal vāṉavar cūḻa
ēḻula kumtoḻu tētta,
orumaka ḷāyar maṭanti
yorutti nilamakaḷ, maṟṟit
tirumaka ḷōṭum varuvāṉ
cittira kūṭattuḷ ḷāṉē.
[[((3.3.9))]]

$$1177

__&&1177__$$3.3.10##__

tēṉamar pūmpoḻil tillic
cittira kūṭa mamarnta,
vāṉavar taṅkaḷ pirāṉi
maṅkiyar kōṉmaru vārf,
ūṉamar vēlkali kaṉṟi
yoṇṭami ḻoṉpatō ṭoṉṟum,
tāṉivi kaṟṟuval lārmēl
cārātīviṉitāṉē. (2)
[[((3.3.10))]]

1178__((PTM_3.4))

$$1178

__&&1178__$$3.4.1##__

orukuṟaḷā yirunilammū vaṭimaṇ vēṇṭi
ulakaṉittu mīraṭiyā loṭukki, oṉṟum
tarukaveṉā māvaliyic ciṟiyil vitta
tāṭāḷaṉ tāḷaṇivīr, takka kīrtti
arumaṟiyiṉ tiraḷnāṉkum vēḷvi yintum
aṅkaṅkaḷ aviyāṟu micika ḷēḻum,
teruvilmali viḻāvaḷamum ciṟakkum kāḻic
cīrāma viṇṇakarē cērmi ṉīrē. (2)
[[((3.4.1))]]

$$1179

__&&1179__$$3.4.2##__

nāṉmukaṉāḷ mikittarukki yirukku vāymi
nalamikucī rurōmacaṉāl naviṟṟi, nakkaṉ
ūṉmukamār taliyōṭṭū ṇoḻitta venti
oḷimalarckē vaṭiyaṇivīr, uḻucē yōṭac
cūṉmukamār vaḷiyaḷivā yukutta muttit
tolkuruku ciṉiyeṉṉac cūḻnti yaṅka,
tēṉmukamār kamalavayal cēlpāy kāḻic
cīrāma viṇṇakarē cērmi ṉīrē.
[[((3.4.2))]]

$$1180

__&&1180__$$3.4.3##__

viyaṇinta ñutikkōṭṭu varāka moṉṟāy
maṇṇellā miṭanteṭuttu mataṅkaḷ ceytu,
neyyaṇinta tikiriyiṉāl vāṇaṉ tiṇṭōḷ
nērntavantā ḷaṇikiṟpīr, neyta lōṭu
miyaṇinta kuvaḷikaḷtaṅ kaṇka ḷeṉṟum
malarkkumutam vāyeṉṟum kaṭici mārkaḷ,
ceyyaṇintu kaḷikaḷiyā tēṟum kāḻic
cīrāma viṇṇakarē cērmi ṉīrē.
[[((3.4.3))]]

$$1181

__&&1181__$$3.4.4##__

pañciyalmel laṭippiṉṉi tiṟattu muṉṉāḷ
pāyviṭika ḷēḻaṭarttum poṉṉaṉpimpūṇ
neñciṭantu kurutiyuka vukirvē lāṇṭa
niṉmalantā ḷaṇikiṟpīr, nīla mālit
tañcuṭiya viruḷtaḻippat taraḷa māṅkē
taṇmatiyiṉ nilākkāṭṭap pavaḷan taṉṉāl,
ceñcuṭara veyilvirikku maḻakār kāḻic
cīrāma viṇṇakarē cērmi ṉīrē.
[[((3.4.4))]]

$$1182

__&&1182__$$3.4.5##__

tevvāya maṟamaṉṉar kuruti koṇṭu
tirukkulatti liṟantōrkkut tirutti ceytu,
vevvāya mākīṇṭu vēḻa maṭṭa
viṇṇavarkkōṉ tāḷaṇivīr, vikirta mātar
avvāya vāḷneṭuṅkaṇ kuvaḷi kāṭṭa
aravintam mukaṅkāṭṭa arukē yāmpal,
cevvāyiṉ tiraḷkāṭṭum vayalcūḻ kāḻic
cīrāma viṇṇakarē cērmi ṉīrē.
[[((3.4.5))]]

$$1183

__&&1183__$$3.4.6##__

piṅkaṇviṟal cemmukattu vāli māḷap
paṭarvaṉattuk kavantaṉoṭum paṭiyārttiṇki,
veṅkaṇviṟal virātaṉuka viṟku ṉitta
viṇṇavarkkōṉ tāḷaṇivīr, veṟpuppōlum
tuṅkamuka māḷikimē lāyaṅ kūṟum
tuṭiyiṭiyār mukakkamalc cōti taṉṉāl,
tiṅkaḷmukam paṉipaṭikku maḻakār kāḻic
cīrāma viṇṇakarē cērmi ṉīrē.
[[((3.4.6))]]

$$1184

__&&1184__$$3.4.7##__

poruvilvalam puriyarakkaṉ muṭikaḷ pattum
puṟṟumaṟin taṉapōlap puvimēl cinta,
ceruvilvalam puricilikki malittōḷ vēntaṉ
tiruvaṭicērn tuykiṟpīr, tirinīrt teḷki
maruvivalam purikitik kaḻiyū ṭāṭi
vayalnaṇṇi maḻitarunīr tavaḻkāl maṉṉi
teruvilvalam puritaraḷa mīṉum kāḻic
cīrāma viṇṇakarē cērmi ṉīrē.
[[((3.4.7))]]

$$1185

__&&1185__$$3.4.8##__

paṭṭaravē rakalalkul pavaḷac cevvāy
paṇineṭuntōḷ piṇineṭuṅkaṇ pālāmiṉcol,
maṭṭaviḻuṅ kuḻalikkā vāṉōr kāvil
maraṅkoṇarntā ṉaṭiyaṇivīr, aṇilkaḷtāva
neṭṭiliya karuṅkamukiṉ ceṅkāy vīḻa
nīḷpalaviṉ tāḻciṉiyil neruṅku, pīṉat
teṭṭapaḻam citintumatuc coriyum kāḻic
cīrāma viṇṇakarē cērmi ṉīrē.
[[((3.4.8))]]

$$1186

__&&1186__$$3.4.9##__

piṟitaṅku caṭiyāṉi valattē vittup
piramaṉittaṉ ṉuntiyilē tōṟṟu vittu,
kaṟitaṅku vēltaṭaṅkaṇ tiruvi mārpil
kalantavantā ḷaṇikiṟpīr, kaḻunīr kūṭit
tuṟitaṅku kamalattut tuyiṉṟu kitit
tōṭārum poticōṟṟuc cuṇṇamnaṇṇi,
ciṟivaṇṭu kaḷipāṭum vayalcūḻ kāḻic
cīrāma viṇṇakarē cērmi ṉīrē.
[[((3.4.9))]]

$$1187

__&&1187__$$3.4.10##__

ceṅkamalat tayaṉaṉiya maṟiyōr kāḻic
cīrāma viṇṇakareṉ ceṅkaṇ māli
aṅkamalat taṭavayalcū ḻāli nāṭaṉ
aruḷmāri yaraṭṭamukki aṭiyār cīyam
koṅkumalark kuḻaliyarvēḷ maṅki vēntaṉ
koṟṟavēl parakālaṉ kaliyaṉ coṉṉa
caṅkamukat tamiḻmāli pattum vallār
taṭaṅkaṭalcū ḻulakukkut talivar tāmē. (2)
[[((3.4.10))]]

1188__((PTM_3.5))

$$1188

__&&1188__$$3.5.1##__

vantuṉa taṭiyēṉ maṉampukuntāy
pukuntataṟpiṉ vaṇaṅkum,eṉ
cintaṉik kiṉiyāy. tiruvē eṉ ṉāruyirē,
antaḷi raṇiyā racōki ḷiḷantaḷirkaḷ
kalantu, avi yeṅkum
centaḻal puriyum tiruvāli yammāṉē. (2)
[[((3.5.1))]]

$$1189

__&&1189__$$3.5.2##__

nīlat taṭavari māmaṇi nikaḻak
kiṭantatupōl, aravaṇi
vēlit talikkiṭantā yaṭiyēṉ maṉattiruntāy,
cōlit talikkaṇa māmayil naṭamāṭa
maḻimukil pōṉṟeḻuntu, eṅkum
ālip pukikamaḻum aṇiyāli yammāṉē.
[[((3.5.2))]]

$$1190

__&&1190__$$3.5.3##__

neṉṉalpōy varumeṉṟeṉ ṟeṇṇi
yirāmiyeṉ maṉattē pukuntatu,
immik keṉṟiruntē neṟinīr vaḷañceṟuvil,
cenneṟ kūḻi varamporīi arivār
mukatteḻu vāḷipōy, karumpu
annaṟ kāṭaṇiyum aṇiyāli yammāṉē.
[[((3.5.3))]]

$$1191

__&&1191__$$3.5.4##__

miṉṉil maṉṉu ñuṭaṅkiṭi maṭavārtam
cinti maṟantu vantu,niṉ
maṉṉu cēvaṭikkē maṟavāmi vittāyāl,
puṉṉi maṉṉu cerunti vaṇpoḻil
vāyakaṉ paṇikaḷ kalanteṅkum,
aṉṉam maṉṉum vayalaṇi āli yammāṉē.
[[((3.5.4))]]

$$1192

__&&1192__$$3.5.5##__

nīṭu paṉmalar māliyiṭṭu niṉṉiṇiyaṭi
toḻutēttum, eṉmaṉam
vāṭa nīniṉiyēl marameyta māmuṉivā,
pāṭa liṉṉoli caṅki nōci parantu
palpaṇi yālmalintu, eṅkum
āṭa lōciyaṟā aṇiyāli yammāṉē.
[[((3.5.5))]]

$$1193

__&&1193__$$3.5.6##__

kanta māmala reṭṭumiṭṭu niṉkāmar
cēvaṭi kitoḻuteḻum,
puntiyēṉ maṉattē pukuntāyip pōkaloṭṭēṉ,
canti vēḷvi caṭaṅku nāṉmaṟi
ōti yōtuvit tāti yāyvarum,
anta ṇāḷa raṟāvaṇiyāli yammāṉē.
[[((3.5.6))]]

$$1194

__&&1194__$$3.5.7##__

ulavutirik kaṭaṟpaḷḷi koṇṭu vantuṉ
aṭiyēṉ maṉampukunta,ap
pulava. puṇṇiya ṉē.pukun tāyip pōkaloṭṭēṉ,
nilavu malarppuṉṉi nāḻal nīḻal
taṇṭāmari malariṉ mici,mali
alavaṉ kaṇpaṭukkum aṇiyāli yammāṉē.
[[((3.5.7))]]

$$1195

__&&1195__$$3.5.8##__

caṅku taṅku taṭaṅkaṭal kaṭaṉmalli
yuḷkiṭantāy, aruḷpurintu
iṅkeṉṉuḷ pukuntā yiṉippōyi nālaṟiyō,
koṅku ceṇpaka malliki malarppulki
iṉṉiḷa vaṇṭu pōy,iḷamf
teṅkiṉ tātaḷiyum tiruvāli yammāṉē.
[[((3.5.8))]]

$$1196

__&&1196__$$3.5.9##__

ōti yāyira nāmamum paṇintētti
niṉṉaṭin tēṟku,oru poruḷ
vētiyā. ariyā.uriyāy orumāṟṟamentāy,
nīti yākiya vētamā muṉiyāḷar
tōṟṟa murittu, maṟṟavark
kātiyāy iruntāy. aṇiyāli yammāṉē.
[[((3.5.9))]]

$$1197

__&&1197__$$3.5.10##__

pulli vaṇṭaṟiyum poḻil puṭicūḻ
teṉṉāli yorunta māyaṉi,
kalliṉ maṉṉu tiṇṭōḷ kaliya ṉoliceyta,
nalla iṉṉici māli nālumō
rintumoṉ ṟumnaviṉṟu, tāmuṭaṉ
valla rāyurip pārkkiṭa mākum vāṉulakē. (2)
[[((3.5.10))]]

1198__((PTM_3.6))

$$1198

__&&1198__$$3.6.1##__

tūviriya malaruḻakkit
tuṇiyōṭum piriyātē,
pūviriya matunukarum
poṟivariya ciṟuvaṇṭē,
tīviriya maṟivaḷarkkum
pukaḻāḷar tiruvāli,
ēvariveñ ciliyāṉuk
keṉṉilimi yuriyāyē. (2)
[[((3.6.1))]]

$$1199

__&&1199__$$3.6.2##__

piṇiyaviḻu naṟunīla
malarkkiḻiyap peṭiyōṭum,
aṇimalarmēl matunukarum
aṟukāla ciṟuvaṇṭē,
maṇikeḻunīr maruṅkalarum
vayalāli maṇavāḷaṉ,
paṇiyaṟiyēṉ nīceṉṟeṉ
payalinō yuriyāyē.
[[((3.6.2))]]

$$1200

__&&1200__$$3.6.3##__

nīrvāṉam maṇṇerikā
lāyniṉṟa neṭumāl,taṉ
tārāya naṟuntuḷavam
peruntakiyeṟ karuḷāṉē,
cīrārum vaḷarppoḻilcūḻ
tiruvāli vayalvāḻum,
kūrvāya ciṟukurukē.
kuṟippaṟintu kūṟāyē.
[[((3.6.3))]]

$$1201

__&&1201__$$3.6.4##__

tāṉāka niṉiyāṉēl
taṉṉiṉintu nivēṟku,ōr
mīṉāya koṭineṭuvēḷ
valiceyya melivēṉō?
tēṉvāya varivaṇṭē.
tiruvāli nakarāḷum,
āṉāyaṟ keṉṉuṟunō
yaṟiyacceṉ ṟuriyāyē.
[[((3.6.4))]]

$$1202

__&&1202__$$3.6.5##__

vāḷāya kaṇpaṉippa
meṉmulikaḷ poṉṉarumpa
nāṇāḷum niṉṉiṉintu
nivēṟku,ōmaṇṇaḷanta
tāḷāḷā taṇkuṭanti
nakarāḷā variyeṭutta
tōḷāḷā, eṉṟaṉakkōr
tuṇiyāḷa ṉākāyē.
[[((3.6.5))]]

$$1203

__&&1203__$$3.6.6##__

tārāya taṇṭuḷava
vaṇṭuḻuta varimārpaṉ,
pōrāṉik kompocitta
puṭpāka ṉeṉṉammāṉ,
tērārum neṭuvītit
tiruvāli nakarāḷum,
kārāyaṉ eṉṉuṭiya
kaṉavaḷiyum kavarvāṉō.
[[((3.6.6))]]

$$1204

__&&1204__$$3.6.7##__

koṇṭaravat tiriyulavu
kurikaṭalmēl kulavaripōl,
paṇṭaravi ṉaṇikkiṭantu
pāraḷanta paṇpāḷā,
vaṇṭamarum vaḷarppoḻilcūḻ
vayalāli mintā,eṉ
kaṇṭuyilnī koṇṭāykkeṉ
kaṉavaḷiyum kaṭavēṉō.
[[((3.6.7))]]

$$1205

__&&1205__$$3.6.8##__

kuyilālum vaḷarppoḻilcūḻ
taṇkuṭantik kuṭamāṭi,
tuyilāta kaṇṇiṇiyēṉ
niṉṉiṉintu tuyarvēṉō,
muyalālu miḷamatikkē
vaḷiyiḻantēṟku, itunaṭuvē
vayalāli maṇavāḷā.
koḷvāyō maṇiniṟamē.
[[((3.6.8))]]

$$1206

__&&1206__$$3.6.9##__

niliyāḷā niṉvaṇaṅka
vēṇṭāyē yākiṉum,eṉ
muliyāḷa vorunāḷuṉ
ṉakalattāl āḷāyē,
ciliyāḷā marameyta
tiṟalāḷā tirumeyya
maliyāḷā, nīyāḷa
vaḷiyāḷa māṭṭōmē.
[[((3.6.9))]]

$$1207

__&&1207__$$3.6.10##__

miyilaṅku karuṅkuvaḷi
maruṅkalarum vayalāli,
neyyilaṅku cuṭarāḻip
paṭiyāṉi neṭumāli,
kiyilaṅku vēlkaliyaṉ
kaṇṭuritta tamiḻmāli,
aiyiraṇṭu mivivallārk
karuviṉika ḷaṭiyāvē. (2)
[[((3.6.10))]]

1208__((PTM_3.7))

$$1208

__&&1208__$$3.7.1##__

kaḷvaṉkol yāṉaṟiyēṉ
kariyāṉoru kāḷivantu,
vaḷḷimaruṅ kuleṉṟaṉ
maṭamāṉiṉip pōtaveṉṟu,
veḷḷivaḷik kippaṟṟap
peṟṟatāyari viṭṭakaṉṟu,
aḷḷalam pūṅkaḻaṉi
yaṇiyāli pukuvarkkolō. (2)
[[((3.7.1))]]

$$1209

__&&1209__$$3.7.2##__

paṇṭiva ṉāyaṉnaṅkāy.
paṭiṟaṉpukuntu, eṉmakaḷtaṉ
toṇṭiyañ ceṅkaṉivāy
nukarntāṉi yukantu,avaṉpiṉ
keṇṭiyoṇ kaṇmiḷirak
kiḷipōlmiḻaṟ ṟinaṭantu,
vaṇṭamar kāṉalmalkum
vayalāli pukuvarkkolō.
[[((3.7.2))]]

$$1210

__&&1210__$$3.7.3##__

añcuvaṉ veñcolnaṅkāy.
arakkarkkulap pāvitaṉṉi,
veñciṉa mūkkarinta
viṟalōntiṟaṅ kēṭkil,meyyē
pañciyal mellaṭiyem
paṇittōḷi parakkaḻintu,
vañciyan taṇpaṇicūḻ
vayalāli pukuvarkkolō.
[[((3.7.3))]]

$$1211

__&&1211__$$3.7.4##__

ētuavaṉ tolpiṟappu
iḷiya vaṉvaḷi yūti,maṉṉar
tūtuva ṉāyavaṉūr
coluvīrkaḷ. colīraṟiyēṉ,
mātavaṉ tantuṇiyā
naṭantāḷtaṭañ cūḻpuṟavil,
pōtuvaṇ ṭāṭucemmal
puṉalāli pukuvarkkolō.
[[((3.7.4))]]

$$1212

__&&1212__$$3.7.5##__

tāyeṉi yeṉṟiraṅkāḷ
taṭantōḷi taṉakkaminta,
māyaṉi mātavaṉi
matitteṉṉi yakaṉṟivaḷ,
vēyaṉa tōḷviciṟip
peṭiyaṉṉa meṉanaṭantu,
pōyiṉa pūṅkoṭiyāḷ
puṉalāli pukuvarkkolō.
[[((3.7.5))]]

$$1213

__&&1213__$$3.7.6##__

entuṇi yeṉṟeṭuttēṟ
kiṟiyēṉu miraṅkiṟṟilaḷ,
taṉtuṇi yāyaveṉṟaṉ
taṉimikku miraṅkiṟṟilaḷ,
vaṉtuṇi vāṉavarkkāy
varañceṟṟaraṅ kattuṟiyum,
intuṇi vaṉṉoṭumpō
yeḻilāli pukuvarkkolō. (2)
[[((3.7.6))]]

$$1214

__&&1214__$$3.7.7##__

aṉṉiyu mattaṉumeṉ
ṟaṭiyōmuk kiraṅkiṟṟilaḷ,
piṉṉitaṉ kātalaṉṟaṉ
peruntōḷnalam pēṇiṉaḷāl,
miṉṉiyum vañciyiyum
veṉṟilaṅku miṭiyāḷnaṭantu,
puṉṉiyum aṉṉamumcūḻ
puṉalāli pukuvarkkolō.
[[((3.7.7))]]

$$1215

__&&1215__$$3.7.8##__

muṟṟilum piṅkiḷiyum
pantumūcalum pēcukiṉṟa,
ciṟṟilmeṉ pūviyumviṭ
ṭakaṉṟaceḻuṅ kōtitaṉṉi,
peṟṟilēṉ muṟṟiḻiyip
piṟappilipiṉ ṉēnaṭantu,
maṟṟellām kitoḻappōy
vayalāli pukuvarkkolō.
[[((3.7.8))]]

$$1216

__&&1216__$$3.7.9##__

kāviyaṅ kaṇṇiyeṇṇil
kaṭimāmalarp pāviyoppāḷ,
pāviyēṉ peṟṟamiyāl
paṇittōḷi parakkaḻintu,
tūvicē raṉṉamaṉṉa
naṭiyāḷneṭu māloṭumpōy,
vāviyan taṇpaṇicūḻ
vayalāli pukuvarkkolō.
[[((3.7.9))]]

$$1217

__&&1217__$$3.7.10##__

tāymaṉam niṉṟiraṅkat
taṉiyēneṭu māltuṇiyā,
pōyiṉa pūṅkoṭiyāḷ
puṉalāli pukuvareṉṟu,
kāyciṉa vēlkaliya
ṉoliceytamiḻ mālipattum,
mēviya neñcuṭiyār
tañcamāvatu viṇṇulakē. (2)
[[((3.7.10))]]

1218__((PTM_3.8))

$$1218

__&&1218__$$3.8.1##__

nantā viḷakkē aḷattaṟ kariyāy.
naranā raṇaṉē karumā mukilpōl
entāy, emakkē yaruḷāy, eṉaniṉṟu
imiyōr paravu miṭam,et ticiyum
kantā ramantē ṉicipāṭamāṭē
kaḷivaṇ ṭumiḻaṟ ṟaniḻal tutintu,
mantā raniṉṟu maṇamal kunāṅkūr
maṇimāṭak kōyil vaṇaṅkeṉ maṉaṉē. (2)
[[((3.8.1))]]

$$1219

__&&1219__$$3.8.2##__

mutalit taṉimā muraṇtīra vaṉṟu
mutunīrt taṭattuc ceṅkaṇvēḻa muyya,
vitalit talicceṉ ṟataṟkē yutavi
viṉitīrtta vammāṉiṭam,viṇṇaṇavum
patalik kapōtat toḷimāṭa neṟṟip
pavaḷak koḻuṅkāl piṅkāl puṟavam,
matalit talimeṉ peṭikūṭu nāṅkūr
maṇimāṭak kōyil vaṇaṅkeṉ maṉaṉē.
[[((3.8.2))]]

$$1220

__&&1220__$$3.8.3##__

kolippuṇ talikkuṉṟa moṉṟuyya vaṉṟu
koṭumā mutalik kiṭarceytu, koṅkār
ilippuṇṭa rīkat tavaḷiṉpa maṉpō
ṭaṇintiṭṭa vammāṉiṭam,āḷariyāl
alippuṇṭa yāṉi maruppu makilum
aṇimuttum veṇcā mariyōṭu,poṉṉi
malippaṇṭa maṇṭat tiriyuntu nāṅkūr
maṇimāṭak kōyil vaṇaṅkeṉ maṉaṉē.
[[((3.8.3))]]

$$1221

__&&1221__$$3.8.4##__

ciṟiyār uvaṇappuḷ ḷoṉṟēṟi yaṉṟu
ticināṉkum nāṉku miriya, ceruvil
kaṟiyār neṭuvē larakkar maṭiyak
kaṭalcū ḻilaṅki kaṭantā ṉiṭantāṉ,
muṟiyāl vaḷarkkiṉṟa muttīyar nālvētar
aivēḷvi yāṟaṅkar ēḻi ṉiciyōr,
maṟiyōr vaṇaṅkap pukaḻeytu nāṅkūr
maṇimāṭak kōyil vaṇaṅkeṉ maṉaṉē.
[[((3.8.4))]]

$$1222

__&&1222__$$3.8.5##__

iḻiyāṭu koṅkit talinañca muṇṭiṭṭu
iḷaṅkaṉṟu koṇṭu viḷaṅkā yeṟintu,
taḻivāṭa vantāḷ kurunta mocittut
taṭantāma rippoyki pukkāṉi ṭantāṉ,
kuḻiyāṭa vallik kulamāṭamāṭē
kuyilkūva nīṭu koṭimāṭa malku,
maḻiyāṭu cōli mayilālu nāṅkūr,
maṇimāṭak kōyil vaṇaṅkeṉ maṉaṉē.
[[((3.8.5))]]

$$1223

__&&1223__$$3.8.6##__

paṇṇēr moḻiyāyc ciyarañca vañcap
pakuvāyk kaḻutuk kiraṅkātu, avaḷtaṉ
uṇṇā mulimaṟ ṟavaḷāvi yōṭum
uṭaṉē cuvittā niṭam,ōṅku pintāḷ
kaṇṇār karumpiṉ kaḻitiṉṟu vikik
kaḻunīril mūḻkic ceḻunīrt taṭattu,
maṇṇēn tiḷamēti kaḷviku nāṅkūr
maṇimāṭak kōyil vaṇaṅkeṉ maṉaṉē.
[[((3.8.6))]]

$$1224

__&&1224__$$3.8.7##__

taḷikkaṭ ṭaviḻtā mariviku poykit
taṭampukku aṭaṅkā viṭaṅkā laravam,
iḷikkat tiḷittiṭ ṭataṉucci taṉmēl
aṭivitta ammā ṉiṭam,māmatiyam
tiḷikkum koṭimāḷikicūḻ teruvil
ceḻumuttu veṇṇeṟ keṉacceṉṟu,mūṉṟil
vaḷikki nuḷippāvi yarmāṟu nāṅkūr
maṇimāṭak kōyil vaṇaṅkeṉ maṉaṉē.
[[((3.8.7))]]

$$1225

__&&1225__$$3.8.8##__

tuḷiyār karumeṉ kuḻalāycci yartam
tukilvāri yumciṟṟil citittum, muṟṟā
viḷiyār viḷiyāṭ ṭoṭukātal veḷḷam
viḷivitta vammāṉiṭam,vēl neṭuṅkaṇ
muḷivāḷeyiṟṟu maṭavār payiṟṟu
moḻikēṭ ṭiruntu mutirātaviṉcol,
vaḷivāya kiḷḷi maṟipāṭu nāṅkūr
maṇimāṭak kōyil vaṇaṅkeṉ maṉaṉē.
[[((3.8.8))]]

$$1226

__&&1226__$$3.8.9##__

viṭiyōṭa veṉṟāycci mentōḷnayanta
vikirtā viḷaṅku cuṭarāḻi yeṉṉum,
paṭiyōṭu caṅkoṉ ṟuṭiyāy eṉaniṉṟu
imiyōr paravu miṭam,pin taṭattup
peṭiyōṭu ceṅkāla vaṉṉam tukippat
tokippuṇṭa rīkatti ṭicceṅ kaḻunīr,
maṭiyōṭa niṉṟu matuvimmu nāṅkūr
maṇimāṭak kōyil vaṇaṅkeṉ maṉaṉē.
[[((3.8.9))]]

$$1227

__&&1227__$$3.8.10##__

vaṇṭār poḻilcūḻn taḻakāya nāṅkūr
maṇimāṭak kōyil neṭumālukku,eṉṟum
toṇṭāya tolcīr vayalmaṅ kiyarkkōṉ
kaliya nolicey tamiḻmāli vallār,
kaṇṭār vaṇaṅkak kaḷiyāṉi mītē
kaṭalcū ḻulakuk korukā valarāy,
viṇṭōy neṭuveṇ kuṭinīḻa liṉkīḻ
virinī rulakāṇ ṭuvirum puvarē. (2)
[[((3.8.10))]]

1228__((PTM_3.9))

$$1228

__&&1228__$$3.9.1##__

calaṅkoṇṭa iraṇiyaṉa
takalmārvam kīṇṭu
taṭaṅkaṭalik kaṭintamutaṅ
koṇṭukanta kāḷi,
nalaṅkoṇṭa karumukilpōl
tirumēṉi yammāṉ
nāṭōṟum makiḻntiṉitu
maruviyuṟi kōyil,
calaṅkoṇṭu malarcoriyum
mallikiyoṇ cerunti
caṇpakaṅkaḷ maṇanāṟum
vaṇpoḻili ṉūṭē,
valaṅkoṇṭu kayalōṭi
viḷiyāṭu nāṅkūr
vikunta viṇṇakaram
vaṇaṅkumaṭa neñcē. (2)
[[((3.9.1))]]

$$1229

__&&1229__$$3.9.2##__

tiṇṇiyatō rariyuruvāyt
ticiyaṉittum naṭuṅkat
tēvaroṭu tāṉavarkaḷ
ticippa,iraṇiyaṉi
naṇṇiyavaṉ mārvakalat
tukirmaṭutta nātaṉ
nāṭōṟum makiḻntiṉitu
maruviyuṟi kōyil,
eṇṇilmiku peruñcelvat
teḻilviḷaṅku maṟiyum
ēḻiciyum kēḷvikaḷu
miyaṉṟaperuṅ kuṇattōr,
maṇṇilmiku maṟiyavarkaḷ
maliveytu nāṅkūr
vikunta viṇṇakaram
vaṇaṅkumaṭa neñcē.
[[((3.9.2))]]

$$1230

__&&1230__$$3.9.3##__

aṇṭamumiv valikaṭalu
mavaṉikaḷu mellām
amutuceyta tiruvayiṟṟaṉ
araṉkoṇṭu tiriyum,
muṇṭamatu niṟittavaṉkaṇ
cāpamatu nīkkum
mutalvaṉavaṉ makiḻntiṉitu
maruviyuṟi kōyil,
eṇṭiciyum peruñcenna
liḷanteṅku katali
ilikkoṭiyoṇ kulikkamuko
ṭikalivaḷam coriya
vaṇṭupala vicipāṭa
mayilālu nāṅkūr
vikunta viṇṇakaram
vaṇaṅkumaṭa neñcē.
[[((3.9.3))]]

$$1231

__&&1231__$$3.9.4##__

kaliyilaṅku makalalkul
arakkarkkulak koṭiyik
kātoṭumūk kuṭaṉariyak
kataṟiyava ḷōṭi,
taliyilaṅki vittumali
yilaṅkipukac ceyta
taṭantōḷaṉ makiḻntiṉitu
maruviyuṟi kōyil,
ciliyilaṅku maṇimāṭat
tuccimicic cūlam
ceḻuṅkoṇṭa lakaṭiriyac
corintaceḻu muttam,
maliyilaṅku māḷikimēl
maliveytu nāṅkūr
vikunta viṇṇakaram
vaṇaṅkumaṭa neñcē.
[[((3.9.4))]]

$$1232

__&&1232__$$3.9.5##__

miṉṉaṉiya ñuṇmaruṅkul
melliyaṟkā yilaṅki
vēntaṉmuṭi yorupatumtō
ḷirupatumpō yutira
taṉnikaril cilivaḷittaṉ
ṟilaṅkipoṭi ceyta
taṭantōḷaṉ makiḻntiṉitu
maruviyuṟi kōyil,
cenneloṭu ceṅkamalam
cēlkayalkaḷ vāḷi
ceṅkaḻunī roṭumiṭintu
kaḻaṉitikaḻn teṅkum,
maṉṉupukaḻ vētiyarkaḷ
maliveytu nāṅkūr
vikunta viṇṇakaram
vaṇaṅkumaṭa neñcē.
[[((3.9.5))]]

$$1233

__&&1233__$$3.9.6##__

peṇmimiku vaṭivukoṭu
vantavaḷip periya
pēyiṉatu uruvukoṭu
māḷavuyi ruṇṭu
tiṇmimiku marutoṭunaṟ
cakaṭamiṟut taruḷum
tēvaṉavaṉ makiḻntiṉitu
maruviyuṟi kōyil,
uṇmimiku maṟiyoṭunaṟ
kalikaḷniṟi poṟikaḷ
utavukoṭi yeṉṟivaṟṟi
ṉoḻivillā, periya
vaṇmimiku maṟiyavarkaḷ
maliveytu nāṅkūr
vikunta viṇṇakaram
vaṇaṅkumaṭa neñcē.
[[((3.9.6))]]

$$1234

__&&1234__$$3.9.7##__

viḷaṅkaṉiyi yiḷaṅkaṉṟu
koṇṭutira veṟintu
vēlneṭuṅka ṇāycciyarkaḷ
vittatayir veṇṇey
uḷaṅkuḷira amutuceytiv
vulakuṇṭa kāḷi
ukantiṉitu nāṭōṟum
maruviyuṟi kōyil,
iḷampaṭinaṟ kamukukulit
teṅkukoṭic cennel
īṉkarumpu kaṇvaḷarak
kāltaṭavum puṉalāl,
vaḷaṅkoṇṭa peruñcelvam
vaḷarumaṇi nāṅkūr
vikunta viṇṇakaram
vaṇaṅkumaṭa neñcē.
[[((3.9.7))]]

$$1235

__&&1235__$$3.9.8##__

āṟāta ciṉattiṉmiku
narakaṉura maḻitta
aṭalāḻit taṭakkiyaṉ
alarmakaṭkum araṟkum,
kūṟākak koṭuttaruḷum
tiruvuṭampaṉ imiyōr
kulamutalvaṉ makiḻntiṉitu
maruviyuṟi kōyil,
māṟāta malarkkamalam
ceṅkaḻunīr tatumpi
matuveḷḷa moḻukavaya
luḻavarmaṭi yaṭippa,
māṟāta peruñcelvam
vaḷarumaṇi nāṅkūr
vikunta viṇṇakaram
vaṇaṅkumaṭa neñcē.
[[((3.9.8))]]

$$1236

__&&1236__$$3.9.9##__

vaṅkamali taṭaṅkaṭaluḷ
vāṉavarka ḷōṭu
māmuṉivar palarkūṭi
māmalarkaḷ tūvi,
eṅkaḷtaṉi nāyakaṉē
emakkaruḷāy eṉṉum
īcaṉavaṉ makiḻntiṉitu
maruviyuṟi kōyil,
ceṅkayalum vāḷikaḷum
cenneliṭik kutippac
cēlukaḷum ceḻumpaṇicūḻ
vītitoṟum miṭintu,
maṅkulmati yakaṭuriñcu
maṇimāṭa nāṅkūr
vikunta viṇṇakaram
vaṇaṅkumaṭa neñcē.
[[((3.9.9))]]

$$1237

__&&1237__$$3.9.10##__

caṅkumali taṇṭumutal
cakkaramuṉēntum
tāmarikkaṇ neṭiyapirāṉ
tāṉamarum kōyil,
vaṅkamali kaṭalulakil
maliveytu nāṅkūr
vikunta viṇṇakarmēl
vaṇṭaṟiyum poḻilcūḻ,
maṅkiyartam talivaṉmaru
valartamuṭal tuṇiya
vāḷvīcum parakālaṉ
kalikaṉṟi coṉṉa,
caṅkamali tamiḻmāli
pattivivallārkaḷ
taraṇiyoṭu vicumpāḷum
taṉmipeṟu vārē. (2)
[[((3.9.10))]]

1238__((PTM_3.10))

$$1238

__&&1238__$$3.10.1##__

tirumaṭanti maṇmaṭanti
yirupālum tikaḻat
tīviṉikaḷ pōyakala
aṭiyavarkaṭ keṉṟum
aruḷnaṭantu,iv vēḻulakat
tavarppaṇiya vāṉōr
amarntētta iruntaviṭam
perumpukaḻvē tiyarvāḻ
tarumiṭaṅkaḷ malarkaḷmiku
kitikaḷceṅk kaḻunīr
tāmarikaḷ taṭaṅkaṭoṟu
miṭaṅkaṭoṟum tikaḻa,
aruviṭaṅkaḷ poḻiltaḻuvi
yeḻiltikaḻu nāṅkūr
arimēya viṇṇakaram
vaṇaṅkumaṭa neñcē. (2)
[[((3.10.1))]]

$$1239

__&&1239__$$3.10.2##__

veṉṟimiku narakaṉura
matuvaḻiya viciṟum
viṟalāḻit taṭakkiyaṉ
viṇṇavarkaṭku, aṉṟu
kuṉṟukoṭu kurikaṭalik
kaṭintamuta maḷikkum
kurumaṇiyeṉ ṉāramutam
kulaviyuṟi kōyil,
eṉṟumiku peruñcelvat
teḻilviḷaṅku maṟiyōr
ēḻiciyum kēḷvikaḷu
miyaṉṟaperuṅ kuṇattōr,
aṉṟulakam paṭittavaṉē
yaṉiyavarkaḷ nāṅkūr
arimēya viṇṇakaram
vaṇaṅkumaṭa neñcē.
[[((3.10.2))]]

$$1240

__&&1240__$$3.10.3##__

umparumiv vēḻulaku
mēḻkaṭalu mellām
uṇṭapirāṉṇṭarkaḷmuṉ
kaṇṭumakiḻa veyta,
kumpamiku matayāṉi
maruppocittuk kañcaṉ
kuñcipiṭit taṭittapirāṉ
kōyil,maruṅ keṅkum
pimpoṉoṭu veṇmuttam
palapuṉṉi kāṭṭap
palaṅkaṉikaḷ tēṉkāṭṭap
paṭavaravē ralkul,
ampaṉiya kaṇmaṭavār
makiḻveytu nāṅkūr
arimēya viṇṇakaram
vaṇaṅkumaṭa neñcē.
[[((3.10.3))]]

$$1241

__&&1241__$$3.10.4##__

ōṭāta vāḷariyi
ṉuruvamatu koṇṭu aṉ
ṟulappilmiku peruvaratta
viraṇiyaṉip paṟṟi,
vāṭāta vaḷḷukirāl
piḷantavaṉṟaṉ makaṉuk
karuḷceytāṉ vāḻumiṭam
mallikiceṅ kaḻunīr,
cēṭēṟu malarccerunti
ceḻuṅkamukam pāḷi
ceṇpakaṅkaḷ maṇanāṟum
vaṇpoḻili ṉūṭē,
āṭēṟu vayalālip
pukikamaḻu nāṅkūr
arimēya viṇṇakaram
vaṇaṅkumaṭa neñcē.
[[((3.10.4))]]

$$1242

__&&1242__$$3.10.5##__

kaṇṭavartam maṉammakiḻa
māvalitaṉ vēḷvik
kaḷavilmiku ciṟukuṟaḷāy
mūvaṭiyeṉ ṟirantiṭṭu,
aṇṭamumiv valikaṭalu
mavaṉikaḷu mellām
aḷantapirā ṉamarumiṭam
vaḷaṅkoḷpoḻi layalē,
aṇṭamuṟu muḻavoliyum
vaṇṭiṉaṅka ḷoliyum
arumaṟiyi ṉoliyummaṭa
vārcilampi ṉoliyum,
aṇṭamuṟu malikaṭali
ṉolitikaḻu nāṅkūr
arimēya viṇṇakaram
vaṇaṅkumaṭa neñcē.
[[((3.10.5))]]

$$1243

__&&1243__$$3.10.6##__

vāṇeṭuṅkaṇ malarkkūntal
mitilikkā ilaṅki
maṉṉaṉmuṭi yorupatumtō
ḷirupatumpō yutira,
tāṇeṭuntiṉ cilivaḷitta
tayarataṉcēy eṉtaṉ
taṉiccaraṇvā ṉavarkkaracu
karutumiṭam, taṭamār
cēṇiṭaṅkoḷ malarkkamalam
cēlkayalkaḷ vāḷi
cenneloṭu maṭuttariya
vutirntaceḻu muttam,
vāṇeṭuṅkaṇ kaṭiciyarkaḷ
vārumaṇi nāṅkūr
arimēya viṇṇakaram
vaṇaṅkumaṭa neñcē.
[[((3.10.6))]]

$$1244

__&&1244__$$3.10.7##__

tīmaṉattāṉ kañcaṉatu
vañcaṉiyil tiriyum
tēṉukaṉum pūtaṉita
ṉāruyirum cekuttāṉ,
kāmaṉittāṉ payantakaru
mēṉiyuṭi yammāṉ
karutumiṭam porutupuṉal
tuṟituṟimut tunti,
nāmaṉattāl mantiraṅkaḷ
nālvētam aintu
vēḷviyō ṭāṟaṅkam
naviṉṟukali payiṉṟu,aṅ
kāmaṉattu maṟiyavarkaḷ
payilumaṇi nāṅkūr
arimēya viṇṇakaram
vaṇaṅkumaṭa neñcē.
[[((3.10.7))]]

$$1245

__&&1245__$$3.10.8##__

kaṉṟataṉāl viḷaveṟintu
kaṉiyutirtta kāḷi
kāmarucīr mukilvaṇṇaṉ
kālikaḷmuṉ kāppāṉ,
kuṉṟataṉāl maḻitaṭuttuk
kuṭamāṭu kūttaṉ
kulavumiṭam koṭimatiḷkaḷ
māḷikikō puraṅkaḷ,
tuṉṟumaṇi maṇṭapaṅkaḷ
cālikaḷtū maṟiyōr
tokkīṇṭit toḻutiyoṭu
mikappayilum cōli,
aṉṟalarvāy matuvuṇṭaṅ
kaḷimuralu nāṅkūr
arimēya viṇṇakaram
vaṇaṅkumaṭa neñcē.
[[((3.10.8))]]

$$1246

__&&1246__$$3.10.9##__

vañcaṉiyāl vantavaḷta
ṉuyiruṇṭu vāytta
tayiruṇṭu veṇṇeyamu
tuṇṭu,vali mikka
kañcaṉuyi ratuvuṇṭiv
vulakuṇṭa kāḷi
karutumiṭam kāvirican
takilkaṉaka munti,
mañculavu poḻilūṭum
vayalūṭum vantu
vaḷaṅkoṭuppa māmaṟiyōr
māmalarkaḷ tūvi,
añcalittaṅ karicaraṇeṉ
ṟiriñcumaṇi nāṅkūr
arimēya viṇṇakaram
vaṇaṅkumaṭa neñcē.
[[((3.10.9))]]

$$1247

__&&1247__$$3.10.10##__

ceṉṟuciṉa viṭiyēḻum
paṭavaṭarttup piṉṉi
cevvittōḷ puṇarntukanta
tirumāltaṉ kōyil,
aṉṟayaṉu maraṉcēyu
maṉiyavarkaḷ nāṅkūr
arimēya viṇṇakara
mamarntaceḻuṅ kuṉṟi,
kaṉṟineṭu vēlvalavaṉ
maṅkiyartam kōmāṉ
kalikaṉṟi yolimāli
yintiṉoṭu mūṉṟum,
oṉṟiṉoṭu moṉṟumivi
kaṟṟuvallār ulakat
tuttamarkaṭ kuttamarā
yumparumā varkaḷē. (2)
[[((3.10.10))]]

periya tirumoḻi nāṉkām pattu

1248__((PTM_4.1))

$$1248

__&&1248__$$4.1.1##__

pōtalarnta poḻilcōlip
puṟameṅkum porutirikaḷ
tātutira vantalikkum
taṭamaṇṇit teṉkarimēl
mātavaṉṟā ṉuṟiyumiṭam
vayalnāṅki varivaṇṭu
tēteṉaveṉ ṟicipāṭum
tiruttēva ṉārtokiyē
[[((4.1.1))]]

$$1249

__&&1249__$$4.1.2##__

yāvarumā yāviyumā
yeḻilvētap poruḷkaḷumāy
mūvarumāy mutalāya
mūrttiyamarn tuṟiyumiṭam,
māvarumtiṇ paṭimaṉṉi
veṉṟikoḷvār maṉṉunāṅki
tēvarumceṉ ṟiṟiñcupoḻil
tiruttēva ṉārtokiyē
[[((4.1.2))]]

$$1250

__&&1250__$$4.1.3##__

vāṉāṭum maṇṇāṭum
maṟṟuḷḷa palluyirum
tāṉāya vemperumāṉ
talivaṉamarn tuṟiyumiṭam,
āṉāta peruñcelvat
tarumaṟiyōr nāṅkitaṉṉuḷ
tēṉāru malarpoḻilcūḻ
tiruttēva ṉārtokiyē
[[((4.1.3))]]

$$1251

__&&1251__$$4.1.4##__

intiraṉu mimiyavarum
muṉivarkaḷum eḻilaminta
cantamalarc catumukaṉum
katiravaṉum cantiraṉum,
entiyemak karuḷ, eṉaniṉ
ṟaruḷumiṭam eḻilnāṅki
cuntaranal poḻilpuṭicūḻ
tiruttēva ṉārtokiyē
[[((4.1.4))]]

$$1252

__&&1252__$$4.1.5##__

aṇṭamumiv valikaṭalu
mavaṉikaḷum kulavariyum
uṇṭapirā ṉuṟiyumiṭam
ōḷimaṇican takilkaṉakam,
teṇṭirikaḷ varattiraṭṭum
tikaḻmaṇṇit teṉkarimēl,
tiṇtiṟalār payilnāṅkit
tiruttēva ṉārtokiyē
[[((4.1.5))]]

$$1253

__&&1253__$$4.1.6##__

ñālamellā mamutuceytu
nāṉmaṟiyum toṭarāta
pālakaṉā yāliliyil
paḷḷikoḷḷum paramaṉiṭam,
cālivaḷam perukivarum
taṭamaṇṇit teṉkarimēl
cēlukaḷum vayalnāṅkit
tiruttēva ṉārtokiyē
[[((4.1.6))]]

$$1254

__&&1254__$$4.1.7##__

ōṭāta vāḷariyi
ṉuruvāki yiraṇiyaṉi
vāṭāta vaḷḷukirāl
piḷantaḷinta mālatiṭam,
ēṭēṟu peruñcelvat
teḻilmaṟiyōr nāṅkitaṉṉuḷ,
cēṭēṟu poḻiltaḻuvu
tiruttēva ṉārtokiyē
[[((4.1.7))]]

$$1255

__&&1255__$$4.1.8##__

vārāru miḷaṅkoṅki
mitiliyi maṇampuṇarvāṉ,
kārārtiṇ ciliyiṟutta
taṉikkāḷi karutumiṭam
ērārum peruñcelvat
teḻilmaṟiyōr nāṅkitaṉṉuḷ,
cīrārum malarpoḻilcūḻ
tiruttēva ṉārtokiyē
[[((4.1.8))]]

$$1256

__&&1256__$$4.1.9##__

kumpamiku matayāṉi
pākaṉoṭum kulintuviḻa
kompataṉip paṟitteṟinta
kūttaṉamarn tuṟiyumiṭam,
vampaviḻum ceṇpakattiṉ
maṇaṅkamaḻum nāṅkitaṉṉuḷ,
cempoṉmatiḷ poḻilpuṭicūḻ
tiruttēva ṉārtokiyē
[[((4.1.9))]]

$$1257

__&&1257__$$4.1.10##__

kārārnta tirumēṉik
kaṇṇaṉamarn tuṟiyumiṭam,
cīrārnta poḻilnāṅkit
tiruttēva ṉārtokimēl
kūrārnta vēṟkaliyaṉ
kūṟutamiḻ pattumvallār
erārnta vikuntat
timiyavarō ṭiruppārē
[[((4.1.10))]]

1258__((PTM_4.2))

$$1258

__&&1258__$$4.2.1##__

kampa mākaṭa laṭittilaṅ kikkumaṉ
katirmuṭi yavipattum
ampi ṉālaṟuttu, aracavaṉ tampikku
aḷittava ṉuṟikōyil
cempa lāniri ceṇpakam mātavi
cūtakam vāḻikaḷcūḻ
vampu lāmkamu kōṅkiya nāṅkūr
vaṇpuru ṭōttamamē
[[((4.2.1))]]

$$1259

__&&1259__$$4.2.2##__

palla vanftikaḻ pūṅkaṭam pēṟiyak
kāḷiyaṉ paṇavaraṅkil,
olli vantuṟap pāyntaru naṭañceyta
umparkkō ṉuṟikōyil,
nalla ventaḻal mūṉṟunāl vētami
vēḷviyō ṭāṟaṅkam,
valla vantaṇar malkiya nāṅkūr
vaṇpuru ṭōttamamē
[[((4.2.2))]]

$$1260

__&&1260__$$4.2.3##__

aṇṭa rāṉavar vāṉavar kōṉukkeṉ
ṟamittacō ṟatuvellām
uṇṭu, kōniri mēyttavi kāttavaṉ
ukantiṉi tuṟikōyil,
koṇṭa lārmuḻa vilkuḷir vārpoḻil
kulamayil naṭamāṭa,
vaṇṭu tāṉici pāṭiṭu nāṅkūr
vaṇpuru ṭōttamamē
[[((4.2.3))]]

$$1261

__&&1261__$$4.2.4##__

paruṅki yāṉiyiṉ kompiṉip paṟittataṉ
pākaṉic cāṭippukku,
oṟuṅka mallarik koṉṟupiṉ kañcaṉi
utittava ṉuṟikōyil,
karumpiṉūṭuyar cālikaḷ viḷitaru
kaḻaṉiyil malivāvi
maruṅke lāmpoḻi lōṅkiya nāṅkūr
vaṇpuru ṭōttamamē
[[((4.2.4))]]

$$1262

__&&1262__$$4.2.5##__

cāṭu pōyviḻat tāḷnimirt tīcaṉtaṉ
paṭiyoṭuṅ kiḷiyōṭum
ōṭa vāṇaṉi yāyiran tōḷkaḷum
tuṇittava ṉuṟikōyil,
āṭu vāṉkoṭi yakalvicum paṇavippōyp
pakalava ṉoḷimaṟikkum
māṭa māḷiki cūḻtaru nāṅkūr
vaṇpuru ṭōttamamē
[[((4.2.5))]]

$$1263

__&&1263__$$4.2.6##__

aṅki yālaṭi mūṉṟunī rēṟṟayaṉ
alarkoṭu toḻutētta,
kaṅki pōtarak kālnimirt taruḷiya
kaṇṇaṉvan tuṟikōyil,
koṅki kōṅkavi kāṭṭavāy kumutaṅkaḷ
kāṭṭamā patumaṅkaḷ,
maṅki mārmukam kāṭṭiṭu nāṅkūr
vaṇpuru ṭōttamamē
[[((4.2.6))]]

$$1264

__&&1264__$$4.2.7##__

uḷiya voṇṭiṟal poṉpeya rōṉtaṉa
turampiḷan tutiratti
aḷiyum, veñciṉat taripari kīṟiya
appaṉvan tuṟikōyil,
iḷiya maṅkiya riṇiyaṭic cilampiṉō
ṭeḻilkoḷpan taṭippōr,ki
vaḷiyil niṉṟoli malkiya nāṅkūr
vaṇpuru ṭōttamamē
[[((4.2.7))]]

$$1265

__&&1265__$$4.2.8##__

vāḷi yārtaṭan kaṇṇumi paṅkaṉvaṉ
cāpamaṟ ṟatunīṅka
mūḷi yārcirat tiyamuṉ aḷittavem
mukilvaṇṇa ṉuṟikōyil
pāḷi vāṉkamu kūṭuyar teṅkiṉvaṉ
paḻamviḻa veruvippōy
vāḷi pāytaṭam cūḻtaru nāṅkūr
vaṇpuru ṭōttamamē
[[((4.2.8))]]

$$1266

__&&1266__$$4.2.9##__

intu vārcaṭi yīcaṉip payantanāṉ
mukaṉittaṉ ṉeḻilārum
unti māmalar mīmicip paṭittavaṉ
ukantiṉi tuṟikōyil,
kunti vāḻiyiṉ koḻuṅkaṉi ñukarntutaṉ
kuruḷiyit taḻuvippōy,
manti māmpaṇi mēlviku nāṅkūr
vaṇpuru ṭōttamamē
[[((4.2.9))]]

$$1267

__&&1267__$$4.2.10##__

maṇṇu ḷārpukaḻ vētiyar nāṅkūr
vaṇpuru ṭōttamattuḷ,
aṇṇal cēvaṭik kīḻaṭin tuyntavaṉ
ālimaṉ aruḷmāri,
paṇṇu ḷārtarap pāṭiya pāṭalip
pattumval lār,ulakil
eṇṇi lātapē riṉpamuṟ ṟimiyava
rōṭum kūṭuvarē
[[((4.2.10))]]

1268__((PTM_4.3))

$$1268

__&&1268__$$4.3.1##__

pēraṇin tulakat tavartoḻu tēttum
pēraru ḷāḷaṉem pirāṉi,
vāraṇi muliyāḷ malarmaka ḷōṭu
maṇmaka ḷumuṭaṉ niṟpa,
cīraṇi māṭa nāṅkinaṉ ṉaṭuvuḷ
cempoṉcey kōyili ṉuḷḷē,
kāraṇi mēkam niṉṟatop pāṉik
kaṇṭukoṇ ṭuyntoḻin tēṉē
[[((4.3.1))]]

$$1269

__&&1269__$$4.3.2##__

piṟappoṭu mūppoṉ ṟillavaṉ ṟaṉṉip
pētiyā viṉpaveḷ ḷatti,
iṟappetir kālak kaḻivumā ṉāṉi
ēḻici yiṉcuvi taṉṉi,
ciṟappuṭi maṟiyōr nāṅkinaṉ ṉaṭuvuḷ
cempoṉcey kōyili ṉuḷḷē,
maṟipperum poruḷi vāṉavar kōṉik
kaṇṭunāṉ vāḻntoḻin tēṉē
[[((4.3.2))]]

$$1270

__&&1270__$$4.3.3##__

tiṭavicum perinīr tiṅkaḷum cuṭarum
ceḻunilat tuyirkaḷum maṟṟum,
paṭarporuḷ kaḷumāy niṉṟavaṉ ṟaṉṉi,
paṅkayat tayaṉava ṉaṉiya,
tiṭamoḻi maṟiyōr nāṅkinaṉ ṉaṭuvuḷ
cempoṉcey kōyili ṉuḷḷē,
kaṭalniṟa vaṇṇaṉ ṟaṉṉinā ṉaṭiyēṉ
kaṇṭukoṇ ṭuyntoḻin tēṉē
[[((4.3.3))]]

$$1271

__&&1271__$$4.3.4##__

vaciyaṟu kuṟaḷāy māvali vēḷvi
maṇṇaḷa viṭṭavaṉ ṟaṉṉi,
acivaṟu mamara raṭiyiṇi vaṇaṅka
alikaṭal tuyiṉṟavam māṉi,
ticimuka ṉaṉiyōr nāṅkinaṉ ṉaṭuvuḷ
cempoṉcey kōyili ṉuḷḷē,
uyarmaṇi makuṭam cūṭiniṉ ṟāṉik
kaṇṭukoṇ ṭuyntoḻin tēṉē
[[((4.3.4))]]

$$1272

__&&1272__$$4.3.5##__

tīmaṉat tarakkar tiṟalaḻit tavaṉē.
eṉṟuceṉ ṟaṭintavar tamakku,
tāymaṉat tiraṅki yaruḷiṉik koṭukkum
tayarataṉ mataliyic cayamē,
tēmalarp poḻilcūḻ nāṅkinaṉ ṉaṭuvuḷ
cempoṉcey kōyili ṉuḷḷē,
kāmaṉip payantāṉ ṟaṉṉinā ṉaṭiyēṉ
kaṇṭukoṇ ṭuyntoḻin tēṉē
[[((4.3.5))]]

$$1273

__&&1273__$$4.3.6##__

mallimā munnī ratarpaṭa maliyāl
aṇiceytu makiḻntavaṉ ṟaṉṉi,
kalliṉmī tiyaṉṟa kaṭimati ḷilaṅki
kalaṅkavōr vāḷitoṭ ṭāṉi,
celvanāṉ maṟiyōr nāṅkinaṉ ṉaṭuvuḷ
cempoṉcey kōyili ṉuḷḷē,
allimā malarāḷ taṉṉoṭu maṭiyēṉ
kaṇṭukoṇ ṭallaltīrn tēṉē
[[((4.3.6))]]

$$1274

__&&1274__$$4.3.7##__

veñciṉak kaḷiṟum villoṭu mallum
vekuṇṭiṟut taṭarttavaṉ ṟaṉṉi,
kañcaṉik kāynta kāḷiyam māṉik
karumukil tiruniṟat tavaṉi,
ceñcolnāṉ maṟiyōr nāṅkinaṉ ṉaṭuvuḷ
cempoṉcey kōyili ṉuḷḷē,
añcaṉak kuṉṟam niṉṟatop pāṉik
kaṇṭukoṇ ṭallaltīrn tēṉē
[[((4.3.7))]]

$$1275

__&&1275__$$4.3.8##__

aṉṟiya vāṇa ṉāyiram tōḷum
tuṇiyavaṉ ṟāḻitoṭ ṭāṉi,
miṉtikaḻ kuṭumi vēṅkaṭa malimēl
mēviya vētanal viḷakki,
teṉticit tilatam aṉiyavar nāṅkic
cempoṉcey kōyili ṉuḷḷē,
maṉṟatu poliya makiḻntuniṉ ṟāṉi
vaṇaṅkināṉ vāḻntoḻin tēṉē
[[((4.3.8))]]

$$1276

__&&1276__$$4.3.9##__

kaḷaṅkaṉi vaṇṇā. kaṇṇaṇē. eṉṟaṉ
kārmuki lē eṉa niṉintiṭṭu,
uḷaṅkaṉin tirukku maṭiyavar taṅkaḷ
uḷḷattu ḷūṟiya tēṉi,
teḷintanāṉ maṟiyōr nāṅkinaṉṉaṭuvuḷ
cempoṉcey kōyili ṉuḷḷē,
vaḷaṅkoḷpē riṉpam maṉṉiniṉ ṟāṉi
vaṇaṅkināṉ vāḻntoḻin tēṉē
[[((4.3.9))]]

$$1277

__&&1277__$$4.3.10##__

tēṉamar cōli nāṅkinaṉ ṉaṭuvuḷ
cempoṉcey kōyili ṉuḷḷē,
vāṉavar kōṉik kaṇṭami collum
maṅkiyār vāṭkali kaṉṟi,
ūṉamil pāṭa loṉpatō ṭoṉṟum
oḻiviṉṟik kaṟṟuval lārkaḷ,
māṉaveṇ kuṭikkīḻ viyaka māṇṭu
vāṉava rākuvar makiḻntē
[[((4.3.10))]]

1278__((PTM_4.4))

$$1278

__&&1278__$$4.4.1##__

māṟṟaracar maṇimuṭiyum
tiṟalum tēcum
maṟṟavartam kātalimār
kuḻiyum, tanti
kāltaḷiyu muṭaṉkaḻala
vantu tōṉṟik
katanākam kāttaḷitta
kaṇṇar kaṇṭīr,
nūṟṟitaḻko ḷaravintam
nuḻinta paḷḷat
tiḷaṅkamukiṉ mutupāḷi
pakuvāy naṇṭiṉ,
cēṟṟaḷiyil veṇmuttam
cintu nāṅkūrt
tirutteṟṟi yampalatteṉ
ceṅkaṇ mālē
[[((4.4.1))]]

$$1279

__&&1279__$$4.4.2##__

poṟṟoṭittōḷ maṭamakaḷtaṉ
vaṭivu koṇṭa
pollāta vaṉpēycci
koṅki vāṅki,
peṟṟeṭutta tāypōla
maṭuppa ārum
pēṇānañ cuṇṭukanta
piḷḷi kaṇṭīr,
nelftoṭutta malarnīlam
niṟinta cūḻal
iruñciṟiya vaṇṭoliyum
neṭuṅka ṇārtam,
ciṟṟaṭimēl cilampoliyum
miḻaṟṟu nāṅkūrt
tirutteṟṟi yampalatteṉ
ceṅkaṇ mālē
[[((4.4.2))]]

$$1280

__&&1280__$$4.4.3##__

paṭalaṭinta ciṟukurampi
nuḻintu pukkup
pacuveṇṇey patamārap
paṇṇi muṟṟum,
aṭalaṭartta vēṟkaṇṇār
tōkki paṟṟi
alantalimi ceytuḻalu
miyaṉ kaṇṭīr,
maṭaleṭutta neṭuṉteṅkiṉ
paḻaṅkal vīḻa
māṅkaṉikaḷ tiraṭṭuruṭṭā
varunīrp poṉṉi,
tiṭaleṭuttu malarcumantaṅ
kiḻiyu nāṅkūrt
tirutteṟṟi yampalatteṉ
ceṅkaṇ mālē
[[((4.4.3))]]

$$1281

__&&1281__$$4.4.4##__

vārārum mulimaṭavāḷ
piṉṉik kāki
vaḷimaruppiṟf kaṭuñciṉattu
vaṉtā ḷārnta,
kārārtiṇ viṭiyaṭarttu
vatuvi yāṇṭa
karumukilpōl tiruniṟatteṉ
kaṇṇar kaṇṭīr,
ērārum malarppoḻilkaḷ
taḻuvi yeṅkum
eḻilmatiyik kāltoṭā
viḷaṅku cōti,
cīrāru maṇimāṭam
tikaḻum nāṅkūrt
tirutteṟṟi yampalatteṉ
ceṅkaṇ mālē
[[((4.4.4))]]

$$1282

__&&1282__$$4.4.5##__

kaliyilaṅku makalalkul
kamalap pāvi
kati rmutta veṇṇakiyāḷ
karuṅka ṇāycci,
muliyilaṅku moḷimaṇippūṇ
vaṭamum tēyppa
mūvāta varineṭuntōḷ
mūrtti kaṇṭīr,
maliyilaṅku niriccanti
māṭa vīti
āṭavari maṭamoḻiyār
mukattu iraṇṭu
cilivilaṅki maṉañciṟikoṇ
ṭirukkum nāṅkūrt
tirutteṟṟi yampalatteṉ
ceṅkaṇ mālē
[[((4.4.5))]]

$$1283

__&&1283__$$4.4.6##__

tāṉpōlu meṉṟeḻuntāṉ
taraṇi yāḷaṉ
atukaṇṭu tarittiruppā
ṉarakkar taṅkaḷ,
kōṉpōlu meṉṟeḻuntāṉ
kuṉṟa maṉṉa
irupatutō ḷuṭaṉtuṇitta
voruvaṉ kaṇṭīr,
māṉpōlu meṉṉōkkiṉ
ceyya vāyār
marakatam pōl maṭakkiḷiyik
kimēl koṇṭu,
tēṉpōlu meṉmaḻali
payiṟṟum nāṅkūrt
tirutteṟṟi yampalatteṉ
ceṅkaṇ mālē
[[((4.4.6))]]

$$1284

__&&1284__$$4.4.7##__

poṅkilaṅku purinūlum
tōlum tāḻap
pollāta kuṟaḷuruvāyp
poruntā vāṇaṉ
maṅkalamcēr maṟivēḷvi
yataṉuḷ pukku
maṇṇakalam kuṟiyiranta
mintaṉ kaṇṭīr,
koṅkalarnta malarkkuḻalār
koṅki tōynta
kuṅkumattiṉ kuḻampaḷinta
kōlan taṉṉāl,
ceṅkalaṅkal veṇmaṇalmēl
tavaḻum nāṅkūrt
tiruteṟṟi yampalatteṉ
ceṅkaṇ mālē
[[((4.4.7))]]

$$1285

__&&1285__$$4.4.8##__

cilampiṉiṭic ciṟuparalpōl
periya mēru
tirukkuḷampil kaṇakaṇappat
tiruvā kāram
kuluṅka, nila maṭantitaṉi
yiṭantu pulkik
kōṭṭiṭivit taruḷiyaveṅ
kōmāṉ kaṇṭīr,
ilaṅkiyanāṉ maṟiyaṉittu
maṅka māṟum
ēḻiciyum kēḷvikaḷu
meṇṭik keṅkum,
cilampiyanaṟ peruñcelvam
tikaḻum nāṅkūrt
tiruteṟṟi yampalatteṉ
ceṅkaṇ mālē
[[((4.4.8))]]

$$1286

__&&1286__$$4.4.9##__

ēḻulakum tāḻvariyu
meṅku mūṭi
eṇṭiciyu maṇṭalamum
maṇṭi, aṇṭam
mōḻiyeḻun tāḻimikum
ūḻi veḷḷam
muṉṉakaṭṭi loṭukkiyavem
mūrtti kaṇṭīr,
ūḻitoṟu mūḻitoṟu
muyarnta celvat
tōṅkiyanāṉ maṟiyaṉittum
tāṅku nāvar,
cēḻuyarnta maṇimāṭam
tikaḻum nāṅkūrt
tiruteṟṟi yampalatteṉ
ceṅkaṇ mālē
[[((4.4.9))]]

$$1287

__&&1287__$$4.4.10##__

cīraṇinta maṇimāṭam
tikaḻum nāṅkūrt
tiruteṟṟi yampalatteṉ
ceṅkaṇ māli,
kūraṇinta vēlvalavaṉ
āli nāṭaṉ
koṭimāṭa maṅkiyarkōṉ
kuṟiya lāḷi
pāraṇinta tolpukaḻāṉ
kaliyaṉ coṉṉa
pāmāli yiviyintu
mintum vallār,
cīraṇinta vulakattu
maṉṉa rākic
cēṇvicumpil vāṉavarāyt
tikaḻvar tāmē
[[((4.4.10))]]

1288__((PTM_4.5))

$$1288

__&&1288__$$4.5.1##__

tūmpuṭip paṉikki vēḻam
tuyarkeṭut taruḷi, maṉṉu
kāmpuṭik kuṉṟa mēntik
kaṭumaḻi kātta enti,
pūmpuṉal poṉṉi muṟṟum
pukuntupoṉ varaṇṭa, eṅkum
tēmpoḻil kamaḻum nāṅkūrt
tirumaṇik kūṭat tāṉē
[[((4.5.1))]]

$$1289

__&&1289__$$4.5.2##__

kavvivā ḷeyiṟṟu vaṉpēyk
katirmuli cuvittu,i laṅki
vavviya iṭumpi tīrak
kaṭuṅkaṇi turanta enti,
kovvivāy makaḷir koṅkik
kuṅkumam kaḻuvip pōnta,
teyvanīr kamaḻum nāṅkūrt
tirumaṇik kūṭat tāṉē
[[((4.5.2))]]

$$1290

__&&1290__$$4.5.3##__

māttoḻil maṭaṅkak ceṟṟu
maṟutiṟa naṭantu vaṉtāḷ
cēttoḻil citittup piṉṉi
cevvittōḷ puṇarnta enti,
nāttoḻil maṟival lārkaḷ
nayantaṟam payanta vaṇkit
tīttoḻil payilum nāṅkūrt
tirumaṇik kūṭat tāṉē
[[((4.5.3))]]

$$1291

__&&1291__$$4.5.4##__

tāṅkaruñ ciṉattu vaṉtāḷ
taṭakkimā maruppu vāṅki,
pūṅkurun tocittup puḷvāy
piḷanteru taṭartta enti,
māṅkaṉi nukarnta manti
vantuvaṇ ṭiriya vāḻit
tīṅkaṉi nukarum nāṅkūrt
tirumaṇik kūṭat tāṉē
[[((4.5.4))]]

$$1292

__&&1292__$$4.5.5##__

karumaka ḷilaṅki yāṭṭi
pilaṅkoḷvāy tiṟantu taṉmēl
varumavaḷ ceviyum mūkkum
vāḷiṉāl taṭinta enti,
perumakaḷ pēti maṅki
taṉṉoṭum pirivi lāta,
tirumakaḷ maruvum nāṅkūrt
tirumaṇik kūṭat taṉē
[[((4.5.5))]]

$$1293

__&&1293__$$4.5.6##__

keṇṭiyum kuṟaḷum puḷḷum
kēḻalu mariyum māvum,
aṇṭamum cuṭarum alla
āṟṟalu māya enti,
ōṇṭiṟal teṉṉa ṉōṭa
vaṭavara cōṭṭaṅ kaṇṭa,
tiṇṭiṟa lāḷar nāṅkūrt
tirumaṇik kūṭat taṉē
[[((4.5.6))]]

$$1294

__&&1294__$$4.5.7##__

kuṉṟamum vāṉum maṇṇum kuḷirpuṉal tiṅka ḷōṭu,
niṉṟaveñ cuṭarum allā nilikaḷu māya enti,
maṉṟamum vayalum kāvum māṭamum maṇaṅkoṇṭu, eṅkum
teṉṟalvan tulavum nāṅkūrt tirumaṇik kūṭat tāṉē
[[((4.5.7))]]

$$1295

__&&1295__$$4.5.8##__

caṅkiyum tuṇivum poyyum meyyum it taraṇi yōmpum,
poṅkiya mukilum allāp poruḷkaḷu māya venti,
paṅkaya mukutta tēṟal parukiya vāḷi pāya,
ceṅkaya lukaḷum nāṅkūrt tirumaṇik kūṭat tāṉē
[[((4.5.8))]]

$$1296

__&&1296__$$4.5.9##__

pāvamum aṟamum vīṭum iṉpamun tuṉpan tāṉum
kōvamum aruḷum allāk kuṇaṅkaḷu māya enti,
mūvari leṅkaḷ mūrtti ivaṉ, eṉa muṉivarōṭu,
tēvarvan tiṟiñcum nāṅkūrt tirumaṇik kūṭat taṉē
[[((4.5.9))]]

$$1297

__&&1297__$$4.5.10##__

tiṅkaḷtōy māṭa nāṅkūrt tirumaṇik kūṭat tāṉi
maṅkiyar talivaṉ vaṇtārkf kaliyaṉvā yolikaḷ vallār,
poṅkunī rulaka māṇṭu poṉṉula kāṇṭu, piṉṉum
veṅkatirp pariti vaṭṭat tūṭupōy viḷaṅku vārē
[[((4.5.10))]]

1298__((PTM_4.6))

$$1298

__&&1298__$$4.6.1##__

tāvaḷan tulaka muṟṟum taṭamalarp poyki pukku,
nāvaḷam naviṉṟiṅ kētta nākattiṉ naṭukkantīrttāy,
māvaḷam peruki maṉṉum maṟiyavar vāḻum nāṅkik,
kāvaḷam pāṭi mēya kaṇṇaṉē kaḷikaṇīyē
[[((4.6.1))]]

$$1299

__&&1299__$$4.6.2##__

maṇṇiṭan tēṉa māki māvali valito lippāṉ,
viṇṇavar vēṇṭac ceṉṟu vēḷviyil kuṟiyi rantāy
tuṇṇeṉa māṟṟār tammit tolittavar nāṅki mēya,
kaṇṇaṉē kāva ḷantaṇ pāṭiyāy kaḷika ṇīyē
[[((4.6.2))]]

$$1300

__&&1300__$$4.6.3##__

urutteḻu vāli mārvil ōrukaṇi yuruva vōṭṭi,
karuttuṭit tampik kiṉpak katimuṭi yaracaḷittāy,
parutteḻu palavum māvum paḻamviḻun toḻukum nāṅkik
karuttaṉē kāva ḷantaṇ pāṭiyāy. kaḷika ṇīyē
[[((4.6.3))]]

$$1301

__&&1301__$$4.6.4##__

muṉimakat tarakkaṉ māḷa muṭikaḷpat taṟuttu vīḻttu, āṅ
kaṉiyavaṟ kiḷiya vaṟkē aracaḷit taruḷi ṉāṉē,
cuṉikaḷil kayalkaḷ pāyac curumputēṉ nukarum nāṅkik,
kaṉikaḻal kāva ḷantaṇ pāṭiyāy. kaḷika ṇīyē
[[((4.6.4))]]

$$1302

__&&1302__$$4.6.5##__

paṭavara vucci taṉmēl pāyntupaṉṉaṭaṅkaḷ ceytu,
maṭavaral maṅki taṉṉi mārvakat tirutti ṉāṉē,
taṭavari taṅku māṭat takupukaḻ nāṅki mēya,
kaṭavuḷē kāva ḷantaṇ pāṭiyāy kaḷika ṇīyē
[[((4.6.5))]]

$$1303

__&&1303__$$4.6.6##__

mallari yaṭṭu māḷak kañcaṉi malintu koṉṟu,
pallara cavintu vīḻap pāratap pōrmu ṭittāy,
nallaraṇ kāviṉ nīḻal naṟikamaḻ nāṅki mēya,
kallaraṇ kāva ḷantaṇ pāṭiyāy kaḷika ṇīyē
[[((4.6.6))]]

$$1304

__&&1304__$$4.6.7##__

mūttavaṟ karacu vēṇṭi muṉpu tūteḻun taruḷi,
māttamar pākaṉ vīḻa matakari maruppo cittāy,
pūttamar cōli yōṅkip puṉalparan toḻukum, nāṅkik
kāttavaṉē kāva ḷantaṇ pāṭiyāy kaḷi kaṇīyē
[[((4.6.7))]]

$$1305

__&&1305__$$4.6.8##__

ēviḷaṅ kaṉṉik kāki imiyavar kōṉic ceṟṟu,
kāvaḷam kaṭiti ṟuttuk kaṟpakam koṇṭu pōntāy,
pūvaḷam poḻilkaḷ cūḻnta purantaraṉ ceyta nāṅkik,
kāvaḷam pāṭi mēya kaṇṇaṉē kaḷika ṇīyē
[[((4.6.8))]]

$$1306

__&&1306__$$4.6.9##__

cantamāy camaya mākic camayavim pūta māki,
antamā yāti yāki arumaṟi yaviyu māṉāy,
mantamār poḻilka ṭōṟum maṭamayi lālum nāṅki,
kantamār kāva ḷantaṇ pāṭiyāy kaḷika ṇīyē
[[((4.6.9))]]

$$1307

__&&1307__$$4.6.10##__

māvaḷam peruki maṉṉum maṟiyavar vāḻum, nāṅkik
kāvaḷam pāṭi mēya kaṇṇaṇik kaliyaṉ coṉṉa,
pāvaḷam pattum vallār pārmici yaraca rāki,
kōviḷa maṉṉar tāḻak kuṭiniḻal polivar tāmē
[[((4.6.10))]]

1308__((PTM_4.7))

$$1308

__&&1308__$$4.7.1##__

kaṇṇār kaṭalpōl tirumēṉi kariyāy,
naṇṇārmuṉi veṉṟikoḷ vārmaṉṉu nāṅkūr,
tiṇṇār matiḷcūḻ tiruveḷḷak kuḷattuḷ
aṇṇā, aṭiyē ṉiṭarik kaḷiyāyē
[[((4.7.1))]]

$$1309

__&&1309__$$4.7.2##__

kontār tuḷava malarkoṉ ṭaṇivāṉē,
nantā taperum pukaḻvē tiyarnāṅkūr,
centā marinīrt tiruveḷḷak kuḷattuḷ
entāy, aṭiyē ṉiṭarik kaḷiyāyē
[[((4.7.2))]]

$$1310

__&&1310__$$4.7.3##__

kuṉṟāl kuḷirmā ritaṭut tukantāṉē,
naṉṟā yaperum pukaḻvē tiyarnāṅkūr
ceṉṟār vaṇaṅkum tiruveḷḷak kuḷattuḷ
niṉṟāy, neṭiyāy aṭiyē ṉiṭarnīkkē
[[((4.7.3))]]

$$1311

__&&1311__$$4.7.4##__

kāṉār karikom patocitta kaḷiṟē,
nāṉā vakinal lavarmaṉ ṉiyanāṅkūr,
tēṉār poḻilcūḻ tiruveḷḷak kuḷattuḷ
āṉāy, aṭiyēṉuk karuḷpuri yāyē
[[((4.7.4))]]

$$1312

__&&1312__$$4.7.5##__

vēṭār tiruvēṅ kaṭammēya viḷakkē,
nāṭār pukaḻvē tiyarmaṉ ṉiyanāṅkūr,
cēṭār poḻilcūḻ tiruveḷḷakkuḷattāy,
pāṭā varuvēṉ viṇiyā yiṉapāṟṟē
[[((4.7.5))]]

$$1313

__&&1313__$$4.7.6##__
