tammāṉik kaṇṭāḷ kollō.
[[((8.1.6))]]

$$1654

__&&1654__$$8.1.7##__

cevvaratta vuṭiyāṭi yataṉmēlōr
civaḷikikkac ceṉkiṉ ṟāḷāl,
avvaratta vaṭiyiṇiyu maṅkikaḷum
paṅkayamē eṉkiṉ ṟāḷāl,
mivaḷarkkum maṇiyuruvam marakatamō
maḻimukilō eṉkiṉ ṟāḷāl,
kivaḷarkku maḻalāḷar kaṇṇapurat
tammāṉik kaṇṭāḷ kollō.
[[((8.1.7))]]

$$1655

__&&1655__$$8.1.8##__

koṟṟappuḷ ḷoṉṟēṟi maṉṉūṭē
varukiṉṟāṉ eṉkiṉ ṟāḷāl,
veṟṟippō rintiraṟku mintiraṉē
okkumāl eṉkiṉ ṟāḷāl,
peṟṟakkā lavaṉākam peṇpiṟantōm
uyyōmō eṉkiṉ ṟāḷāl,
kaṟṟanūl maṟiyāḷar kaṇṇapurat
tammāṉik kaṇṭāḷ kollō.
[[((8.1.8))]]

$$1656

__&&1656__$$8.1.9##__

vaṇṭamarum vaṉamāli maṇimuṭimēl
maṇanāṟum eṉkiṉ ṟāḷāl,
uṇṭivarpā laṉpeṉakkeṉ ṟorukālum
pirikilēṉ eṉkiṉ ṟāḷāl,
paṇṭivarik kaṇṭaṟiva tevvūril
yām eṉṟē payilkiṉ ṟāḷāl,
kaṇṭavartam maṉamvaḻaṅkum kaṇṇapurat
tammāṉik kaṇṭāḷ kollō.
[[((8.1.9))]]

$$1657

__&&1657__$$8.1.10##__

māvaḷaru meṉṉōkki mātarāḷ
māyavaṉik kaṇṭāḷ eṉṟu,
kāvaḷarum kaṭipoḻilcūḻ kaṇṇapurat
tammāṉik kaliyaṉ coṉṉa,
pāvaḷarum tamiḻmāli paṉṉiyanūl
iviyintu mintum vallār,
pūvaḷarum kaṟpakamcēr poṉṉulakil
maṉṉavarāyp pukaḻtak kōrē. (2)
[[((8.1.10))]]

1658__((PTM_8.2))

$$1658

__&&1658__$$8.2.1##__

teḷḷiyīr. tēvarkkum tēvar tiruttakkīr
veḷḷiyīr veyya viḻuniti vaṇṇar,ō
tuḷḷunīrk kaṇṇa puramtoḻu tāḷivaḷ
kaḷviyō, kivaḷi koḷvatu takkatē? (2)
[[((8.2.1))]]

$$1659

__&&1659__$$8.2.2##__

nīṇilā muṟṟattu niṉṟivaḷ nōkkiṉāḷ,
kāṇumō kaṇṇa purameṉṟu kāṭṭiṉāḷ,
pāṇaṉār tiṇṇa mirukka iṉiyivaḷ
nāṇumō, naṉṟunaṉ ṟunaṟi yūrarkkē.
[[((8.2.2))]]

$$1660

__&&1660__$$8.2.3##__

aruvicōr vēṅkaṭam nīrmali eṉṟuvāy
veruviṉāḷ meyyam viṉavi yirukkiṉṟāḷ,
perukucīrk kaṇṇa puram eṉṟu pēciṉāḷ
urukiṉāḷ, uḷmelin tāḷ itu veṉkolō. (2)
[[((8.2.3))]]

$$1661

__&&1661__$$8.2.4##__

uṇṇumnā ḷilli uṟakkamun tāṉilli,
peṇmiyum cāla niṟintilaḷ pētitāṉ,
kaṇṇaṉūr kaṇṇa puramtoḻum kārkkaṭal
vaṇṇarmēl, eṇṇa mivaṭkitu veṉkolō.
[[((8.2.4))]]

$$1662

__&&1662__$$8.2.5##__

kaṇṇaṉūr kaṇṇa puramtoḻum kāriki,
peṇmiyum taṉṉuṭi yuṇmi yurikkiṉṟāḷ,
veṇṇeyuṇ ṭāppuṇṭa vaṇṇam viḷampiṉāḷ,
vaṇṇamum poṉṉiṟa māva toḻiyumē.
[[((8.2.5))]]

$$1663

__&&1663__$$8.2.6##__

vaṭavari niṉṟumvantu iṉṟu kaṇapuram,
iṭavaki koḷvatu yām eṉṟu pēciṉāḷ,
maṭavaral mātareṉ pēti yivarkkivaḷ
kaṭavateṉ, kaṇṭuyi liṉṟivar koḷḷavē.
[[((8.2.6))]]

$$1664

__&&1664__$$8.2.7##__

taraṅkanīr pēciṉum taṇmati kāyiṉum,
iraṅkumō ettaṉi nāḷirun teḷkiṉāḷ
turaṅkamvāy kīṇṭukan tāṉatu toṉmi ūr
araṅkamē eṉpa tivaḷtaṉak kāciyē.
[[((8.2.7))]]

$$1665

__&&1665__$$8.2.8##__

toṇṭellām niṉṉaṭi yētoḻu tuyyumā
kaṇṭu,tāṉ kaṇapuram kitoḻap pōyiṉāḷ
vaṇṭulām kōtiyeṉ pēti maṇiniṟam
koṇṭutāṉ, kōyiṉmi ceyvatu takkatē?
[[((8.2.8))]]

$$1666

__&&1666__$$8.2.9##__

muḷḷeyi ṟēyntila, kūḻi muṭikoṭā,
teḷḷiya ḷeṉpatōr tēcilaḷ eṉceykēṉ,
kaḷḷaviḻ cōlik kaṇapuram kitoḻum
piḷḷiyi, piḷḷiyeṉ ṟeṇṇap peṟuvarē?
[[((8.2.9))]]

$$1667

__&&1667__$$8.2.10##__

kārmali kaṇṇa purattem aṭikaḷi,
pārmali maṅkiyar kōṉpara kālaṉcol,
cīrmali pāṭa livipattum vallavar,
nīrmali viyattu nīṭuniṟ pārkaḷē. (2)
[[((8.2.10))]]

1668__((PTM_8.3))

$$1668

__&&1668__$$8.3.1##__

kariyeṭutta curicaṅkum kaṉapavaḷat teḻukoṭiyum,
tiriyeṭuttu varupuṉalcūḻ tirukkaṇṇa purattuṟiyum,
viriyeṭutta tuḻāyalaṅkal viṟalvarittōḷ puṭipeyara
variyeṭutta perumāṉuk kiḻantēṉeṉ varivaḷiyē. (2)
[[((8.3.1))]]

$$1669

__&&1669__$$8.3.2##__

ariviravu mukiṟfkaṇattā ṉakilpukiyāl variyōṭum
terivariya maṇimāṭat tirukkaṇṇa purattuṟiyum,
variyaravi ṉaṇittuyiṉṟu maḻimatatta ciṟutaṟukaṇ,
kariveruva maruppocittāṟ kiḻantēṉeṉ kaṉavaḷiyē.
[[((8.3.2))]]

$$1670

__&&1670__$$8.3.3##__

tuṅkamā maṇimāṭa neṭumukaṭṭiṉ cūlikipōm
tiṅkaḷmā mukiltuṇikkum tirukkaṇṇa purattuṟiyum
piṅkaṇmāl viṭiyaṭarttup paṉimatikōḷ viṭuttukanta
ceṅkaṇmā lammāṉuk kiḻantēṉeṉ ceṟivaḷiyē.
[[((8.3.3))]]

$$1671

__&&1671__$$8.3.4##__

kaṇamaruvu mayilakavu kaṭipoḻilcūḻ neṭumaṟukil,
tiṇamaruvu kaṉamatiḷcūḻ tirukkaṇṇa purattuṟiyum,
maṇamaruvu tōḷāycci yārkkappōy uralōṭum
puṇarmaruta miṟanaṭantāṟ kiḻantēṉeṉ poṉvaḷiyē.
[[((8.3.4))]]

$$1672

__&&1672__$$8.3.5##__

vāyeṭutta mantirattā lantaṇartam ceytoḻilkaḷ
tīyeṭuttu maṟivaḷarkkum tirukkaṇṇa purattuṟiyum
tāyeṭutta ciṟukōluk kuḷintōṭit tayiruṇṭa,
vāytuṭitta mintaṉuk kiḻantēṉeṉ varivaḷiyē.
[[((8.3.5))]]

$$1673

__&&1673__$$8.3.6##__

maṭaleṭutta neṭuntāḻi maruṅkellām vaḷarpavaḷam,
tiṭaleṭuttuc cuṭarimikkum tirukkaṇṇa purattuṟiyum,
aṭalaṭarttaṉ ṟiraṇiyaṉi muraṇaḻiya aṇiyukirāl,
uṭaleṭutta perumāṉuk kiḻantēṉeṉ oḷivaḷiyē.
[[((8.3.6))]]

$$1674

__&&1674__$$8.3.7##__

vaṇṭamarum malarppuṉṉi varinīḻa laṇimuttam,
teṇṭirikaḷ varattiraṭṭum tirukkaṇṇa purattuṟiyum,
eṇṭiciyu meḻucuṭaru mirunilaṉum peruvicumpum,
uṇṭumiḻnta perumāṉuk kiḻantēṉeṉ oḷivaḷiyē.
[[((8.3.7))]]

$$1675

__&&1675__$$8.3.8##__

koṅkumali karuṅkuvaḷi kaṇṇāka teṇkayaṅkaḷ
ceṅkamala mukamalarttum tirukkaṇṇa purattuṟiyum,
vaṅkamali taṭaṅkaṭaluḷ variyaravi ṉaṇittuyiṉṟā,
ceṅkamala nāpaṉuk kiḻantēṉeṉ ceṟivaḷiyē.
[[((8.3.8))]]

$$1676

__&&1676__$$8.3.9##__

vārāḷu miḷaṅkoṅki neṭumpaṇittōḷ maṭappāvi,
cīrāḷum varimārvaṉ tirukkaṇṇa purattuṟiyum,
pērāḷa ṉāyirampē rāyiravā yaravaṇimēl
pērāḷar perumāṉuk kiḻantēṉeṉ peyvaḷiyē.
[[((8.3.9))]]

$$1677

__&&1677__$$8.3.10##__

tēmaruvu poḻilpuṭicūḻ tirukkaṇṇa purattuṟiyum
vāmaṉaṉi, maṟikaṭalcūḻ vayalāli vaḷanāṭaṉ,
kāmarucīrk kalikaṉṟi kaṇṭuritta tamiḻmāli,
nāmaruvi yivipāṭa viṉiyāya naṇṇāvē. (2)
[[((8.3.10))]]

1678__((PTM_8.4))

$$1678

__&&1678__$$8.4.1##__

viṇṇavar taṅkaḷ perumāṉ tirumārvaṉ,
maṇṇava rellām vaṇaṅkum malipukaḻcēr,
kaṇṇa purattem perumāṉ katirmuṭimēl,
vaṇṇa naṟuntuḻāy vantūtāy kōltumpī. (2)
[[((8.4.1))]]

$$1679

__&&1679__$$8.4.2##__

vēta mutalvaṉ viḷaṅku purinūlaṉ,
pātam paravip palarum paṇintētti,
kātaṉmi ceyyum kaṇṇapurat temperumāṉ,
tātu naṟuntuḻāy tāḻntūtāy kōltumpī.
[[((8.4.2))]]

$$1680

__&&1680__$$8.4.3##__

viṇṭamala rellā mūtinī yeṉpeṟuti,
aṇṭa mutalva ṉamararka ḷellārum,
kaṇṭu vaṇaṅkum kaṇṇapurat temperumāṉ
vaṇṭu naṟuntuḻāy vantūtāy kōltumpī.
[[((8.4.3))]]

$$1681

__&&1681__$$8.4.4##__

nīrmali kiṉṟatōr mīṉāyō rāmiyumāy,
cīrmali kiṉṟatōr ciṅka vuruvāki,
kārmali vaṇṇaṉ kaṇṇapurat temperumāṉ,
tārmali taṇṭuḻāy tāḻntūtāy kōltumpī.
[[((8.4.4))]]

$$1682

__&&1682__$$8.4.5##__

ērār malarellā mūtinī yeṉpeṟuti,
pārā rulakam paravap peruṅkaṭaluḷ,
kārāmi yāṉa kaṇṇapurat temperumāṉ,
tārār naṟuntuḻāy tāḻntūtāy kōltumpī.
[[((8.4.5))]]

$$1683

__&&1683__$$8.4.6##__

mārvil tiruvaṉ valaṉēntu cakkarattaṉ,
pārip piḷanta paramaṉ parañcōti,
kāril tikaḻkāyā vaṇṇaṉ katirmuṭimēl,
tāril naṟuntuḻāy tāḻntūtāy kōltumpī.
[[((8.4.6))]]

$$1684

__&&1684__$$8.4.7##__

vāmaṉaṉ kaṟki matucūtaṉ mātavaṉ
tārmaṉṉu tāca ratiyāya taṭamārvaṉ,
kāmaṉṟaṉ tāti kaṇṇapurat temperumāṉ,
tāma naṟuntuḻāy tāḻntūtāy kōltumpī.
[[((8.4.7))]]

$$1685

__&&1685__$$8.4.8##__

nīla malarkaḷ neṭunīr vayalmaruṅkil,
cāla malarellā mūtātē, vāḷarakkar
kālaṉ kaṇṇapurat temperumāṉ katirmuṭimēl,
kōla naṟuntuḻāy koṇṭūtāy kōltumpī.
[[((8.4.8))]]

$$1686

__&&1686__$$8.4.9##__

nantaṉ matali nilamaṅki naltuṇivaṉ,
anta mutalvaṉ amararkaḷ tamperumāṉ,
kantam kamaḻkāyā vaṇṇaṉ katirmuṭimēl,
kontu naṟuntuḻāy koṇṭūtāy kōltumpī.
[[((8.4.9))]]

$$1687

__&&1687__$$8.4.10##__

vaṇṭamaruñ cōli vayalāli naṉṉāṭaṉ,
kaṇṭacīr veṉṟik kaliya ṉolimāli,
koṇṭal niṟavaṇṇaṉ kaṇṇa purattāṉi,
toṇṭarōm pāṭa niṉintūtāy kōltumpī. (2)
[[((8.4.10))]]

1688__((PTM_8.5))

$$1688

__&&1688__$$8.5.1##__

tanti kālil vilaṅkaṟavantu
tōṉṟiya tōṉṟalpiṉ, tamiyēṉṟaṉ
cinti pōyiṟṟut tiruvaruḷ
avaṉiṭip peṟumaḷa viruntēṉi,
anti kāvalaṉamutuṟu
pacuṅkati ravicuṭa ataṉōṭum,
manta mārutam vaṉamuli
taṭavantu valiceyva toḻiyātē. (2)
[[((8.5.1))]]

$$1689

__&&1689__$$8.5.2##__

māri mākkaṭal vaḷivaṇaṟ
kiḷiyavaṉ varipuri tirumārpil,
tāri ṉāciyil pōyiṉa
neñcamum tāḻntatōr tuṇikāṇēṉ,
ūrum tuñciṟṟulakamum
tuyiṉṟatu oḷiyavaṉ vicumpiyaṅkum,
tērum pōyiṟṟut ticikaḷum
maṟintaṉa ceyvatoṉ ṟaṟiyēṉē.
[[((8.5.2))]]

$$1690

__&&1690__$$8.5.3##__

āyaṉ māyamē yaṉṟimaṟ
ṟeṉkiyil vaḷikaḷum iṟinillā,
pēyiṉ āruyi ruṇṭiṭum
piḷḷinam peṇṇuyirk kiraṅkumō,
tūya māmatik katirccuṭat
tuṇiyilli iṇimuli vēkiṉṟatāl,
āyaṉ vēyiṉuk kaḻikiṉṟa
tuḷḷamum añcēleṉ pāriliyē.
[[((8.5.3))]]

$$1691

__&&1691__$$8.5.4##__

kayaṅkoḷ puṇtalik kaḷiṟuntu
ventiṟal kaḻalmaṉṉar perumpōril,
mayaṅakaveṇcaṅkam vāyvitta
mintaṉum vantilaṉ, maṟikaṭalnīr
tayaṅku veṇtirit tivalinuṇ
paṉiyeṉṉum taḻalmukan tiḷamulimēl,
iyaṅku mārutam vilaṅkileṉ
āviyi eṉakkeṉap peṟalāmē.
[[((8.5.4))]]

$$1692

__&&1692__$$8.5.5##__

ēḻu māmaram tuḷipaṭac
cilivaḷit tilaṅkiyi malaṅkuvitta
āḻi yāṉ,namak karuḷiya
aruḷoṭum pakalelli kaḻikiṉṟatāl,
tōḻi. nāmitaṟ keṉceytum
tuṇiyilli cuṭarpaṭu mutunīril,
āḻa āḻkiṉṟa āviyi
aṭuvatōr antivan taṭikiṉṟatē.
[[((8.5.5))]]

$$1693

__&&1693__$$8.5.6##__

muriyum veṇṭiri mutukayam
tīppaṭa muḻaṅkaḻa leriyampiṉ,
varikoḷ veñcili vaḷivitta
mintaṉum vantilaṉ eṉceykēṉ,
eriyum veṅkatir tuyiṉṟatu
pāviyēṉ iṇineṭuṅ kaṇtuyilā,
kariya nāḻiki ūḻiyil
periyaṉa kaḻiyumā ṟaṟiyēṉē.
[[((8.5.6))]]

$$1694

__&&1694__$$8.5.7##__

kalaṅka mākkaṭal kaṭintaṭit
tilaṅkiyar kōṉatu variyākam,
malaṅka veñcamat taṭucaram
turantavem maṭikaḷum vārāṉāl,
ilaṅku veṅkati riḷamati
yataṉoṭum viṭimaṇi yaṭum,āyaṉ
vilaṅkal vēyiṉa tōciyu
māyiṉi viḷivatoṉ ṟaṟiyēṉē.
[[((8.5.7))]]

$$1695

__&&1695__$$8.5.8##__

muḻutiv viyakam muṟikeṭa
maṟitalum muṉivaṉum muṉiveyti,
maḻuvi ṉālmaṉṉar āruyir
vavviya mintaṉum vārāṉāl,
oḻuku nuṇpaṉik koṭuṅkiya
pēṭiyi yaṭaṅkavañ ciṟikōli,
taḻuvu naḷḷiruḷ taṉimiyiṟ
kaṭiyatōr koṭuviṉi yaṟiyēṉē.
[[((8.5.8))]]

$$1696

__&&1696__$$8.5.9##__

kaṉañcey māmatiḷ kaṇapurat
tavaṉoṭum kaṉaviṉi lavaṉtanta,
maṉañce yiṉpamvan tuḷpuka
veḷkiyeṉ vaḷineka iruntēṉi,
ciṉañcey mālviṭic ciṟumaṇi
ōciyeṉ cintiyic cintuvikkum,
aṉanta laṉṟiliṉ arikural
pāviyē ṉāviyi yaṭukiṉṟatē.
[[((8.5.9))]]

$$1697

__&&1697__$$8.5.10##__

vārkoḷ meṉmuli maṭantiyar
taṭaṅkaṭal vaṇṇaṉit tāḷnayantu,
ārvat tālavar pulampiya
pulampali aṟintumuṉ uriceyta,
kārkoḷ pimpoḻil maṅkiyar
kāvalaṉ kalikaṉṟi yolivallār,
ērkoḷ vikunta mānakar
pukkimi yavaroṭum kūṭuvarē. (2)
[[((8.5.10))]]

1698__((PTM_8.6))

$$1698

__&&1698__$$8.6.1##__

toṇṭīr. uyyum vakikaṇṭēṉ
tuḷaṅkā arakkar tuḷaṅka muṉ
tiṇṭōḷ nimirac cilivaḷiyac
ciṟitē muṉinta tirumārpaṉ,
vaṇṭār kūntal malarmaṅki
vaṭikkaṇ maṭanti mānōkkam
kaṇṭāḷ, kaṇṭu koṇṭukanta
kaṇṇa puramnām toḻutumē. (2)
[[((8.6.1))]]

$$1699

__&&1699__$$8.6.2##__

poruntā arakkar veñcamattup
poṉṟa aṉṟu puḷḷūrntu,
peruntōḷ māli talipuraḷap
pērnta arakkar teṉṉilaṅki,
iruntār tammi yuṭaṉkoṇṭaṅ
keḻilār pilattup pukkoḷippa,
karuntāḷ cilikik koṇṭāṉūr
kaṇṇa puramnām toḻutumē.
[[((8.6.2))]]

$$1700

__&&1700__$$8.6.3##__

valli yiṭiyāḷ poruṭṭāka
matiḷnī rilaṅki yārkōvi,
allal ceytu veñcamattuḷ
āṟṟal mikunta āṟṟalāṉ,
vallāḷ arakkar kulappāvi
vāṭa muṉitaṉ vēḷviyi,
kalvic ciliyāl kāttāṉūr
kaṇṇa puramnām toḻutumē.
[[((8.6.3))]]

$$1701

__&&1701__$$8.6.4##__

malli munnī ratarpaṭa
variveñ cilikāl vaḷivittu,
kolli vilaṅku paṇiceyyak
koṭiyōṉ ilaṅki pukaluṟṟu,
tolli maraṅkaḷ pukappeytu
tuvali nimirntu vāṉaṇava,
kallāl kaṭali yaṭittāṉūr
kaṇṇa puramnām toḻutumē.
[[((8.6.4))]]

$$1702

__&&1702__$$8.6.5##__

āmi yāki ariyāki
aṉṉa māki antaṇartam
ōma māki ūḻiyāy
ulaku cūḻnta neṭumpuṇari
cēma matiḷcūḻilaṅkikkōṉ
ciramuṅkaramum tuṇittu muṉ
kāmaṟ payantāṉ karutumūr
kaṇṇa puramnām toḻutumē.
[[((8.6.5))]]

$$1703

__&&1703__$$8.6.6##__

varuntā tirunī maṭaneñcē
nammēl viṉikaḷ vārā muṉ
tiruntā arakkar teṉṉilaṅki
centī yuṇṇac civantorunāḷ,
peruntōḷ vāṇaṟ karuḷpurintu
piṉṉi maṇāḷa ṉāki muṉ
karuntāḷ kaḷiṟoṉ ṟocittāṉūr
kaṇṇa puramnām toḻutumē.
[[((8.6.6))]]

$$1704

__&&1704__$$8.6.7##__

iliyār malarppūm poykivāy
mutali taṉṉāl aṭarppuṇṭu,
koliyār vēḻam naṭukkuṟṟuk
kuliya ataṉuk karuḷpurintāṉ,
alinī rilaṅkit tacakkirīvaṟku
iḷiyōṟ karaci yaruḷi,muṉ
kalimāc ciliyāl eytāṉūr
kaṇṇa puramnām toḻutumē.
[[((8.6.7))]]

$$1705

__&&1705__$$8.6.8##__

mālāy maṉamēyaruntuyaril
varuntā tirunī valimikka
kālār marutum kāyciṉatta
kaḻutum katamāk kaḻutiyum,
mālār viṭiyum matakariyum
mallar uyirum maṭivittu,
kālāl cakaṭam pāyntāṉūr
kaṇṇa puramnām toḻutumē.
[[((8.6.8))]]

$$1706

__&&1706__$$8.6.9##__

kuṉṟāl māri paḻutākkik
koṭiyē riṭiyāḷ poruṭṭāka,
vaṉṟāḷ viṭiyē ḻaṉṟaṭartta
vāṉōr perumāṉ māmāyaṉ,
ceṉṟāṉ tūtu pañcavarkkāyt
tirikāṟf cakaṭam ciṉamaḻittu,
kaṉṟāl viḷaṅkā yeṟintāṉūr
kaṇṇa puramnām toḻutumē.
[[((8.6.9))]]

$$1707

__&&1707__$$8.6.10##__

karumā mukiltōy neṭumāṭak
kaṇṇa purattem aṭikaḷi,
tirumā makaḷā laruḷmāri
ceḻunī rāli vaḷanāṭaṉ,
maruvār puyalkik kalikaṉṟi
maṅki vēnta ṉolivallār
irumā nilattuk karacāki
imiyōr iṟiñca vāḻvārē. (2)
[[((8.6.10))]]

1708__((PTM_8.7))

$$1708

__&&1708__$$8.7.1##__

viyamuṭi viṭiyiṉam uṭitara maṭamakaḷ,
kuyamiṭi taṭavari yakalama tuṭiyavar,
nayamuṭi naṭiyaṉam iḷiyavar naṭipayil,
kayamiṭi kaṇapuram aṭikaḷtamiṭamē. (2)
[[((8.7.1))]]

$$1709

__&&1709__$$8.7.2##__

iṇimali marutiṉo ṭerutiṟa ikalceytu
tuṇimali muliyavaḷ maṇamiku kalaviyuḷ,
maṇamali viḻaviṉo ṭaṭiyavar aḷaviya,
kaṇamali kaṇapuram aṭikaḷtam iṭamē.
[[((8.7.2))]]

$$1710

__&&1710__$$8.7.3##__

puyaluṟu varimaḻi poḻitara maṇiniri,
mayaluṟa varikuṭi yeṭuviya neṭiyavar,
muyaltuḷar miḷimuyal tuḷavaḷa viḷivayal,
kayaltuḷu kaṇapuram aṭikaḷtam iṭamē.
[[((8.7.3))]]

$$1711

__&&1711__$$8.7.4##__

ētalar nakiceya iḷiyavar aḷiveṇey
pōtucey tamariya puṉitarnal virimalar
kōtiya matukaram kulaviya malarmakaḷ
kātalcey kaṇapuram aṭikaḷtam iṭamē.
[[((8.7.4))]]

$$1712

__&&1712__$$8.7.5##__

toṇṭarum amararum muṉivarum toḻuteḻa
aṇṭamo ṭakaliṭam aḷantavar amarcceytu
viṇṭavar paṭamati ḷilaṅkimuṉ ṉeriyeḻa
kaṇṭavar kaṇapuram aṭikaḷtam iṭamē.
[[((8.7.5))]]

$$1713

__&&1713__$$8.7.6##__

maḻuviyal paṭiyuṭi yavaṉiṭam maḻimukil,
taḻuviya uruviṉar tirumakaḷ maruviya
koḻuviya ceḻumalar muḻuciya paṟavipaṇ
eḻuviya kaṇapuram aṭikaḷtam iṭamē.
[[((8.7.6))]]

$$1714

__&&1714__$$8.7.7##__

paritiyo ṭaṇimati paṉivari ticinilam
eritiyo ṭeṉaviṉa iyalviṉar celaviṉar
curutiyo ṭarumaṟi muṟicolu maṭiyavar
karutiya kaṇapuram aṭikaḷtam iṭamē.
[[((8.7.7))]]

$$1715

__&&1715__$$8.7.8##__

paṭipulku maṭiyiṇi palartoḻa malarviku
koṭipulku taṭavari akalama tuṭiyavar
muṭipulku neṭuvayal paṭicela aṭimalar
kaṭipulku kaṇapuram aṭikaḷtam iṭamē.
[[((8.7.8))]]

$$1716

__&&1716__$$8.7.9##__

pulamaṉu malarmici malarmakaḷ puṇariya
nilamaka ḷeṉaviṉa makaḷirka ḷivaroṭum
valamaṉu paṭiyuṭi maṇivaṇar nitikuvi
kalamaṉu kaṇapuram aṭikaḷtam iṭamē.
[[((8.7.9))]]

$$1717

__&&1717__$$8.7.10##__

malipukaḻ kaṇapura muṭiyavem aṭikaḷi
valikeḻu matiḷayal vayalaṇi maṅkiyar
kaliyaṉa tamiḻivi viḻumiya iciyiṉoṭu
olicolum aṭiyavar uṟutuya rilarē. (2)
[[((8.7.10))]]

1718__((PTM_8.8))

$$1718

__&&1718__$$8.8.1##__

vāṉō raḷavum mutumunnīr
vaḷarnta kālam, valiyuruvil
mīṉāy vantu viyantuyyak
koṇṭa taṇṭā marikkaṇṇaṉ,
āṉā vuruvi lāṉāyaṉ
avaṉi yammā viḷivayaluḷ,
kāṉār puṟavil kaṇṇapurat
taṭiyēṉ kaṇṭu koṇṭēṉē. (2)
[[((8.8.1))]]

$$1719

__&&1719__$$8.8.2##__

malaṅku vilaṅku neṭuveḷḷam
maṟuka aṅkōr varinaṭṭu
ilaṅku cōti yāramutam
eytu maḷavōr āmiyāy,
vilaṅkal tiriyat taṭaṅkaṭaluḷ
cumantu kiṭanta vittakaṉi,
kalaṅkal munnīrk kaṇṇapurat
taṭiyēṉ kaṇṭu koṇṭēṉē.
[[((8.8.2))]]

$$1720

__&&1720__$$8.8.3##__

pārār aḷavum mutumunnīr
paranta kālam, vaḷimaruppil
ērār uruvat tēṉamāy
eṭutta āṟṟa lammāṉi,
kūrār āral irikarutik
kuruku pāyak kayaliriyum,
kārār puṟavil kaṇṇapurat
taṭiyēṉ kaṇṭu koṇṭēṉē.
[[((8.8.3))]]

$$1721

__&&1721__$$8.8.4##__

uḷinta ariyum māṉiṭamum
uṭaṉāyt tōṉṟa oṉṟuvittu,
viḷinta cīṟṟam viṇvetumpa
vēṟṟōṉ akalam veñcamattu,
piḷantu vaḷinta vukirāṉip
peruntaṇ cenneṟ kulitaṭintu,
kaḷañcey puṟavil kaṇṇapurat
taṭiyēṉ kaṇṭu koṇṭēṉē.
[[((8.8.4))]]

$$1722

__&&1722__$$8.8.5##__

toḻunīr vaṭivil kuṟaḷuruvāy
vantu tōṉṟi māvalipāl,
muḻunīr viyam muṉkoṇṭa
mūvā vuruvi ṉammāṉi
uḻunīr vayaluḷ poṉkiḷippa
orupāl mulli mukiyōṭum
kaḻunīr malarum kaṇṇapurat
taṭiyēṉ kaṇṭu koṇṭēṉē.
[[((8.8.5))]]

$$1723

__&&1723__$$8.8.6##__

vaṭivāy maḻuvē paṭiyāka
vantu tōṉṟi mūveḻukāl,
paṭiyār aracu kaḷikaṭṭa
pāḻi yāṉi yammāṉi,
kuṭiyā vaṇṭu koṇṭuṇṇak
kōla nīlam maṭṭukukkum,
kaṭiyār puṟavil kaṇṇapurat
taṭiyēṉ kaṇṭu koṇṭēṉē.
[[((8.8.6))]]

$$1724

__&&1724__$$8.8.7##__

viya mellā muṭaṉvaṇaṅka
vaṇaṅkā maṉṉa ṉāyttōṉṟi,
veyya cīṟṟak kaṭiyilaṅki
kuṭikoṇ ṭōṭa veñcamattu,
ceyta vempōr namparaṉic
ceḻuntaṇ kāṉal maṇanāṟum,
kiti vēlik kaṇṇapurat
taṭiyēṉ kaṇṭu koṇṭēṉē.
[[((8.8.7))]]

$$1725

__&&1725__$$8.8.8##__

oṟṟik kuḻiyum nāñcilum
orupāl tōṉṟat tāṉtōṉṟi,
veṟṟit toḻilār vēlvēntar
viṇpāṟf cella veñcamattu,
ceṟṟa koṟṟat toḻilāṉic
centī mūṉṟum milliruppa,
kaṟṟa maṟiyōr kaṇṇapurat
taṭiyēṉ kaṇṭu koṇṭēṉē.
[[((8.8.8))]]

$$1726

__&&1726__$$8.8.9##__

tuvarik kaṉivāy nilamaṅki
tuyartīrn tuyyap pāratattuḷ,
ivarit taracar taṭumāṟa
iruḷnāḷ piṟanta ammāṉi,
uvari yōtam muttunta
orupā lorupā loṇcennel,
kavari vīcum kaṇṇapurat
taṭiyēṉ kaṇṭu koṇṭēṉē.
[[((8.8.9))]]

$$1727

__&&1727__$$8.8.10##__

mīṉō ṭāmi kēḻalari
kuṟaḷāy muṉṉu mirāmaṉāyt
tāṉāy piṉṉu mirāmaṉāyt
tāmō taraṉāyk kaṟkiyum
āṉāṉ ṟaṉṉi kaṇṇapurat
taṭiyēṉ kaliya ṉoliceyta
tēṉā riṉcol tamiḻmāli
ceppap pāvam nillāvē. (2)
[[((8.8.10))]]

1728__((PTM_8.9))

$$1728

__&&1728__$$8.9.1##__

kimmāṉa matayāṉi yiṭartīrtta karumukili
mimmāṉa maṇiyi aṇikoḷ marakatatti,
emmāṉi yempirāṉi yīcaṉi yeṉmaṉattuḷ
ammāṉi, aṭiyē ṉaṭintuyntu pōṉēṉē. (2)
[[((8.9.1))]]

$$1729

__&&1729__$$8.9.2##__

tarumāṉa maḻimukilip piriyātu taṉṉaṭintār,
varumāṉam tavirkkum maṇiyiyaṇiyuruvil,
tirumāli yammāṉi amutattik kaṭaṟkiṭanta
perumāṉi aṭiyē ṉaṭintuyntu piḻittēṉē.
[[((8.9.2))]]

$$1730

__&&1730__$$8.9.3##__

viṭiyēḻaṉ ṟaṭarttu vekuṇṭu vilaṅkaluṟa
paṭiyālāḻi taṭṭa paramaṉ parañcōti,
maṭiyār nīlammalkum vayalcūḻ kaṇṇapuramoṉ
ṟuṭiyāṉukku aṭiyēṉ oruvark kuriyēṉō? (2)
[[((8.9.3))]]

$$1731

__&&1731__$$8.9.4##__

mikkāṉi maṟiyāy virinta viḷakki eṉṉuḷ
pukkāṉip pukaḻcēr polikiṉṟa poṉmaliyi
takkāṉik kaṭikit taṭaṅkuṉṟiṉ miciyirunta
akkārak kaṉiyi aṭintuyntu pōṉēṉē. (2)
[[((8.9.4))]]

$$1732

__&&1732__$$8.9.5##__

vantāyeṉ maṉattē vantunī pukuntapiṉṉi,
entāy. pōyaṟiyāy ituvē yamiyātō
kontār pimpoḻilcūḻ kuṭantik kiṭantukanta
mintā uṉṉiyeṉṟum maṟavāmip peṟṟēṉē.
[[((8.9.5))]]

$$1733

__&&1733__$$8.9.6##__

eñcā vennarakat taḻunti naṭuṅkukiṉṟēṟku,
añcēleṉ ṟaṭiyēṉi āṭkoḷḷa vallāṉi,
neñcē nīniṉiyātu iṟippoḻutumiruttikaṇṭāy,
mañcār māḷikicūḻ vayalāli mintaṉiyē.
[[((8.9.6))]]

$$1734

__&&1734__$$8.9.7##__

peṟṟār peṟṟoḻintār piṉṉumniṉ ṟaṭiyēṉukku,
uṟṟāṉāy vaḷarttu eṉṉuyirāki niṉṟāṉi,
muṟṟā māmatikōḷ viṭuttāṉi yemmāṉi
ettāl yāṉmaṟakkēṉ itucolleṉaṉēḻineñcē.
[[((8.9.7))]]

$$1735

__&&1735__$$8.9.8##__

kaṟṟār paṟṟaṟukkum piṟavip peruṅkaṭalē
paṟṟā vantaṭiyēṉ piṟantēṉ piṟantapiṉṉi
vaṟṟā nīrvayalcūḻ vayalāli yammāṉip
peṟṟēṉ peṟṟatum piṟavāmi peṟṟēṉē.
[[((8.9.8))]]

$$1736

__&&1736__$$8.9.9##__

kaṇṇār kaṇṇapuram kaṭiki kaṭikamaḻum
taṇṇār tāmaricūḻ taliccaṅka mēlticiyuḷ
viṇṇōr nāṇmatiyi virikiṉṟa veñcuṭari
kaṇṇārak kaṇṭukoṇṭu kaḷikkiṉṟatiṅ keṉṟukolō. (2)
[[((8.9.9))]]

$$1737

__&&1737__$$8.9.10##__

cerunīra vēlvalavaṉ kalikaṉṟi maṅkiyarkōṉ
karunīr mukilvaṇṇaṉ kaṇṇa purattāṉi
irunī riṉtamiḻ iṉṉici mālikaḷ koṇṭutoṇṭīr,
varunīr viyamuyya ivipāṭi yāṭumiṉē. (2)
[[((8.9.10))]]

1738__((PTM_8.10))

$$1738

__&&1738__$$8.10.1##__

vaṇṭārpū māmalar maṅki maṇanōkkam
uṇṭāṉē uṉṉi yukantukan tuṉṟaṉakkē
toṇṭāṉēṟku eṉceykiṉ ṟāycollu nālvētam
kaṇṭāṉē kaṇṇa puṟattuṟi yammāṉē. (2)
[[((8.10.1))]]

$$1739

__&&1739__$$8.10.2##__

perunīrum viṇṇum maliyu mulakēḻum
orutārā niṉṉu ḷoṭukkiya niṉṉiyallāl
varutēvar maṟṟuḷareṉ ṟeṉmaṉat tiṟiyum
karutēṉnāṉ kaṇṇa purattuṟi yammāṉē.
[[((8.10.2))]]

$$1740

__&&1740__$$8.10.3##__

maṟṟumōr teyva muḷateṉ ṟiruppārō
ṭuṟṟilēṉ uṟṟatu muṉṉaṭi yārkkaṭimi
maṟṟellam pēcilum niṉtiru veṭṭeḻuttum
kaṟṟu nāṉ kaṇṇa purattuṟi yammāṉē. (2)
[[((8.10.3))]]

$$1741

__&&1741__$$8.10.4##__

peṇṇāṉāḷ pēriḷaṅ koṅkiyi ṉāraḻalpōl
uṇṇānañ cuṇṭukan tāyi yukantēṉnāṉ
maṇṇāḷā. vāḷneṭuṅ kaṇṇi matumalarāḷ
kaṇṇāḷā kaṇṇa purattuṟi yammāṉē.
[[((8.10.4))]]

$$1742

__&&1742__$$8.10.5##__

peṟṟārum cuṟṟamu meṉṟivi pēṇēṉnāṉ
maṟṟārum paṟṟilē ṉātalāl niṉṉaṭintēṉ
uṟṟāṉeṉ ṟuḷḷattu vittaruḷ ceykaṇṭāy
kaṟṟārccēr kaṇṇa purattuṟi yammāṉē.
[[((8.10.5))]]

$$1743

__&&1743__$$8.10.6##__

ēttiyuṉ cēvaṭi yeṇṇi yiruppāri,
pārttirun taṅku namaṉṟamar paṟṟātu
cōttamnām añcutu meṉṟu toṭāmi nī
kāttipōl kaṇṇa purattuṟi yammāṉē.
[[((8.10.6))]]

$$1744

__&&1744__$$8.10.7##__

veḷḷinīr veḷḷat taṇinta aravaṇimēl
tuḷḷunīr meḷḷat tuyiṉṟa perumāṉē
vaḷḷalē uṉṟamark keṉṟum namaṉṟamar
kaḷḷarpōl kaṇṇa purattuṟi yammāṉē.
[[((8.10.7))]]

$$1745

__&&1745__$$8.10.8##__

māṇāki viya maḷantatuvum vāḷavuṇaṉ
pūṇākam kīṇṭatuvum īṇṭu niṉintiruntēṉ
pēṇāta valviṉi yēṉiṭa rettaṉiyum
kāṇēṉnāṉ kaṇṇa purattuṟi yammāṉē.
[[((8.10.8))]]

$$1746

__&&1746__$$8.10.9##__

nāṭṭiṉā yeṉṉi yuṉakkumuṉ toṇṭāka
māṭṭiṉē ṉattaṉiyē koṇṭeṉ valviṉiyi
pāṭṭiṉā luṉṉiyeṉ neñcat tiruntami
kāṭṭiṉāy kaṇṇa purattuṟi yammāṉē.
[[((8.10.9))]]

$$1747

__&&1747__$$8.10.10##__

kaṇṭacīrk kaṇṇa purattuṟi yammāṉi
koṇṭacīrt toṇṭaṉ kaliya ṉolimāli
paṇṭamāyp pāṭu maṭiyavark keññāṉṟum
aṇṭampō yāṭci yavarkka taṟintōmē. (2)
[[((8.10.10))]]

periya tirumoḻi oṉpatām pattu

1748__((PTM_9.1))

$$1748

__&&1748__$$9.1.1##__

vaṅkamā munnīr variniṟap periya
vāḷara viṉaṇi mēvi
caṅkamā raṅkit taṭamala runtic
cāmamā mēṉiyeṉ talivaṉ
aṅkamā ṟintu vēḷvināl vētam
aruṅkali payiṉṟu eri mūṉṟum
ceṅkiyāl vaḷarkkum tuḷakkamil maṉattōr
tirukkaṇṇaṅ kuṭiyuḷniṉ ṟāṉē. (2)
[[((9.1.1))]]

$$1749

__&&1749__$$9.1.2##__

kavaḷamā katatta kariyuyyap poykik
karāmkoḷak kalaṅkiyuḷ niṉintu
tuvaḷamēl vantu tōṉṟivaṉ mutali
tuṇipaṭac cuṭupaṭi turantōṉ
kuvaḷinīḷ muḷari kumutamoṇ kaḻunīr
koymmalar neytaloṇ kaḻaṉi
tivaḷummā ḷikacūḻ ceḻumaṇip puricit
tirukkaṇṇaṅ kuṭiyuḷniṉ ṟāṉē.
[[((9.1.2))]]

$$1750

__&&1750__$$9.1.3##__

vātivan taṭara vāṉamum nilaṉum
malikaḷum alikaṭal kuḷippa
mītukoṇ ṭukaḷum mīṉuru vāki
viripuṉal variyakaṭ ṭoḷittōṉ
pōtalar puṉṉi malliki muval
putuviri matumala raṇintu
cītavoṇ teṉṟal ticitoṟum kamaḻum
tirukkaṇṇaṅ kuṭiyuḷniṉ ṟāṉē.
[[((9.1.3))]]

$$1751

__&&1751__$$9.1.4##__

veṉṟicēr tiṇmi vilaṅkalmā mēṉi
veḷḷeyiṟ ṟoḷḷerit taṟukaṇ
paṉṟiyāy aṉṟu pārmakaḷ payali
tīrttavaṉ pañcavar pākaṉ
oṉṟalā vuruvat tulappilpal kālat
tu uyarkoṭi yoḷivaḷar matiyam,
ceṉṟucēr ceṉṉic cikaranaṉ māṭat
tirukkaṇṇaṅ kuṭiyuḷniṉ ṟāṉē.
[[((9.1.4))]]

$$1752

__&&1752__$$9.1.5##__

maṉṉavaṉ periya vēḷviyil kuṟaḷāy
mūvaṭi nīroṭum koṇṭu
piṉṉumē ḻulakum īraṭi yākap
peruntici yaṭaṅkiṭa nimirntōṉ
aṉṉameṉ kamalat taṇimalarp pīṭat
talipuṉa lilikkuṭi nīḻal
cenneloṇ kavari yaciyavīṟ ṟirukkum
tirukkaṇṇaṅ kuṭiyuḷniṉ ṟāṉē.
[[((9.1.5))]]

$$1753

__&&1753__$$9.1.6##__

maḻuviṉāl avaṉi aracimū veḻukāl
maṇimuṭi poṭipaṭuttu utirak
kuḻuvuvār puṉaluḷ kuḷittuveṅ kōpam
tavirntavaṉ , kulimali katalik
kuḻuvumvār kamukum kuravumnaṟ palavum
kuḷirtaru cūtammā taviyum
ceḻumiyār poḻilkaḷ taḻuvunaṉ māṭat
tirukkaṇṇaṅ kuṭiyuḷniṉ ṟāṉē.
[[((9.1.6))]]

$$1754

__&&1754__$$9.1.7##__

vāṉuḷā ravari valimiyāl naliyum
malikaṭal ilaṅkiyār kōṉi
pāṉucēr carattāl paṉaṅkaṉi pōlap
parumuṭi yutiravil vaḷittōṉ
kāṉulā mayiliṉ kaṇaṅkaḷniṉ ṟāṭak
kaṇamukil muracaniṉ ṟatira
tēṉulā varivaṇ ṭiṉṉici muralum
tirukkaṇṇaṅ kuṭiyuḷniṉ ṟāṉē.
[[((9.1.7))]]

$$1755

__&&1755__$$9.1.8##__

aravunīḷ koṭiyōṉ aviyuḷā caṉatti
añciṭā tēyiṭa, ataṟkup
periyamā mēṉi yaṇṭamū ṭuruvap
peruntici yaṭaṅkiṭa nimirntōṉ
variyiṉmā maṇiyum marakatat tiraḷum
vayiramum vetirutir muttum
tirikoṇarn tunti vayaltoṟum kuvikkum
tirukkaṇṇaṅ kuṭiyuḷ niṉ ṟāṉē.
[[((9.1.8))]]

$$1756

__&&1756__$$9.1.9##__

paṉṉiya pāram pārmakaṭ koḻiyap
pārata māperum pōril
maṉṉarkaḷ maṭiya maṇineṭun tiṇṭēr
mittuṉark kuyttamā māyaṉ
tuṉṉumā taviyum curapuṉip poḻilum
cūḻnteḻu ceṇpaka malarvāy
teṉṉaveṉ ṟaḷikaḷ muraṉṟici pāṭum
tirukkaṇṇaṅ kuṭiyuḷniṉ ṟāṉē.
[[((9.1.9))]]

$$1757

__&&1757__$$9.1.10##__

kaliyulāvalkul kāriki tiṟattuk
kaṭalperum paṭiyoṭum ceṉṟu
ciliyiṉāl ilaṅki tīyeḻac ceṟṟa
tirukkaṇṇaṅ kuṭiyuḷniṉ ṟāṉi
malikulā māṭa maṅkiyar talivaṉ
māṉavēl kaliyaṉvā yolikaḷ
ulavucol māli yoṉpatō ṭoṉṟum
vallavark killinal kuravē. (2)
[[((9.1.10))]]

1758__((PTM_9.2))

$$1758

__&&1758__$$9.2.1##__

poṉṉivar mēṉi maraka tattiṉ
poṅkiḷañ cōti yakalattāram
miṉ ivar vāyilnal vēta mōtum
vētiyar vāṉava rāvartōḻī,
eṉṉiyum nōkkiyeṉ ṉalkulum nōkki
ēntiḷaṅ koṅkiyum nōkkukiṉṟār
aṉṉiyeṉ ṉōkkumeṉ ṟañcu kiṉṟēṉ
accō oruvar aḻakiyavā. (2)
[[((9.2.1))]]

$$1759

__&&1759__$$9.2.2##__

tōṭaviḻ nīlam maṇaṅko ṭukkum
cūḻpuṉal cūḻkuṭan tikkiṭanta
cēṭarko leṉṟu terikka māṭṭēṉ
ceñcuṭa rāḻiyum caṅkumēnti
pāṭaka mellaṭi yārva ṇaṅkap
paṉmaṇi muttoṭi laṅkucōti
āṭakam pūṇṭoru nāṉku tōḷum
accō oruvar aḻakiyavā.
[[((9.2.2))]]

$$1760

__&&1760__$$9.2.3##__

vēyiruñ cōli vilaṅkal cūḻnta
meyya maṇāḷar iv viyamellām
tāyiṉa nāyaka rāvar tōḻī.
tāmarik kaṇkaḷ iruntavāṟu,
cēyiruṅ kuṉṟam tikaḻnta toppac
cevviya vāki malarntacōti
āyiram tōḷo ṭilaṅku pūṇum
accō oruvar aḻakiyavā.
[[((9.2.3))]]

$$1761

__&&1761__$$9.2.4##__

vampavi ḻumtuḻāy māli tōḷmēl
kiyaṉa āḻiyum caṅkum ēnti,
namparnam millam pukuntu niṉṟār
nākari karperi tumiḷiyar
cempava ḷamivar vāyiṉ vaṇṇam
tēva rivara turuvamcollil
ampava ḷattira ḷēyu moppar
accō oruvar aḻakiyavā.
[[((9.2.4))]]

$$1762

__&&1762__$$9.2.5##__

kōḻiyum kūṭalum kōyil koṇṭa
kōvala rēyoppar kuṉṟamaṉṉa
pāḻiyum tōḷumōr nāṉku ṭiyar
paṇṭivar tammiyum kaṇṭaṟiyōm
vāḻiya rōvivar vaṇṇa meṇṇil
mākaṭal pōṉṟuḷar kiyilveyya,
āḻiyoṉ ṟēntiyōr caṅku paṟṟi
accōvoruvaraḻakiyavā.
[[((9.2.5))]]

$$1763

__&&1763__$$9.2.6##__

veñciṉa vēḻam maruppo citta
vēntarkol ēntiḻi yārmaṉatti
tañcuṭi yāḷarkol yāṉa ṟiyēṉ
tāmarik kaṇka ḷiruntavāṟu
kañcaṉi yañcamuṉ kālvi citta
kāḷiyā ravarkaṇ ṭārvaṇaṅkum
añcaṉa māmali yēyu moppar
accōvoruvaraḻakiyavā.
[[((9.2.6))]]

$$1764

__&&1764__$$9.2.7##__

piṇiyaviḻ tāmari moṭṭa larttum
pēraru ḷāḷarkol? yāṉaṟiyēṉ,
paṇiyumeṉ neñcami teṉkol tōḻī.
paṇṭivar tammiyum kaṇṭaṟiyōm
aṇikeḻu tāmari yaṉṉa kaṇṇum
aṅkiyum paṅkaya mēṉivāṉattu,
aṇikeḻu māmuki lēyu moppar
accōvoruvaraḻakiyavā.
[[((9.2.7))]]

$$1765

__&&1765__$$9.2.8##__

mañcuyar māmati tīṇṭa nīṇṭa
māliruñ cōlima ṇāḷarvantu eṉ
neñcuḷḷum kaṇṇuḷḷum niṉṟu nīṅkār
nīrmali yārkol? niṉikkamāṭṭēṉ?
mañcuyar poṉmali mēle ḻunta
māmukil pōṉṟuḷar vantukāṇīr
añciṟip puḷḷumoṉ ṟēṟi vantār
accōvoruvaraḻakiyavā.
[[((9.2.8))]]

$$1766

__&&1766__$$9.2.9##__

eṇṭici yumeṟi nīrkka ṭalum
ēḻula kumuṭa ṉēviḻuṅki
maṇṭiyō rālilip paḷḷi koḷḷum
māyarkol? māyam aṟiyamāṭṭēṉ
koṇṭalnaṉ mālvari yēyu moppar
koṅkalar tāmarik kaṇṇumvāyum
aṇṭat tamarar paṇiya niṉṟār
accōvoruvaraḻakiyavā.
[[((9.2.9))]]

$$1767

__&&1767__$$9.2.10##__

aṉṉamum kēḻalum mīṉu māya
ātiyi nāki yaḻakiyāri
kaṉṉinaṉ māmatiḷ maṅki vēntaṉ
kāmaru cīrkkali kaṉṟi kuṉṟā
iṉṉici yālcoṉṉa ceñcol māli
ēḻu miraṇṭumo roṉṟumvallār,
maṉṉava rāyula kāṇṭu mīṇṭum
vāṉava rāymakiḻ veytuvarē. (2)
[[((9.2.10))]]

1768__((PTM_9.3))

$$1768

__&&1768__$$9.3.1##__

taṉṉi nivik kilēṉval viṉiyēṉ toḻutumeḻu
poṉṉi nivikkum appūñ cerunti maṇinīḻalvāy
eṉṉini vitteḻal koṇṭakaṉ ṟaperu māṉiṭam,
puṉṉimut tampoḻil cūḻntaḻa kāya pullāṇiyē. (2)
[[((9.3.1))]]

$$1769

__&&1769__$$9.3.2##__

uruki neñcē niṉintiṅ kirunteṉ toḻutumeḻu
murukuvaṇ ṭuṇmalark kitiyiṉ nīḻalil muṉṉorunāḷ,
perukukā taṉmi yeṉṉuḷḷa meytap pirintāṉiṭam
porutumun nīrkkarik kēmaṇi yuntu pullāṇiyē.
[[((9.3.2))]]

$$1770

__&&1770__$$9.3.3##__

ētu ceytāl maṟakkēṉ maṉamē toḻutumeḻu
tātu malku taṭañcūḻ poḻiltāḻvar toṭarntu piṉ
pēti niṉip piriyē ṉiṉiyeṉ ṟakaṉṟāṉiṭam
pōtu nāḷuṅ kamaḻum poḻilcūḻnta pullāṇiyē.
[[((9.3.3))]]

$$1771

__&&1771__$$9.3.4##__

koṅkuṇ vaṇṭē kariyāka vantāṉ koṭiyēṟku muṉ
naṅka ḷīcaṉ namakkē paṇitta moḻiceytilaṉ
maṅki nallāy toḻutu meḻupō yavaṉ maṉṉumūr
poṅku munnīrk karikkē maṇiyuntu pullāṇiyē.
[[((9.3.4))]]

$$1772

__&&1772__$$9.3.5##__

uṇari luḷḷam cuṭumāl viṉiyēṉ toḻutumeḻu
tuṇari nāḻal naṟumpōtu namcūḻ kuḻalpeytu piṉ
taṇari lāvi taḷarumeṉa aṉpu tantāṉiṭam,
puṇari yōtam paṇilam maṇiyuntu pullāṇiyē.
[[((9.3.5))]]

$$1773

__&&1773__$$9.3.6##__

eḷki neñcē niṉintiṅ kirunteṉ toḻutumeḻu
vaḷḷal māyaṉ maṇivaṇṇa ṉemmāṉ maruvumiṭam
kaḷḷa viḻummalark kāviyum tūmaṭaṟkitiyum,
puḷḷu maḷḷal paḻaṉaṅ kaḷumcūḻnta pullāṇiyē.
[[((9.3.6))]]

$$1774

__&&1774__$$9.3.7##__

paravi neñcē toḻutum eḻupō yavaṉpālamāy
iravum nāḷum iṉikkaṇ tuyilā tirunteṉpayaṉ?
viravi muttam neṭuveṇ maṇalmēl koṇṭu veṇtiri
puravi yeṉṉap putañceytu vantuntu pullāṇiyē.
[[((9.3.7))]]

$$1775

__&&1775__$$9.3.8##__

alamu māḻip paṭiyu muṭiyār namakkaṉparāy,
calama tākit takavoṉ ṟilarnām toḻutumeḻu,
ulavu kālnal kaḻiyōṅku taṇpim poḻilūṭu ici
pulavu kāṉal kaḷivaṇ ṭiṉampāṭu pullāṇiyē.
[[((9.3.8))]]

$$1776

__&&1776__$$9.3.9##__

ōti nāmam kuḷittucci taṉṉāl oḷimāmalarp
pātam nāḷum paṇivōm namakkē nalamātaliṉ
ātu tārā ṉeṉilum tarum aṉṟiyumaṉparāyp
pōtum mātē toḻutum avaṉmaṉṉu pullāṇiyē.
[[((9.3.9))]]

$$1777

__&&1777__$$9.3.10##__

ilaṅku muttum pavaḷak koḻuntu meḻiltāmari
pulaṅkaḷ muṟṟum poḻilcūḻn taḻakāya pullāṇimēl
kalaṅka lillāp pukaḻāṉ kaliya ṉolimāli
valaṅkoḷ toṇṭark kiṭamā vatupāṭil vikuntamē. (2)
[[((9.3.10))]]

1778__((PTM_9.4))

$$1778

__&&1778__$$9.4.1##__

kāvār maṭalpeṇṇi aṉṟil arikuralum
ēvāyi ṉūṭiyaṅkum eḵkil koṭitālō
pūvār maṇamkamaḻum pullāṇi kitoḻutēṉ
pāvāy itunamakkōr pāṉmiyē yākātē. (2)
[[((9.4.1))]]

$$1779

__&&1779__$$9.4.2##__

muṉṉam kuṟaḷuruvāy mūvaṭimaṇ koṇṭaḷanta,
maṉṉaṉ caritikkē mālākip poṉpayantēṉ
poṉṉam kaḻikkāṉal puḷḷiṉaṅkāḷ pullāṇi
aṉṉamāy nūlpayantāṟ kāṅkitaṉic ceppumiṉē.
[[((9.4.2))]]

$$1780

__&&1780__$$9.4.3##__

vavvit tuḻāyataṉmēl ceṉṟa taṉineñcam
cevvi yaṟiyātu niṟkuṅkol nittilaṅkaḷ
pavvat tiriyulavu pullāṇi kitoḻutēṉ
teyvac ciliyāṟkeṉ cintinōy ceppumiṉē.
[[((9.4.3))]]

$$1781

__&&1781__$$9.4.4##__

pariya iraṇiyaṉ tākam aṇiyukirāl
ariyuruvāyk kīṇṭāṉ aruḷtanta vānamakku
porutirikaḷ pōntulavu pullāṇi kitoḻutēṉ
arimalarkkaṇ ṇīrtatumpa antukilum nillāvē.
[[((9.4.4))]]

$$1782

__&&1782__$$9.4.5##__

villāl ilaṅki malaṅkac caranfturanta
vallāḷaṉ piṉpōṉa neñcam varumaḷavum,
ellāru meṉṟaṉṉi yēcilum pēciṭiṉum,
pullāṇi yemperumāṉ poykēṭ ṭiruntēṉē.
[[((9.4.5))]]

$$1783

__&&1783__$$9.4.6##__

cuḻaṉṟilaṅku veṅkatirōṉ tērōṭum pōymaṟintāṉ
aḻaṉṟu koṭitāki añcuṭaril tāṉaṭumāl
ceḻuntaṭampūñ cōlicūḻ pullāṇi kitoḻutēṉ
iḻantiruntē ṉeṉṟaṉ eḻilniṟamum caṅkumē.
[[((9.4.6))]]

$$1784

__&&1784__$$9.4.7##__

kaṉiyār iṭikuraliṉ kārmaṇiyiṉ nāvāṭal
tiṉiyēṉum nillātu tīyiṟ koṭitālō
puṉiyār maṇimāṭap pullāṇi kitoḻutēṉ
viṉiyēṉmēl vēliyum ventaḻalē vīcumē.
[[((9.4.7))]]

$$1785

__&&1785__$$9.4.8##__

tūmpuṭikki vēḻam veruva maruppocitta
pāmpi ṉaṇiyāṉ aruḷtanta vānamakku
pūñcerunti poṉcoriyum pullāṇi kitoḻutēṉ
tēmpaliḷampiṟiyum eṉṟaṉakkōr ventaḻalē.
[[((9.4.8))]]

$$1786

__&&1786__$$9.4.9##__

vētamum vēḷviyum viṇṇum irucuṭarum,
ātiyu māṉāṉ aruḷtanta vānamakku,
pōtalarum puṉṉicūḻ pullāṇi kitoḻutēṉ,
ōtamum nāṉum uṟaṅkā tiruntēṉē.
[[((9.4.9))]]

$$1787

__&&1787__$$9.4.10##__

poṉṉalarum puṉṉicūḻ pullāṇi yammāṉi
miṉṉiṭiyār vēṭkinōy kūra viruntataṉi
kaṉṉavilum tiṇṭōḷ kaliya ṉolivallār
maṉṉavarāy maṇṇāṇṭu vāṉāṭu muṉṉuvarē. (2)
[[((9.4.10))]]

1788__((PTM_9.5))

$$1788

__&&1788__$$9.5.1##__

tavaḷa iḷampiṟi tuḷḷumunnīrt
taṇmalart teṉṟalō ṭaṉṟiloṉṟit
tuvaḷa eṉ ṉeñcakam cōravīrum
cūḻpaṉi nāḷtuyi lātiruppēṉ
ivaḷumōr peṇkoṭi yeṉṟiraṅkār
eṉṉala mintumuṉ koṇṭupōṉa
kuvaḷi malarniṟa vaṇṇarmaṉṉu
kuṟuṅkuṭik kēyeṉṉi yuyttiṭumiṉ. (2)
[[((9.5.1))]]

$$1789

__&&1789__$$9.5.2##__

tātaviḻ malliki pullivanta
taṇmati yiṉiḷa vāṭiyiṉṉē
ūti tiritan tuḻaṟiyuṇṇa
ōrira vumuṟaṅ kēṉ uṟaṅkum
pētiyar pētimi yāliruntu
pēcilum pēcuka peyvaḷiyār
kōti naṟumalar maṅkimārvaṉ
kuṟuṅkuṭik kēyeṉṉi yuyttiṭumiṉ.
[[((9.5.2))]]

$$1790

__&&1790__$$9.5.3##__

kāliyum māliyot tuṇṭukaṅkul
nāḻiki yūḻiyiṉf nīṇṭulāvum,
pōlvatōr taṉmi pukuntuniṟkum
poṅkaḻa lēyokkum vāṭicolli
mālavaṉ māmaṇi vaṇṇaṉmāyam
maṟṟu muḷavavi vantiṭāmuṉ
kōla mayilpayi lumpuṟavil
kuṟuṅkuṭik kēyeṉṉi yuyttiṭumiṉ.
[[((9.5.3))]]

$$1791

__&&1791__$$9.5.4##__

karumaṇi pūṇṭuveṇ ṇākaṇintu
kārimi lēṟṟaṇar tāḻntulāvum
orumaṇi yōciyeṉ ṉuḷḷantaḷḷa
ōrira vumuṟaṅ kātiruppēṉ
perumaṇi vāṉava ruccivitta
pēraru ḷāḷaṉ perumipēci
kurumaṇi nīrkoḻik kumpuṟavil
kuṟuṅkuṭik kēyeṉṉi uyttiṭumiṉ.
[[((9.5.4))]]

$$1792

__&&1792__$$9.5.5##__

tiṇṭimi lēṟṟiṉ maṇiyum āyaṉ
tīṅkuḻa lōciyum teṉṟalōṭu
koṇṭatōr māliyum antiyīṉṟa
kōla iḷampiṟi yōṭukūṭi
paṇṭiyavallavivinamakkup
pāviyē ṉāviyi vāṭṭañceyyum
koṇṭal maṇiniṟa vaṇṇarmaṉṉu
kuṟuṅḵuṭik kēyeṉṉi uyttiṭumiṉ.
[[((9.5.5))]]

$$1793

__&&1793__$$9.5.6##__

elliyum naṉpaka lumiruntē
ēcilum ēcuka ēntiḻiyār
nallar avartiṟam nāmaṟiyōm
nāṇmaṭam accam namakkiṅkilli
vallaṉa colli makiḻvarēṉum
māmaṇi vaṇṇari nāmmaṟavōm
kolli vaḷariḷa mullipulku
kuṟuṅkuṭik kēyeṉṉi uyttiṭumiṉ.
[[((9.5.6))]]

$$1794

__&&1794__$$9.5.7##__

ceṅka ṇeṭiya kariyamēṉit
tēva roruvariṅ kēpukuntu eṉ
aṅkam meliya vaḷikaḻala
ātuko lō eṉṟu coṉṉapiṉṉi
aiṅkaṇi villitaṉ āṇmiyeṉṉō
ṭāṭu mataṉi yaṟiyamāṭṭēṉ
koṅkalar taṇpaṇi cūḻpuṟavil
kuṟuṅkuṭik kēyeṉṉi uyttiṭumiṉ.
[[((9.5.7))]]

$$1795

__&&1795__$$9.5.8##__

kēvala maṉṟu kaṭaliṉōci
kēṇmiṉkaḷ āyaṉki āmpalvantu eṉ
āvi yaḷavum aṇintuniṟkum
aṉṟiyum aintu kaṇiterintiṭṭu
ēvalaṅ kāṭṭi ivaṉoruvaṉ
ippaṭi yēpukun teytiṭāmuṉ
kōvalar kūttaṉ kuṟippaṟintu
kuṟuṅkuṭik kēyeṉṉi uyttiṭumiṉ.
[[((9.5.8))]]

$$1796

__&&1796__$$9.5.9##__

cōtteṉa niṉṟu toḻaviraṅkāṉ
toṉṉalaṅ koṇṭeṉakku iṉṟukāṟum
pōrppatōr poṟpaṭam tantupōṉāṉ
pōyiṉa vūraṟi yēṉ eṉkoṅki
mūttiṭu kiṉṟaṉa maṟṟavaṉṟaṉ
moyyaka lam aṇi yātuvāḷā
kūtta ṉimiyavar kōṉvirumpum
kuṟuṅkuṭik kēyeṉṉi uyttiṭumiṉ.
[[((9.5.9))]]

$$1797

__&&1797__$$9.5.10##__

ceṟṟavaṉ teṉṉilaṅ kimalaṅkat
tēvarpi rāṉtiru māmakaḷip
peṟṟum eṉ neñcakam kōyilkoṇṭa
pēraru ḷāḷaṉ perumipēcak
kaṟṟavaṉ kāmaru cīrkkaliyaṉ
kaṇṇakat tummaṉat tumakalāk
koṟṟavaṉ muṟṟula kāḷiniṉṟa
kuṟuṅkuṭik kēyeṉṉi uyttiṭumiṉ. (2)
[[((9.5.10))]]

1798__((PTM_9.6))

$$1798

__&&1798__$$9.6.1##__

akkum puliyiṉ ataḷum uṭiyār avaroruvar,
pakkam niṟka niṉṟa paṇpar ūrpōlum
takka marattiṉ tāḻciṉiyēṟi, tāyvāyil
kokkiṉ piḷḷi veḷḷiṟa vuṇṇum kuṟuṅkuṭiyē. (2)
[[((9.6.1))]]

$$1799

__&&1799__$$9.6.2##__

tuṅkārāravattirivan tulavat toṭukaṭaluḷ,
poṅkārāravil tuyilum puṉitar ūrpōlum,
ceṅkā laṉṉam tikaḻtaṇ paṇiyil peṭiyōṭum,
koṅkār kamalat talaril cērum kuṟuṅkuṭiyē.
[[((9.6.2))]]

$$1800

__&&1800__$$9.6.3##__

vāḻak kaṇṭōm vantu kāṇmiṉ toṇṭīrkāḷ,
kēḻal ceṅkaṇ māmukil vaṇṇar maruvumūr,
ēḻic ceṅkāl iṉtuṇi nārik kiritēṭi,
kūḻip pārvik kārvayal mēyum kuṟuṅkuṭiyē.
[[((9.6.3))]]

$$1801

__&&1801__$$9.6.4##__

ciramunṉintumintum cintac ceṉṟu, arakkaṉ
uramum karamum tuṇitta vuravōṉ ūrpōlum,
iravum pakalum īṉtēṉ murala, maṉṟellām
kuraviṉ pūvē tāṉmaṇam nāṟuṅ kuṟuṅkuṭiyē.
[[((9.6.4))]]

$$1802

__&&1802__$$9.6.5##__

kavvik kaḷiṟṟu maṉṉar māḷak kalimāṉtēr
aivark kāy,aṉ ṟamaril uyttāṉ ūrpōlum,
mivit tilaṅku kaṇṇār taṅkaḷ moḻiyoppāṉ,
kovvik kaṉivāyk kiḷḷi pēcum kuṟuṅḵuṭiyē.
[[((9.6.5))]]

$$1803

__&&1803__$$9.6.6##__

tīnīr vaṇṇa māmalar koṇṭu viriyēnti,
tūnīr paravit toḻumi ṉeḻumiṉ toṇṭīrkāḷ,
mānīr vaṇṇaṉ maruvi yuṟiyum iṭam vāṉil
kūṉīr matiyi māṭam tīṇṭum kuṟuṅkuṭiyē.
[[((9.6.6))]]

$$1804

__&&1804__$$9.6.7##__

vallic ciṟuñuṇ ṇiṭiyā riṭinīr vikkiṉṟa,
allal cinti tavira aṭimiṉaṭiyīrkāḷ
collil tiruvē yaṉiyār kaṉivāy eyiṟoppāṉ,
kolli mulli mellarum pīṉum kuṟuṅkuṭiyē.
[[((9.6.7))]]

$$1805

__&&1805__$$9.6.8##__

nārār iṇṭi nāṇmalar koṇṭu namtamarkāḷ,
ārā aṉpō ṭemperumāṉūr aṭimiṉkaḷ,
tārāvārum vārpuṉal mēyntu vayalvāḻum
kūrvāy nāri pēṭiyo ṭāṭum kuṟuṅkuṭiyē.
[[((9.6.8))]]

$$1806

__&&1806__$$9.6.9##__

niṉṟa viṉiyum tuyarum keṭamā malarēnti,
ceṉṟu paṇimi ṉeḻumiṉ toḻumiṉ toṇṭīrkāḷ,
eṉṟum miravum pakalum varivaṇ ṭicipāṭa,
kuṉṟiṉ mulli maṉṟiṭi nāṟum kuṟuṅkuṭiyē.
[[((9.6.9))]]

$$1807

__&&1807__$$9.6.10##__

ciliyāl ilaṅki ceṟṟāṉ maṟṟōr ciṉavēḻam,
koliyār kompu koṇṭāṉ mēya kuṟuṅkuṭimēl,
kaliyār paṉuval vallāṉ kaliya ṉolimāli
niliyār pāṭal pāṭap pāvam nillāvē. (2)
[[((9.6.10))]]

1808__((PTM_9.7))

$$1808

__&&1808__$$9.7.1##__

tantitāy makkaḷē cuṟṟameṉ
ṟuṟṟuvar paṟṟi niṉṟa
pantamār vāḻkkiyi nontunī
paḻiyeṉak karuti ṉāyēl
antamā yātiyāy ātikkum
ātiyāy āya ṉāya
mintaṉār vallavāḻ collumā
valliyāy maruvu neñcē. (2)
[[((9.7.1))]]

$$1809

__&&1809__$$9.7.2##__

miṉṉumā valliyum vañciyum
veṉṟañuṇ ṇiṭinuṭaṅkum,
aṉṉameṉ ṉaṭiyiṉār kalaviyi
aruvarut tañci ṉāyēl,
tuṉṉumā maṇimuṭip pañcavark
kākimuṉ tūtu ceṉṟa
maṉṉaṉār vallavāḻ collumā
valliyāy maruvu neñcē.
[[((9.7.2))]]

$$1810

__&&1810__$$9.7.3##__

pūṇulā meṉmulip pāvimār
poyyiṉi meyyi te ṉṟu,
pēṇuvār pēcumap pēccinī
piḻiyeṉak karuti ṉāyēl
nīṇilā veṇkuṭi vāṇaṉār
vēḷviyil maṇṇi ranta
māṇiyār vallavāḻ collumā
valliyāy maruvu neñcē.
[[((9.7.3))]]

$$1811

__&&1811__$$9.7.4##__

paṇṇulām meṉmoḻip pāvimār
paṇimuli aṇitum nām eṉṟu,
eṇṇuvār eṇṇama toḻittunī
piḻittuyyak karuti ṉāyēl,
viṇṇuḷār viṇṇiṉmī tiyaṉṟavēṅ
kaṭattuḷār vaḷaṅkoḷ munnīr
vaṇṇaṉār vallavāḻ collumā
valliyāy maruvu neñcē.
[[((9.7.4))]]

$$1812

__&&1812__$$9.7.5##__

mañcutōy veṇkuṭi maṉṉarāy
vāraṇam cūḻa vāḻntār
tuñciṉā reṉpatōr collinī
tuyareṉak karuti ṉāyēl
nañcutōy koṅkimēl aṅkivāy
vittavaḷ nāḷi yuṇṭa
mañcaṉār vallavāḻ collumā
valliyāy maruvu neñcē.
[[((9.7.5))]]

$$1813

__&&1813__$$9.7.6##__

uruviṉār piṟavicēr ūṉpoti
naramputōl kurampi yuḷpukku
aruvinōy ceytuniṉ ṟivartām
vāḻvataṟ kañci ṉāyēl
tiruviṉār vētanāṉ kintutī
vēḷviyō ṭaṅka māṟum
maruviṉār vallavāḻ collumā
valliyāy maruvu neñcē.
[[((9.7.6))]]

$$1814

__&&1814__$$9.7.7##__

nōyelām peytatōr ākkiyi
meyyeṉak koṇṭu vāḷā
pēyartām pēcumap pēccinī
piḻiyeṉak karuti ṉāyēl,
tīyalā veṅkatirt tiṅkaḷāy
maṅkulvā ṉāki niṉṟa
māyaṉār vallavāḻ collumā
valliyāy maruvu neñcē.
[[((9.7.7))]]

$$1815

__&&1815__$$9.7.8##__

mañcucēr vāṉeri nīrnilam
kālivi mayaṅki niṉṟa
añcucērākkiyi araṇamaṉ
ṟeṉṟuyyak karuti ṉāyēl,
cantucēr meṉmulip poṉmalarp
pāviyum tāmum nāḷum
vantucēr vallavāḻ collumā
valliyāy maruvu neñcē.
[[((9.7.8))]]

$$1816

__&&1816__$$9.7.9##__

veḷḷiyār piṇṭiyār pōtiyār
eṉṟivar ōtu kiṉṟa
kaḷḷanūl taṉṉiyum karumamaṉ
ṟeṉṟuyyak karuti ṉāyēl,
teḷḷiyār kitoḻum tēvaṉār
māmunīr amutu tanta,
vaḷḷalār vallavāḻ collumā
valliyāy maruvu neñcē.
[[((9.7.9))]]

$$1817

__&&1817__$$9.7.10##__

maṟivalār kuṟivilār uṟiyumūr
vallavāḻaṭikaḷ tammi,
ciṟikulā vaṇṭaṟi cōlicūḻ
kōlanīḷ āli nāṭaṉ
kaṟiyulā vēlvalla kaliyaṉvāy
oliyivi kaṟṟu vallār
iṟivarāy irunilam kāvalpūṇ
ṭiṉpanaṉ keytu vārē. (2)
[[((9.7.10))]]

1818__((PTM_9.8))

$$1818

__&&1818__$$9.8.1##__

muntuṟa vurikkēṉ virikkuḻal maṭavār
kalaviyi viṭutaṭu māṟal
antara mēḻum alikaṭa lēḻum
āyavem maṭikaḷtam kōyil,
cantoṭu maṇiyum aṇimayil taḻiyum
taḻuvivan taruvikaḷ nirantu,
vantiḻi cāral māliruñ cōli
vaṇaṅkutum vāmaṭa neñcē. (2)
[[((9.8.1))]]

$$1819

__&&1819__$$9.8.2##__

iṇṭiyum puṉalum koṇṭiṭi yiṉṟi
eḻumiṉō toḻutum eṉṟu imiyōr
aṇṭarum parava aravaṇit tuyiṉṟa
cuṭarmuṭik kaṭavuḷtam kōyil
viṇṭalar tūḷi vēyvaḷar puṟavil
virimalark kuṟiñciyiṉ naṟuntēṉ
vaṇṭamar cāral māliruñ cōli
vaṇaṅkutum vāmaṭa neñcē.
[[((9.8.2))]]

$$1820

__&&1820__$$9.8.3##__

piṇivaḷar ākki nīṅka niṉ ṟēttap
perunilam aruḷiṉmuṉ aruḷi
aṇivaḷar kuṟaḷāy akaliṭam muḻutum
aḷantavem maṭikaḷtam kōyil
kaṇivaḷar vēṅki neṭunila mataṉil
kuṟavartam kavaṇiṭit turanta
maṇivaḷar cāral māliruñcōli
vaṇaṅkutum vāmaṭa neñcē.
[[((9.8.3))]]

$$1821

__&&1821__$$9.8.4##__

cūrmayi lāya pēymuli cuvittuc
cuṭucaram aṭucilit turantu
nīrmiyi lāta tāṭaki māḷa
niṉintavar maṉamkoṇṭa kōyil
kārmali vēṅki kōṅkalar puṟavil
kaṭimalar kuṟiñciyiṉ naṟuntēṉ
vārpuṉal cūḻtaṇ māliruñ cōli
vaṇaṅkutum vāmaṭa neñcē.
[[((9.8.4))]]

$$1822

__&&1822__$$9.8.5##__

vaṇaṅkalil arakkaṉ cerukkaḷat taviya
maṇimuṭi orupatum puraḷa
aṇaṅkeḻun tavaṉṟaṉ kavantamniṉ ṟāṭa
amarcceyta aṭikaḷtam kōyil
piṇaṅkalil neṭuvēy nutimukam kiḻippap
piracamvan tiḻitara peruntēṉ
maṇaṅkamaḻ cāral māliruñ cōli
vaṇaṅkutum vāmaṭa neñcē.
[[((9.8.5))]]

$$1823

__&&1823__$$9.8.6##__

viṭaṅkalan tamarnta aravaṇit tuyiṉṟu
viḷaṅkaṉik kiḷaṅkaṉṟu viciṟi,
kuṭaṅkalan tāṭik kuravimuṉ kōtta
kūttavem maṭikaḷtam kōyil
taṭaṅkaṭal mukantu vicumpiṭip piḷiṟat
taṭavarik kaḷiṟeṉṟu muṉintu
maṭaṅkalniṉ ṟatirum māliruñ cōli
vaṇaṅkutum vāmaṭa neñcē.
[[((9.8.6))]]

$$1824

__&&1824__$$9.8.7##__

tēṉukaṉ āvi pōyuka aṅkōr
ceḻuntiraḷ paṉaṅkaṉi yutira
tāṉukan teṟinta taṭaṅkaṭal vaṇṇar
eṇṇimuṉ iṭaṅkoṇṭa kōyil,
vāṉakac cōli marakatac cāyal
māmaṇik kallatar niṟintu,
māṉukar cāral māliruñ cōli
vaṇaṅkutum vāmaṭa neñcē.
[[((9.8.7))]]

$$1825

__&&1825__$$9.8.8##__

putamiku vicumpil puṇariceṉ ṟaṇavap
porukaṭal aravaṇit tuyiṉṟu,
patamiku pariyiṉ mikuciṉam tavirtta
paṉimukil vaṇṇartam kōyil,
katamiku ciṉatta kaṭataṭak kaḷiṟṟiṉ
kavuḷvaḻik kaḷivaṇṭu paruka,
matamiku cāral māliruñ cōli
vaṇaṅkutum vāmaṭa neñcē.
[[((9.8.8))]]

$$1826

__&&1826__$$9.8.9##__

puntiyil camaṇar puttareṉ ṟivarkaḷ
ottaṉa pēcavum ukantiṭṭu,
entipem māṉār imiyavar talivar
eṇṇimuṉ iṭaṅkoṇṭa kōyil,
cantaṉap poḻiliṉ tāḻciṉi nīḻal
tāḻvari makaḷirkaḷ nāḷum,
mantirat tiṟiñcum māliruñ cōli
vaṇaṅkutum vāmaṭa neñcē.
[[((9.8.9))]]

$$1827

__&&1827__$$9.8.10##__

vaṇṭamar cāral māliruñ cōli
māmaṇi vaṇṇari vaṇaṅkum,
toṇṭarip paravum cuṭaroḷi neṭuvēl
cūḻvayal ālinaṉ ṉāṭaṉ
kaṇṭalnal vēli maṅkiyar talivaṉ
kaliyaṉvā yoliceyta paṉuval,
koṇṭivi pāṭum tavamuṭi yārkaḷ
āḷvarik kurikaṭa lulakē. (2)
[[((9.8.10))]]

1828__((PTM_9.9))

$$1828

__&&1828__$$9.9.1##__

mūvaril muṉmutal vaṉmuḻaṅ kārkaṭa luḷkiṭantu,
pūvula runtitaṉ ṉuḷpuva ṉampaṭit tuṇṭumiḻnta,
tēvarkaḷ nāyaka ṉittiru māliruñ cōliniṉṟa,
kōvalar kōvin taṉikkoṭi yēriṭi kūṭuṅkolō. (2)
[[((9.9.1))]]

$$1829

__&&1829__$$9.9.2##__

puṉivaḷar pūmpoḻi lārpoṉṉi cūḻaraṅ kanakaruḷ
muṉivaṉi, mūvula kumpaṭit tamutal mūrttitaṉṉi,
ciṉivaḷar pūmpoḻil cūḻtiru māliruñ cōliniṉṟāṉ
kaṉikaḻal kāṇuṅko lōkayal kaṇṇiyem kārikiyē. (2)
[[((9.9.2))]]

$$1830

__&&1830__$$9.9.3##__

uṇṭula kēḻiṉi yum oru pālakaṉ ālilimēl,
kaṇṭuyil koṇṭukan takaru māṇikka māmaliyi,
tiṇṭiṟal mākari cērtiru māliruñ cōliniṉṟa,
aṇṭartam kōviṉi yiṉṟaṇu kuṅkoleṉ āyiḻiyē.
[[((9.9.3))]]

$$1831

__&&1831__$$9.9.4##__

ciṅkama tāyavu ṇaṉtiṟa lākammuṉ kīṇṭukanta,
paṅkaya māmalark kaṇpara ṉiyem parañcuṭari,
tiṅkaḷnaṉ māmukil cērtiru māliruñ cōliniṉṟa,
naṅkaḷpi rāṉiyiṉ ṟunaṇu kuṅkoleṉ naṉṉutalē.
[[((9.9.4))]]

$$1832

__&&1832__$$9.9.5##__

tāṉavaṉ vēḷvitaṉ ṉiltaṉi yēkuṟa ḷāynimirntu,
vāṉamum maṇṇaka mum aḷan tatiri vikkiramaṉ,
tēṉamar pūmpoḻil cūḻtiru māliruñ cōliniṉṟa,
vāṉavar kōṉiyiṉ ṟuvaṇaṅ kittoḻa vallaḷ kolō.
[[((9.9.5))]]

$$1833

__&&1833__$$9.9.6##__

nēcami lātavark kumniṉi yātavark kummariyāṉ,
vācama larppoḻil cūḻvaṭa māmatu rippiṟantāṉ,
tēcamel lāmvaṇaṅ kumtiru māliruñ cōliniṉṟa,
kēcava nampitaṉ ṉikkeṇṭi yoṇkaṇṇi kāṇuṅkolō. (2)
[[((9.9.6))]]

$$1834

__&&1834__$$9.9.7##__

puḷḷiṉi vāypiḷan tuporu mākari kompocittu,
kaḷḷac cakaṭutit takaru māṇikka māmaliyi,
teḷḷaru vikoḻik kumtiru māliruñ cōliniṉṟa,
vaḷḷali vāṇuta lāḷvaṇaṅ kittoḻa vallaḷkolō.
[[((9.9.7))]]

$$1835

__&&1835__$$9.9.8##__

pārttaṉuk kaṉṟaru ḷippāra tattoru tērmuṉṉiṉṟu,
kāttavaṉ ṟaṉṉiviṇ ṇōrkaru māṇikka māmaliyi,
tīrttaṉip pūmpoḻil cūḻtiru māliruñ cōliniṉṟa,
mūrttiyik kitoḻa vummuṭi yuṅkoleṉ moykuḻaṟkē. (2)
[[((9.9.8))]]

$$1836

__&&1836__$$9.9.9##__

valampuri yāḻi yaṉivari yārtiraḷ tōḷaṉṟaṉṉi,
pulampuri nūlava ṉippoḻil vēṅkaṭa vētiyaṉi,
cilampiya lāṟuṭi yatiru māliruñ cōliniṉṟa,
nalantikaḻ nāraṇa ṉinaṇu kuṅkoleṉ naṉṉutalē. (2)
[[((9.9.9))]]

$$1837

__&&1837__$$9.9.10##__

tēṭaṟ kariyava ṉittiru māliruñ cōli niṉṟa,
āṭal paṟavi yaṉi aṇi yāyiḻi kāṇumeṉṟu,
māṭak koṭimatiḷ cūḻmaṅki yārkali kaṉṟicoṉṉa
pāṭal paṉuvalpat tumpayil vārkkilli pāvaṅkaḷē. (2)
[[((9.9.10))]]

1838__((PTM_9.10))

$$1838

__&&1838__$$9.10.1##__

eṅka ḷemmiṟi yempirā ṉimiyōrkku
nāyakaṉ, ēt taṭiyavar
taṅkaḷ tammaṉattup piriyā taruḷpurivāṉ,
poṅkutaṇ ṇaruvi putamceyyap
poṉkaḷē citaṟu milaṅkoḷi,
ceṅkamala malarum tirukkōṭṭi yūrāṉē. (2)
[[((9.10.1))]]

$$1839

__&&1839__$$9.10.2##__

evvanōy tavirppāṉ emakkiṟi
iṉṉakit tuvarvāy, nilamakaḷ
cevvi tōya vallāṉ tirumā makaṭkiṉiyāṉ,
muval māli vaṇṭāṭum malliki
māliyoṭu maṇantu, mārutam
teyvam nāṟa varumtiruk kōṭṭi yūrāṉē.
[[((9.10.2))]]

$$1840

__&&1840__$$9.10.3##__

veḷḷiyāṉ kariyāṉ maṇiniṟa vaṇṇaṉ
viṇṇavar tamakkiṟi, emakku
oḷḷiyā ṉuyarntā ṉulakēḻu muṇṭumiḻntāṉ,
tuḷḷunīr moṇṭu koṇṭu cāmarik
kaṟṟic cantaṉa munti vantaci,
teḷḷunīrp puṟavil tirukkōṭṭi yūrāṉē.
[[((9.10.3))]]

$$1841

__&&1841__$$9.10.4##__

ēṟu mēṟi ilaṅkumoṇ maḻuppaṟṟum
īcaṟ kicintu, uṭampilōr
kūṟutāṉ koṭuttāṉ kulamāmakaṭ kiṉiyāṉ,
nāṟu caṇpaka malliki malarpulki
iṉṉiḷa vaṇṭu, naṉṉaṟun
tēṟalvāy maṭukkum tirukkōṭṭi yūrāṉē.
[[((9.10.4))]]

$$1842

__&&1842__$$9.10.5##__

vaṅka mākaṭal vaṇṇaṉ māmaṇi vaṇṇaṉ
viṇṇavar kōṉma tumalart
toṅkal nīṇmuṭi yāṉneṭi yāṉpaṭi kaṭantāṉ,
maṅkul tōymaṇi māṭa veṇkoṭi
māka mītuyarn tēṟi, vāṉuyar
tiṅkaḷ tāṉaṇavum tirukkōṭṭi yūrāṉē.
[[((9.10.5))]]

$$1843

__&&1843__$$9.10.6##__

kāvala ṉilaṅkik kiṟikalaṅ kaccaram
celavuyttu, maṟṟavaṉ
ēvalam tavirttāṉ eṉṉi yāḷuṭi yempirāṉ,
nāva lampuvi maṉṉar vantu vaṇaṅka
māluṟi kiṉṟatiṅkeṉa,
tēvar vantiṟiñcum tirukkōṭṭi yūrāṉē.
[[((9.10.6))]]

$$1844

__&&1844__$$9.10.7##__

kaṉṟu koṇṭu viḷaṅkaṉi yeṟintu ānirik
kaḻiveṉṟu, māmaḻi
niṉṟu kāttukan tāṉnila māmakaṭ kiṉiyāṉ,
kuṉṟiṉ mulliyiṉ vācamum kuḷirmalliki
maṇamum aḷintu,iḷan
teṉṟal vantulavum tirukkōṭṭi yūrāṉē.
[[((9.10.7))]]

$$1845

__&&1845__$$9.10.8##__

pūṅku runtocit tāṉikāyn tarimāc cekuttu,
aṭiyēṉi yāḷukantu
īṅkeṉ ṉuḷpukun tāṉimi yōrkaḷtam perumāṉ,
tūṅku taṇpala viṉkaṉi tokuvāḻiyiṉ
kaṉiyoṭu māṅkaṉi
tēṅku taṇpuṉalcūḻ tirukkōṭṭi yūrāṉē.
[[((9.10.8))]]

$$1846

__&&1846__$$9.10.9##__

kōvi yiṉtamiḻ pāṭu vārkuṭam
āṭu vārtaṭa māmalarmici,
mēvu nāṉmukaṉil viḷaṅku purinūlar,
mēvu nāṉmaṟi vāṇar aivaki vēḷvi
āṟaṅkam vallavar toḻum,
tēva tēvapirāṉ tirukkōṭṭi yūrāṉē.
[[((9.10.9))]]

$$1847

__&&1847__$$9.10.10##__

ālumā valavaṉ kalikaṉṟi maṅkiyar
talivaṉ aṇipoḻil
cēlkaḷ pāykaḻaṉit tirukkōṭṭi yūrāṉi,
nīla māmukil vaṇṇaṉi neṭumāli
iṉtami ḻālni ṉinta,in
nālu māṟumval lārkkiṭa mākum vāṉulakē. (2)
[[((9.10.10))]]

tirumaṅkiyāḻvār aruḷicceyta periya tirumoḻi pattām pattu

((kali viruttam))

1848__((PTM_10.1))

$$1848

__&&1848__$$10.1.1##__

orunaṟ cuṟṟam eṉakkuyir oṇporuḷ
varunal tolkati yākiya mintaṉi
nerunal kaṇṭatu nīrmali yiṉṟupōy
karunel cuḻkaṇṇa maṅkiyuḷ kāṇṭumē (2)
[[((10.1.1))]]

$$1849

__&&1849__$$10.1.2##__

poṉṉi māmaṇi yiyaṇi yārntatōr
miṉṉi vēṅkaṭat tucciyiṟ kaṇṭupōy
eṉṉi yāḷuṭi yīcaṉi yempirāṉ
ṟaṉṉi yāmceṉṟu kāṇṭumtaṇ kāvilē
[[((10.1.2))]]

$$1850

__&&1850__$$10.1.3##__

vēli yālilip paḷḷi virumpiya
pāli āramu tattiṉip pintuḻāy
māli āliyil kaṇṭu makiḻntu pōy
ñāla muṉṉiyik kāṇṭumnāṅ kūrilē
[[((10.1.3))]]

$$1851

__&&1851__$$10.1.4##__

tuḷakka milcuṭa ri,avu ṇaṉuṭal
piḷakkum mintaṉip pēril vaṇaṅkippōy
aḷappi lāramu tiyama rarkkaruḷ
viḷakkiṉi ceṉṟu veḷḷaṟik kāṇṭumē
[[((10.1.4))]]

$$1852

__&&1852__$$10.1.5##__

cuṭali yilcuṭu nīṟaṉ amarntatuōr
naṭali tīrttava ṉinaṟi yūrkaṇṭu,eṉ
uṭali yuḷpukun tuḷḷa murukkiyuṇ
viṭali yicceṉṟu kāṇṭummey yattuḷē
[[((10.1.5))]]

$$1853

__&&1853__$$10.1.6##__

vāṉi āramu tamtanta vaḷḷali
tēṉi nīḷvayal cēṟiyil kaṇṭupōy
āṉi vāṭṭi yaruḷum amararttam
kōṉi, yāmkuṭan ticceṉṟu kāṇṭumē
[[((10.1.6))]]

$$1854

__&&1854__$$10.1.7##__

kūnta lārmakiḻ kōvala ṉāy veṇṇey
mānta ḻuntiyil kaṇṭu makiḻntupōy
pāntaḷ pāḻiyil paḷḷi virumpiya
vēnta ṉicceṉṟu kāṇṭumveḵ kāvuḷē
[[((10.1.7))]]

$$1855

__&&1855__$$10.1.8##__

patta rāviyip pāṉmati yi,aṇit
totti māliruñ cōlit toḻutupōy
mutti ṉimaṇi yimaṇi māṇikka
vitti ṉi,ceṉṟu viṇṇakark kāṇṭumē
[[((10.1.8))]]

$$1856

__&&1856__$$10.1.9##__

kampa mākaḷi ṟañcik kalaṅka,ōr
kompu koṇṭa kurikaḻal kūttaṉi
kompu lāmpoḻil kōṭṭiyūrk kaṇṭupōy
nampa ṉicceṉṟu kaṇṭumnā vāyuḷē
[[((10.1.9))]]

$$1857

__&&1857__$$10.1.10##__

peṟṟam āḷiyi pēril maṇāḷaṉi
kaṟṟa ñūlkali kaṉṟi yuriceyta
coṟṟi ṟamivi colliya toṇṭarkaṭku
aṟṟa milliyaṇ ṭamavark kāṭciyē
[[((10.1.10))]]
eṇcīrk kaḻineṭilaṭi āciriya viruttam

1858__((PTM_10.2))

$$1858

__&&1858__$$10.2.1##__

irakka miṉṟiyeṅ kōṉceyta tīmi
immi yēyemak keytiṟṟuk kāṇīr
parakka yāmiṉ ṟuritteṉ iravaṇaṉ
paṭṭa ṉaṉiṉi yavarkku rikkōm
kurakku nāyakar kāḷ.iḷaṅ kōvē
kōla valvi lirāma pirāṉē
arakka rāṭaḻip pārilli nāṅkaḷ
añci ṉōntaṭam poṅkattam poṅkō
[[((10.2.1))]]

$$1859

__&&1859__$$10.2.2##__

pattu nīḷmuṭi yumavaṟ ṟiraṭṭip
pāḻit tōḷum paṭittavaṉ celvam,
cittam maṅkiyar pālvittuk keṭṭāṉ
ceyva toṉṟaṟi yāvaṭi yōṅkaḷ
otta tōḷiraṇ ṭumoru muṭiyum
oruvar tamtiṟat tōmaṉṟi vāḻntōm
atta. emperu māṉ.emmik kollēl
añci ṉēmtaṭam poṅkattam poṅkō
[[((10.2.2))]]

$$1860

__&&1860__$$10.2.3##__

taṇṭa kāraṇi yampukun taṉṟu
tiya littaka viliyeṅ kōmāṉ
koṇṭu pōntukeṭ ṭāṉemak kiṅkōr
kuṟṟa millikol lēlkula vēntē
peṇṭi rālkeṭu mikkuṭi taṉṉip
pēcu kiṉṟateṉ? tācara tī,uṉ
aṇṭa vaṇar ukappatē ceytāy
añci ṉōmtaṭam poṅkattam poṅkō
[[((10.2.3))]]

$$1861

__&&1861__$$10.2.4##__

eñca lililṅ kikkiṟi yeṅkōṉ
ṟaṉṉi muṉpaṇintu eṅkaḷkaṇ mukappē
nañcu tāṉarak karkuṭik keṉṟu
naṅki yiyavaṉ tampiyē coṉṉāṉ
viñci vāṉavar vēṇṭiṟṟē paṭṭōm
vēri vārpoḻil māmayi laṉṉa
añci lōtiyik koṇṭu naṭamiṉ
añci ṉōmtaṭam poṅkattam poṅkō
[[((10.2.4))]]

$$1862

__&&1862__$$10.2.5##__

cempoṉ nīḷmuṭi eṅkaḷ iravaṇaṉ
cīti yeṉpatōr teyvam koṇarntu
vampu lāmkaṭi kāvil ciṟiyā
vitta tēkuṟṟa māyiṟṟuk kāṇīr
kumpa ṉōṭu nikumpaṉum paṭṭāṉ
kūṟṟam maṉiṭa māyvantu tōṉṟi
ampi ṉālemmik koṉṟiṭu kiṉṟatu
añci ṉōmtaṭam poṅkattam poṅkō
[[((10.2.5))]]

$$1863

__&&1863__$$10.2.6##__

ōta mākaṭa likkaṭan tēṟi
uyarkkoḷ mākkaṭi kāvi yiṟuttu
kātal makkaḷum cuṟṟamuṅ koṉṟu
kaṭiyi laṅki malaṅka erittut
tūtu vanta kuraṅkukkē uṅkaḷ
tōṉṟal tēviyi viṭṭu koṭātē
ātar niṉṟu paṭukiṉṟa tantō.
añci ṉōmtaṭam poṅkattam poṅkō
[[((10.2.6))]]

$$1864

__&&1864__$$10.2.7##__

taḻa miṉṟimun nīriyañ ñāṉṟu
takinta tēkaṇṭu vañciñuṇ maruṅkul
māḻi māṉmaṭa nōkkiyi viṭṭu
vāḻki lāmati yilmaṉat tāṉi
ēḻi yiyilaṅ kikkiṟi taṉṉi
eṅka ḷiyoḻi yakkoli yavaṉi
cūḻa māniṉi māmaṇi vaṇṇā.
colli ṉōmtaṭam poṅkattam poṅkō
[[((10.2.7))]]

$$1865

__&&1865__$$10.2.8##__

maṉaṅkoṇ ṭēṟummaṇ ṭōtari mutalā
aṅka yaṟkaṇṇi ṉārkaḷ iruppa
taṉaṅkoḷ meṉmuli nōkka moḻintu
tañca mēcila tāpata reṉṟu
puṉaṅkoḷ meṉmayi licciṟi vitta
puṉmi yāḷaṉ neñ cilpuka eyta
aṉaṅka ṉaṉṉatiṇ ṭōḷemmi rāmaṟ
kañci ṉōmtaṭam poṅkattam poṅkō
[[((10.2.8))]]

$$1866

__&&1866__$$10.2.9##__

puraṅkaḷ mūṉṟumōr māttirip pōtil
poṅke rikkiri kaṇṭavaṉ ampiṉ
caraṅka ḷēkoṭi tāyaṭu kiṉṟa
cāmpa vāṉuṭaṉ niṟkat toḻutōm
iraṅku nīyemak kentipi rāṉē.
ilaṅku veṅkati rōṉṟaṉ ciṟuvā
kuraṅku kaṭkara cē.emmik kollēl.
kūṟi ṉōmtaṭam poṅkattam poṅkō
[[((10.2.9))]]

$$1867

__&&1867__$$10.2.10##__

aṅkav vāṉavark kākulam tīra
aṇiyi laṅki aḻittavaṉ ṟaṉṉi
poṅku māvala vaṉkali kaṉṟi
pukaṉṟa poṅkattaṅ koṇṭu,iv vulakil
eṅkum pāṭiniṉ ṟāṭumiṉ toṇṭīr.
immi yēyiṭa rilli, iṟantāl
taṅku mūraṇṭa mēkaṇṭu koṇmiṉ
cāṟṟi ṉōmtaṭam poṅkattam poṅkō.
[[((10.2.10))]]
aṟucīrk kaḻineṭilaṭi āciriya viruttam

1868__((PTM_10.3))

$$1868

__&&1868__$$10.3.1##__

ēttu kiṉṟōm nātta
ḻumpa irāmaṉ tirunāmam
cōttam nampī. cukki
rīvā. ummit toḻukiṉṟōm
vārtti pēcīr emmi
yuṅkaḷ vāṉaram kollāmē
kūttar pōla āṭu
kiṉṟōm kuḻamaṇi tūramē
[[((10.3.1))]]

$$1869

__&&1869__$$10.3.2##__

empi rāṉē. eṉṉi
yāḷvāy eṉṟeṉ ṟalaṟṟātē
ampiṉ vāyppaṭ ṭāṟṟa
killā tintira cittaḻintāṉ
nampi aṉumā. cukki
rīva. aṅkata ṉē.naḷaṉē
kumpa karṇaṉ paṭṭup
pōṉāṉ kuḻamaṇi tūramē
[[((10.3.2))]]

$$1870

__&&1870__$$10.3.3##__

ñāla māḷu muṅkaḷ
kōmāṉ eṅkaḷ iravaṇaṟkuk
kāla ṉāki vanta
vākaṇ ṭañcik karumukilpōl
nīlaṉ vāḻkacu ṭēṇaṉ
vāḻka aṅkataṉ vāḻkaveṉṟu
kōla māka āṭu
kiṉṟōm kuḻamaṇi tūramē
[[((10.3.3))]]

$$1871

__&&1871__$$10.3.4##__

maṇaṅkaḷ nāṟum vārkuḻa
lārkaḷ mātarka ḷātaratti
puṇarnta cintip puṉmi
yāḷaṉ poṉṟa variciliyāl
kaṇaṅka ḷuṇṇa vāḷi
yāṇṭa kāvala ṉukkiḷiyōṉ
kuṇaṅkaḷ pāṭi yāṭu
kiṉṟōm kuḻamaṇi tūramē
[[((10.3.4))]]

$$1872

__&&1872__$$10.3.5##__

veṉṟi tantōm māṉam
vēṇṭōm tāṉam emakkāka
iṉṟu tammi ṉeṅkaḷ
vāṇāḷ emperu māṉtamarkāḷ
niṉṟu kāṇīr kaṇka
ḷāra nīremik kollātē
kuṉṟu pōla āṭu
kiṉṟōm kuḻamaṇi tūramē
[[((10.3.5))]]

$$1873

__&&1873__$$10.3.6##__

kalliṉ munnīr māṟṟi
vantu kāval kaṭantu,ilaṅki
allal ceytā ṉuṅkaḷ
kōmāṉ emmi amarkkaḷattu
vella killā tañci
ṉōṅkāṇ veṅkati rōṉciṟuvā,
kolla vēṇṭā āṭu
kiṉṟōm kuḻamaṇi tūramē
[[((10.3.6))]]

$$1874

__&&1874__$$10.3.7##__

māṟṟa māva titta
ṉiyē vammiṉ arakkaruḷīr
cīṟṟam ñummēl tīra
vēṇṭil cēvakam pēcātē
āṟṟal cāṉṟa tolpi
ṟappil aṉumaṉi vāḻkaveṉṟu
kūṟṟa maṉṉār kāṇa
āṭīr kuḻamaṇi tūramē
[[((10.3.7))]]

$$1875

__&&1875__$$10.3.8##__

kavaḷa yāṉi pāypura
vittē rōṭa rakkarellām
tuvaḷa, veṉṟa veṉṟi
yāḷaṉ ṟaṉtamar kollāmē
tavaḷa māṭam nīṭa
yōtti kāvalaṉ ṟaṉciṟuvaṉ
kuvaḷi vaṇṇaṉ kāṇa
āṭīr kuḻamaṇi tūramē
[[((10.3.8))]]

$$1876

__&&1876__$$10.3.9##__

ēṭot tēntum nīḷi
livēl eṅkaḷ iravaṇaṉār
ōṭip pōṉār, nāṅkaḷ
eyttōm uyvatōr kāraṇattāl
cūṭip pōntōm uṅkaḷ
kōma ṉāṇi toṭarēṉmiṉ
kūṭi kūṭi yāṭu
kiṉṟōm kuḻamaṇi tūramē
[[((10.3.9))]]

$$1877

__&&1877__$$10.3.10##__

veṉṟa tolcīrt teṉṉi
laṅki veñcamattu aṉṟarakkar
kuṉṟa maṉṉā rāṭi
uynta kuḻamaṇi tūratti
kaṉṟi neynnīr niṉṟa
vēṟkik kaliya ṉolimāli
oṉṟum oṉṟum aintum
mūṉṟum paṭiniṉ ṟāṭumiṉē
[[((10.3.10))]]
aṟucīrk kaḻineṭilaṭi āciriya viruttam

1878__((PTM_10.4))

$$1878

__&&1878__$$10.4.1##__

canta malarkkuḻal tāḻat
tāṉukan tōṭit taṉiyē
vantu,eṉ mulittaṭan taṉṉi
vāṅkiniṉ vāyil maṭuttu,
nantaṉ peṟappeṟṟa nampī.
nāṉukan tuṇṇum amutē,
enti perumaṉē. uṇṇāy
eṉṉammam cēmamuṇ ṇāyē
[[((10.4.1))]]

$$1879

__&&1879__$$10.4.2##__

vaṅka maṟikaṭal vaṇṇā.
māmuki lēyokkum nampī
ceṅka ṇeṭiya tiruvē
ceṅkama lampuri vāyā,
koṅki curantiṭa vuṉṉik
kūviyum kāṇāti runtēṉ
eṅkirun tāyarka ḷōṭum
eṉviḷi yāṭukiṉ ṟāyē
[[((10.4.2))]]

$$1880

__&&1880__$$10.4.3##__

tiruvil polinta eḻilār
āyartam piḷḷika ḷōṭu
teruvil tiḷikkiṉṟa nampī
ceykiṉṟa tīmikaḷ kaṇṭiṭṭu,
urukiyeṉ koṅkiyiṉ tīmpāl
ōṭṭantu pāyntiṭu kiṉṟa,
maruvik kuṭaṅkā liruntu
vāymuli yuṇṇanī vārāy
[[((10.4.3))]]

$$1881

__&&1881__$$10.4.4##__

makkaḷ peṟutavam pōlum
viyattu vāḻum maṭavār
makkaḷ piṟarkaṇṇuk kokkum
mutalvā matakkaḷi ṟaṉṉāy
cekkar iḷampiṟi taṉṉi
vāṅkiniṉ kiyil taruvaṉ
okkali mēlirun tammam
ukantiṉi tuṇṇanī vārāy
[[((10.4.4))]]

$$1882

__&&1882__$$10.4.5##__

mitta karuṅkuñci mintā.
māmaru tūṭu naṭantāy,
vittaka ṉēviri yātē
veṇṇey viḻuṅkum vikirtā,
ittaṉi pōtaṉṟi yeṉṟaṉ
koṅki curantirukka killā,
uttama ṉē.ammam uṇṇāy
ulakaḷan tāy.ammam uṇṇāy
[[((10.4.5))]]

$$1883

__&&1883__$$10.4.6##__

piḷḷaykaḷ ceyvaṉa ceyyāy
pēciṉ peritum valiyi
kaḷḷam maṉatti luṭiyi
kāṇavē tīmikaḷ ceyti
uḷḷa murukiyeṉ koṅki
ōṭṭantu pāyntiṭu kiṉṟa
paḷḷik kuṟippuccey yātē
pālamu tuṇṇanī vārāy
[[((10.4.6))]]

$$1884

__&&1884__$$10.4.7##__

taṉmaka ṉākavaṉ pēycci
tāṉmuli yuṇṇak koṭukka
vaṉmaka ṉāyavaḷ āvi
vāṅki muliyuṇṭa nampi
naṉmakaḷ āymaka ḷōṭu
nāṉila maṅki maṇāḷā
eṉmaka ṉē.amma muṇṇāy
eṉṉammam cēmamuṇ ṇāyē
[[((10.4.7))]]

$$1885

__&&1885__$$10.4.8##__

untam aṭikaḷ muṉivar
uṉṉināṉ eṉkiyiṟ kōlāl
nontiṭa mōtavuṅ killēṉ
ñuṅkaḷtam āniri yellām
vantu pukutarum pōtu
vāṉiṭit teyvaṅkaḷ kāṇa
antiyam pōtaṅku nillēl
āḻiyaṅ kiyaṉē. vārāy
[[((10.4.8))]]

$$1886

__&&1886__$$10.4.9##__

peṟṟat talivaṉeṅ kōmāṉ
pēraru ḷāḷaṉ matalāy,
cuṟṟak kuḻāttiḷaṅ kōvē.
tōṉṟiya tolpuka ḻāḷā,
kaṟṟiṉan tōṟum maṟittuk
kāṉam tirinta kaḷiṟē
eṟṟukkeṉ ammamuṇ ṇātē
emperu māṉirun tāyē
[[((10.4.9))]]

$$1887

__&&1887__$$10.4.10##__

immi yiṭarkkeṭa vēṇṭi
ēnteḻil tōḷkali kaṉṟi
cemmip paṉuvalñūl koṇṭu
ceṅka ṇeṭiyavaṉ ṟaṉṉi
ammamuṇ eṉṟurik kiṉṟa
pāṭa liviyintu mintum
meymmi maṉattuvit tētta
viṉava rākalu māmē
[[((10.4.10))]]
kalittāḻici

1888__((PTM_10.5))

$$1888

__&&1888__$$10.5.1##__

pūṅkōti yāycci kaṭiveṇṇi pukkuṇṇa,
āṅkava ḷārttup puṭikkap puṭiyuṇṭu
ēṅki yiruntu ciṇuṅki viḷiyāṭum
ōṅkōta vaṇṇaṉē. cappāṇi
oḷimaṇi vaṇṇaṉē. cappāṇi (2).
[[((10.5.1))]]

$$1889

__&&1889__$$10.5.2##__

tāyar maṉaṅkaḷ taṭippat tayirneyyuṇ
ṭēyem pirākkaḷ irunilat teṅkaḷtam
āyar aḻaka aṭikaḷ aravinta
vāyava ṉē. koṭṭāy cappāṇi
mālvaṇṇa ṉē.koṭṭāy cappāṇi.
[[((10.5.2))]]

$$1890

__&&1890__$$10.5.3##__

tāmmōr uruṭṭit tayirney viḻuṅkiṭṭu
tāmō tavaḻvareṉ ṟāycciyar tāmpiṉāl
tāmō tirakkiyā lārkkat taḻumpirunta
tāmō tarā. koṭṭāy cappāṇi
tamarik kaṇṇaṉē. cappāṇi
[[((10.5.3))]]

$$1891

__&&1891__$$10.5.4##__

peṟṟār taḷikaḻalap pērntaṅ kayaliṭattu
uṟṟā roruvaru miṉṟi yulakiṉil,
maṟṟaru mañcappōy vañcappeṇ nañcuṇṭa
kaṟṟāya ṉē.koṭṭāy cappāṇi
kārvaṇṇa ṉē.koṭṭāy cappāṇi
[[((10.5.4))]]

$$1892

__&&1892__$$10.5.5##__

cōtteṉa niṉṉit toḻuvaṉ varantara,
pēycci muliyuṇṭa piḷḷāy, periyaṉa
āycciyar appam taruvar avarkkākac
cāṟṟiyō rāyiram cappāṇi
taṭaṅkika ḷālkoṭṭāy cappāṇi
[[((10.5.5))]]

$$1893

__&&1893__$$10.5.6##__

kēvala maṉṟuṉ vayiṟu, vayiṟṟukku
nāṉaval appam taruvaṉ karuviḷip
pūvalar nīḷmuṭi nantaṉṟaṉ pōrēṟē,
kōvala ṉē. koṭṭāy cappāṇi
kuṭamā ṭī.koṭṭāy cappāṇi.
[[((10.5.6))]]

$$1894

__&&1894__$$10.5.7##__

puḷḷiṉi vāypiḷantu pūṅkuruntam cāyttu,
tuḷḷi viḷayāṭit tūṅkuṟi veṇṇeyi,
aḷḷiya kiyā laṭiyēṉ mulineruṭum
piḷḷippi rāṉ. koṭṭāy cappāṇi
pēymuli yuṇṭāṉē. cappāṇi.
[[((10.5.7))]]

$$1895

__&&1895__$$10.5.8##__

yāyum piṟarum aṟiyāta yāmattu,
māya valavippeṇ vantu mulitara,
pēyeṉ ṟavaḷip piṭittuyi riyuṇṭa,
vāyava ṉē.koṭṭāy cappāṇi
mālvaṇṇa ṉē.koṭṭāy cappāṇi.
[[((10.5.8))]]

$$1896

__&&1896__$$10.5.9##__

kaḷḷak kuḻaviyāyk kālāl cakaṭatti
taḷḷi yutittiṭṭut tāyāy varuvāḷi,
meḷḷat toṭarntu piṭittā ruyiruṇṭa,
vaḷḷalē. koṭṭāy cappāṇi
mālvaṇṇa ṉē.koṭṭāy cappāṇi.
[[((10.5.9))]]

$$1897

__&&1897__$$10.5.10##__

kārār puyalkik kalikaṉṟi maṅkiyarkōṉ,
pērāḷaṉ neñcil piriyā tiṭaṅkoṇṭa
cīrāḷā, centā marikkaṇṇā. taṇṭuḻāyt
tārāḷā, koṭṭāy cappāṇi
taṭamārvā koṭṭāy cappāṇi.
[[((10.5.10))]]
eṇcīrk kaḻineṭilaṭi āciriya viruttam

1898__((PTM_10.6))

$$1898

__&&1898__$$10.6.1##__

eṅkāṉum ītoppa tōrmāya muṇṭē?
naranā raṇaṉā yulakat taṟañūl
ciṅkā mivirit tavaṉem perumāṉ
atuvaṉ ṟiyumceñ cuṭarum nilaṉum,
poṅkār kaṭalum poruppum neruppum
nerukkip pukapoṉ miṭaṟat taṉipōtu,
aṅkān tavaṉkāṇmiṉ iṉṟāyc ciyarāl
aḷiveṇṇe yuṇṭāp puṇṭirun tavaṉē (2)
[[((10.6.1))]]

$$1899

__&&1899__$$10.6.2##__

kuṉṟoṉṟu mattā aravam aḷavik
kurimā kaṭalik kaṭintiṭṭu, orukāl
niṉṟuṇṭi koṇṭōṭṭi vaṅkūṉ nimira
niṉitta perumāṉ atuvaṉ ṟiyummuṉ,
naṉṟuṇṭa tolcīr makarak kaṭalēḻ
maliyē ḻulakē ḻoḻiyā minampi,
aṉṟuṇ ṭavaṉkāṇmiṉ iṉṟāyc ciyarāl
aḷiveṇṇe yuṇṭāp puṇṭirun tavaṉē
[[((10.6.2))]]

$$1900

__&&1900__$$10.6.3##__

uḷintiṭ ṭeḻunta matuki ṭavarkaḷ
ulappil valiyāl avarpāl, vayiram
viḷintiṭṭa teṉṟeṇṇi viṇṇōr parava
avarnā ḷoḻitta perumāṉ muṉanāḷ,
vaḷintiṭṭa villāḷi valvā ḷeyiṟṟu
malipō lavuṇa ṉuṭalvaḷ ḷukirāl,
aḷintiṭ ṭavaṉkāṇmiṉ iṉṟāyc ciyarāl
aḷiveṇṇe yuṇṭāp puṇṭiruntavaṉē
[[((10.6.3))]]

$$1901

__&&1901__$$10.6.4##__

taḷarntiṭ ṭimiyōr caraṇtā veṉattāṉ
caraṇāy muraṇā yavaṉi ukirāl
piyaḷntiṭ ṭamarark karuḷcey tukanta
perumāṉ tirumāl virinī rulaki,
vaḷarntiṭṭa tolcīr viṟalmā valiyi
maṇkoḷḷa vañcit torumāṇ kuṟaḷāy
aḷantiṭ ṭavaṉkāṇmiṉ iṉṟāyc ciyarāl
aḷiveṇṇe yuṇṭāp puṇṭirun tavaṉē
[[((10.6.4))]]

$$1902

__&&1902__$$10.6.5##__

nīṇṭāṉ kuṟaḷāy neṭuvā ṉaḷavum
aṭiyār paṭumāḻ tuyarāya vellām,
tīṇṭā miniṉin timiyō raḷavum
celavit tapirāṉ atuvaṉ ṟiyummuṉ,
vēṇṭā minamaṉ ṟamareṉ tamari
viṉavap peṟuvār alar,eṉṟu, ulakēḻ
āṇṭā ṉavaṉkāṇmiṉ iṉṟāyc ciyarāl
aḷiveṇṇe yuṇṭāp puṇṭiruntavaṉē
[[((10.6.5))]]

$$1903

__&&1903__$$10.6.6##__

paḻittiṭṭa iṉpap payaṉpaṟ ṟaṟuttup
paṇintētta vallār tuyarāya vellām,
oḻittiṭ ṭavarit taṉakkākka valla
perumāṉ tirumā latuvaṉ ṟiyummuṉ,
teḻittiṭ ṭeḻuntē etirniṉṟu maṉṉaṉ
ciṉattōḷ aviyā yiramum maḻuvāl
aḻittiṭ ṭavaṉkāṇmiṉ iṉṟāyc ciyarāl
aḷiveṇṇe yuṇṭāp puṇṭiruntavaṉē
[[((10.6.6))]]

$$1904

__&&1904__$$10.6.7##__

paṭittiṭṭa tivviya muyya muṉanāḷ
paṇintētta vallār tuyarāya vellām,
tuṭittiṭ ṭavarit taṉakkākka veṉṉat
teḷiyā arakkar tiṟalapōy aviya,
miṭittiṭ ṭeḻunta kuraṅkip paṭiyā
vilaṅkal pukappāycci vimma, kaṭali
aṭittiṭ ṭavaṉkāṇmiṉ iṉṟāyc ciyarāl
aḷiveṇṇe yuṇṭāp puṇṭiruntavaṉē
[[((10.6.7))]]

$$1905

__&&1905__$$10.6.8##__

neṟittiṭṭa meṉkūḻi naṉṉē riḻiyō
ṭuṭaṉāya villeṉṉa vallē yataṉi,
iṟuttiṭ ṭavaḷiṉpa maṉpō ṭaṇintiṭa
ṭiḷaṅkoṟ ṟavaṉāyt tuḷaṅkāta munnīr,
ceṟittiṭ ṭilaṅki malaṅka arakkaṉ
ceḻunīṇ muṭitō ḷoṭutāḷ tuṇiya,
aṟuttiṭ ṭavaṉkāṇmiṉ iṉṟāyc ciyarāl
aḷiveṇṇe yuṇṭāp puṇṭiruntavaṉē
[[((10.6.8))]]

$$1906

__&&1906__$$10.6.9##__

curintiṭṭa ceṅkēḻ uḷippoṅ karimāt
toliyap piriyātu ceṉṟeyti, eytā
tirintiṭ ṭiṭaṅkoṇ ṭaṭaṅkāta taṉvāy
irukūṟu ceyta perumāṉ muṉanāḷ
varintiṭṭa villāl maramēḻu meytu
malipō luruvat torirāk katimūkku,
arintiṭ ṭavaṉkāṇmiṉ iṉṟāyc ciyarāl
aḷiveṇṇe yuṇṭāp puṇṭiruntavaṉē
[[((10.6.9))]]

$$1907

__&&1907__$$10.6.10##__

niṉṟār mukappuc ciṟitum niṉiyāṉ
vayiṟṟi niṟippā ṉuṟippāl tayirney,
aṉṟāyc ciyarveṇṇey viḻuṅki yuralō
ṭāppuṇ ṭirunta perumāṉ aṭimēl,
naṉṟāya tolcīr vayalmaṅ kiyarkōṉ
kaliya ṉolicey tamiḻmāli vallār,
eṉṟāṉum eytā riṭariṉpa meyti
imiyōrkku mappāl celavey tuvārē
[[((10.6.10))]]
eḻucīr kaḻineṭilaṭi āciriya viruttam

1908__((PTM_10.7))

$$1908

__&&1908__$$10.7.1##__

māṉa muṭittuṅka ḷāyar kulamata
ṉālpiṟar makkaḷ tammi
ūṉa muṭiyaṉa ceyyap peṟāyeṉ
ṟirappa ṉurappa killēṉ
nāṉu murittilēṉ nantaṉ paṇittilaṉ
naṅkikāḷ. nāṉeṉ ceykēṉ?
tāṉumōr kaṉṉiyum kīḻi yakattut
tayirkaṭi kiṉṟāṉ pōlum.
[[((10.7.1))]]

$$1909

__&&1909__$$10.7.2##__

kāli yeḻuntu kaṭintavim mōrviṟkap
pōkiṉṟēṉ kaṇṭē pōṉēṉ,
māli naṟuṅkuñci nantaṉ makaṉallāl
maṟṟuvan tāru milli,
mēli yakattunaṅ kāy. vantu kāṇmiṉkaḷ
veṇṇe yēyaṉṟu, irunta
pālum patiṉkuṭam kaṇṭilēṉ pāviyēṉ
eṉceykēṉ eṉcey kēṉō.
[[((10.7.2))]]

$$1910

__&&1910__$$10.7.3##__

teḷḷiya vāycciṟi yāṉnaṅki kāḷ. uṟi
mēlit taṭāni ṟinta,
veḷḷi maliyirun tālotta veṇṇeyi
vāri viḻuṅki yiṭṭu,
kaḷva ṉuṟaṅkukiṉ ṟāṉvantu kāṇmiṉkaḷ
kiyel lāmney, vayiṟu
piḷḷi paramaṉṟuiv vēḻula kumkoḷḷum
pētiyēṉ eṉcey kēṉō.
[[((10.7.3))]]

$$1911

__&&1911__$$10.7.4##__

minnampu vēlkaṇnal lāḷmuṉṉam peṟṟa
vaḷivaṇṇa naṉmā mēṉi,
taṉnampi nampiyu miṅku vaḷarntatu
avaṉi vicey taṟiyāṉ
poynnampi puḷḷuvaṉ kaḷvam potiyaṟi
pōkiṉṟa vātavaḻn tiṭṭu,
innampi nampiyā āycciyark kuyvilli
eṉceykēṉ eṉcey kēṉō.
[[((10.7.4))]]

$$1912

__&&1912__$$10.7.5##__

tanti pukuntilaṉ nāṉiṅki runtilēṉ
tōḻimā rāru milli,
canta malarkkuḻa lāḷtaṉi yēviḷi
yāṭu miṭamku ṟuki,
pantu paṟittut tukilpaṟṟik kīṟip
paṭiṟaṉ paṭiṟu ceyyum,
nantaṉ matalikkiṅ keṉkaṭa vōm?naṅkāy.
eṉceykēṉ eṉcey kēṉō.
[[((10.7.5))]]

$$1913

__&&1913__$$10.7.6##__

maṇmakaḷ kēḷvaṉ malarmaṅki nāyakaṉ
nantaṉ peṟṟa matali,
aṇṇal ilikkuḻa lūtinam cērikkē
alliṟ ṟāṉvanta piṉṉi,
kaṇmalar cōrntu mulivantu vimmik
kamalac cevvāyve ḷuppa,
eṉmakaḷ vaṇṇa mirukkiṉṟa vānaṅkāy.
eṉceykēṉ eṉcey kēṉō.
[[((10.7.6))]]

$$1914

__&&1914__$$10.7.7##__

āyiram kaṇṇuṭi intira ṉārukkeṉ
ṟāyar viḻave ṭuppa,
pācaṉam nallaṉa paṇṭika ḷālpukap
peyta ataṉi yellām,
pōyirun taṅkoru pūta vaṭivukoṇ
ṭuṉmaka ṉiṉṟu naṅkāy,
māyaṉ ataṉiyel lāmmuṟṟa vāri
vaḷittuṇ ṭiruntāṉ pōlum.
[[((10.7.7))]]

$$1915

__&&1915__$$10.7.8##__

tōytta tayirum naṟuneyyum pālumōr
ōrkkuṭaṉ tuṟṟiṭu meṉṟu,
āycciyar kūṭi yaḻikkavum nāṉitaṟ
keḷki yivaṉi naṅkāy
cōttam pirāṉ. ivi ceyyap peṟāy. eṉ
ṟirappaṉ urappa killēṉ,
pēycci muliyuṇṭa piṉṉiyip piḷḷiyip
pēcuva tañcu vēṉē.
[[((10.7.8))]]

$$1916

__&&1916__$$10.7.9##__

īṭum valiyum uṭiyavin nampi
piṟanta ēḻu tiṅkaḷil,
āṭalar kaṇṇiyi ṉāṉi vaḷartti
yamuṉi nīrāṭap pōṉēṉ,
cēṭaṉ tirumaṟu mārvaṉ kiṭantu
tiruvaṭi yāl,mali pōla
ōṭum cakaṭattic cāṭiya piṉṉi
urappuva tañcu vēṉē.
[[((10.7.9))]]

$$1917

__&&1917__$$10.7.10##__

añcuvaṉ colli yaḻittiṭa naṅkikāḷ.
āyira nāḻi neyyip,
pañciyal mellaṭip piḷḷika ḷuṇkiṉṟa
pākan tāṉvi yārkaḷē,
kañcaṉ kaṭiyaṉ kaṟaveṭṭu nāḷil
eṉki valattātu milli,
neñcat tiruppaṉa ceytuvit tāynampī .
eṉceykē ṉeṉcey kēṉō .
[[((10.7.10))]]

$$1918

__&&1918__$$10.7.11##__

aṅṅaṉam tīmikaḷ ceyvarka ḷōnampī.
āyar maṭamak kaḷip,
paṅkaya nīrkuṭin tāṭukiṉ ṟārkaḷ
piṉṉē ceṉṟoḷit tiruntu,
aṅkavar pūntukil vārikkoṇ ṭiṭṭara
vēri ṭiyā rirappa,
maṅkinal līr.vantu koṇmiṉ eṉṟumaram
ēṟi yiruntāy pōlum
[[((10.7.11))]]

$$1919

__&&1919__$$10.7.12##__

accam tiṉittaṉi yilliyip piḷḷik
kāṇmi yumcē vakamum,
ucciyil mutti vaḷartteṭut tēṉuk
kurittilaṉ tāṉiṉ ṟupōy,
paccilip pūṅkaṭam pēṟi vicikoṇṭu
pāyntu pukku,ā yiravāy
naccaḻal poykiyil nākatti ṉōṭu
piṇaṅkinī vantāy pōlum.
[[((10.7.12))]]

$$1920

__&&1920__$$10.7.13##__

tampara mallaṉ āṇmika ḷittaṉi
yēniṉṟu tāmcey vārō?,
emperu māṉ. uṉṉip peṟṟa vayiṟuṭi
yēṉiṉi yāṉeṉ ceykēṉ?,
ampara mēḻum atirum iṭikural
aṅkaṉaṟ ceṅka ṇuṭi,
vampaviḻ kāṉattu mālviṭi yōṭu
piṇaṅkinī vantāy pōlum.
[[((10.7.13))]]

$$1921

__&&1921__$$10.7.14##__

aṉṉa naṭimaṭa āycci vayiṟaṭit
tañca aruvari pōla,
maṉṉu karuṅkaḷiṟ ṟāruyir vavviya
mintaṉi māka ṭalcūḻu,
kaṉṉinaṉ māmatiḷ maṅkiyar kāvalaṉ
kāmaru cīrkkali kaṉṟi
iṉṉici mālika ḷīrēḻum vallavark
kētu miṭaril liyē. (2)
[[((10.7.14))]]
kaliviruttam

1922__((PTM_10.8))

$$1922

__&&1922__$$10.8.1##__

kātil kaṭippiṭuk kaliṅka muṭuttu,
tātunal lataṇṇan tuḻāyko ṭaṇintu,
pōtu maṟuttup puṟamēvan tuniṉṟīr,
ētukkitu eṉṉitu eṉṉitu eṉṉō. (2)
[[((10.8.1))]]

$$1923

__&&1923__$$10.8.2##__

tuvarā ṭiyuṭut toruceṇṭu ciluppi,
kavarāka muṭittuk kalikkaccuk kaṭṭi,
cuvarār kataviṉ puṟamēvantu niṉṟīr,
ivarā rituveṉ ṉituveṉ ṉituveṉṉō.
[[((10.8.2))]]

$$1924

__&&1924__$$10.8.3##__

karuḷak koṭiyoṉ ṟuṭiyīr. taṉippākīr,
uruḷac cakaṭama tuṟakkil nimirttīr,
maruḷik koṭupāṭi vantillam pukuntīr,
iruḷat tituveṉ ṉituveṉ ṉituveṉṉō.
[[((10.8.3))]]

$$1925

__&&1925__$$10.8.4##__

nāmam palavu muṭinā raṇanampī,
tāmat tuḷavam mikanā ṟiṭukiṉṟīr,
kāma ṉeṉappāṭi ventillam pukuntīr,
ēmat titu veṉ ṉituveṉ ṉituveṉṉō.
[[((10.8.4))]]

$$1926

__&&1926__$$10.8.5##__

cuṟṟum kuḻaltāḻac curiki yaṇittu,
maṟṟum palamāmaṇi poṉko ṭaṇintu,
muṟṟam pukuntu muṟuvalceytu niṉṟīr,
eṟṟuk kituveṉ ṉituveṉ ṉituveṉṉō.
[[((10.8.5))]]

$$1927

__&&1927__$$10.8.6##__

āṉā yarumā ṉiriyumaṅ koḻiyak,
kūṉāya tōrkoṟṟa villoṉṟu kiyēntip,
pōṉā riruntā riyumpārttup pukutīr,
ēṉōrkaḷ muṉṉeṉ ṉituveṉ ṉituveṉṉō.
[[((10.8.6))]]

$$1928

__&&1928__$$10.8.7##__

mallē poruta tiraḷtōl maṇavāḷīr,
allē yaṟintōmñum maṉattiṉ karutti,
collā toḻiyīr coṉṉapō tiṉālvārīr
ellē yituveṉ ṉituveṉ ṉituveṉṉō.
[[((10.8.7))]]

$$1929

__&&1929__$$10.8.8##__

pukkā ṭaravam piṭittāṭṭum puṉitīr,
ikkālaṅkaḷ yāmumak kētoṉṟu mallōm,
takkār palarttēvi mārcāla vuṭiyīr,
ekkē. ituveṉ ṉituveṉ ṉituveṉṉō.
[[((10.8.8))]]

$$1930

__&&1930__$$10.8.9##__

āṭi yacintāy maṭavā roṭunīpōyk
kūṭik kuravi piṇikō maḷappiḷḷāy,
tēṭit tirumā makaḷmaṇ makaḷniṟpa,
ēṭi. ituveṉ ṉituveṉ ṉituveṉṉō.
[[((10.8.9))]]

$$1931

__&&1931__$$10.8.10##__

allik kamalak kaṇṇaṉi aṅkorāycci
ellip poḻutū ṭiyavūṭal tiṟatti,
kalliṉ malitōḷ kaliyaṉ coṉṉa māli,
collit tutippā ravartukka milarē (2)
[[((10.8.10))]]
eḻucīrk kaḻineṭilaṭi āciriya viruttam

1932__((PTM_10.9))

$$1932

__&&1932__$$10.9.1##__

puḷḷuru vāki naḷḷiruḷ vanta
pūtaṉi māḷa, ilaṅki
oḷḷeri maṇṭi yuṇṇap paṇitta
ūkka mataṉi niṉintō,
kaḷḷaviḻ kōti kātalu meṅkaḷ
kāriki mātar karuttum,
piḷḷitaṉ kiyil kiṇṇamē yokkap
pēcuva tenti pirāṉē. (2)
[[((10.9.1))]]

$$1933

__&&1933__$$10.9.2##__

maṉṟil malintu kūttuvan tāṭi
mālviṭi yēḻuma ṭarttu, āyar
aṉṟu naṭuṅka āṉiri kātta
āṇmiko lōvaṟi yēṉ nāṉ,
niṉṟa pirāṉē. nīḷkaṭal vaṇṇā.
nīyivaḷ taṉṉi niṉ kōyil,
muṉṟi leḻunta muruṅkiyil tēṉā
muṉki vaḷikavarn tāyē.
[[((10.9.2))]]

$$1934

__&&1934__$$10.9.3##__

ārmali yāḻi caṅkoṭu paṟṟi
āṟṟali yāṟṟal mikuttuk,
kārmukil vaṇṇā. kaṉcaṉi muṉṉam
kaṭantaniṉ kaṭuntiṟal tāṉō,
nēriḻi māti nittilat totti
neṭuṅkaṭal amutaṉi yāḷi,
āreḻil vaṇṇā. aṅkiyil vaṭṭām
ivaḷeṉak karutukiṉ ṟāyē.
[[((10.9.3))]]

$$1935

__&&1935__$$10.9.4##__

malkiya tōḷum māṉuri yataḷum
uṭiyavar tamakkumōr pākam,
nalkiya nalamō? narakaṉi tolitta
karatalat tamitiyiṉ karuttō?
alliyaṅ kōti yaṇiniṟam koṇṭu
vantumuṉ ṉēniṉṟu pōkāy,
colliyeṉ nampī. ivaḷinī yuṅkaḷ
toṇṭarkit taṇṭeṉṟa vāṟē
[[((10.9.4))]]

$$1936

__&&1936__$$10.9.5##__

ceruvaḻi yāta maṉṉarkaḷ māḷat
tērvalaṅ koṇṭavar cellum,
aruvaḻi vāṉam atarpaṭak kaṇṭa
āṇmiko lōvaṟi yēṉnāṉ,
tirumoḻi yeṅkaḷ tēmalark kōti
cīrmiyi niṉintili yantō,
peruvaḻi nāvaṟ kaṉiyiṉu meḷiyaḷ
ivaḷeṉap pēcukiṉ ṟāyē
[[((10.9.5))]]

$$1937

__&&1937__$$10.9.6##__

arakkiya rākam pulleṉa villāl
aṇimati ḷilaṅkiyar kōṉi,
cerukkaḻit tamarar paṇiyamuṉ ṉiṉṟa
cēvaka mōceyta tiṉṟu
murukkitaḻ vāycci muṉkiveṇ caṅkam
koṇṭumuṉ ṉēniṉṟu pōkāy,
erukkilik kāka eṟimaḻu vōccal
eṉceyva tenti pirāṉē.
[[((10.9.6))]]

$$1938

__&&1938__$$10.9.7##__

āḻiyan tiṇṭēr aracarvan tiṟiñca
alikaṭal ulakammuṉ āṇṭa,
pāḻiyan tōḷō rāyiram vīḻap
paṭimaḻup paṟṟiya valiyō?
māḻimeṉ ṉōkki maṇiniṟaṅ koṇṭu
vantumuṉ ṉēniṉṟu pōkāy,
kōḻiveṇ muṭṭik keṉceyva tentāy.
kuṟuntaṭi neṭuṅkaṭal vaṇṇā.
[[((10.9.7))]]

$$1939

__&&1939__$$10.9.8##__

poruntalaṉ ākam puḷḷuvan tēṟa
vaḷḷuki rālpiḷantu, aṉṟu
peruntakik kiraṅki vāliyi muṉinta
perumiko lōceyta tiṉṟu,
peruntaṭaṅ kaṇṇi curumpuṟu kōti
perumiyi niṉintili pēcil,
karuṅkaṭal vaṇṇā. kavuḷkoṇṭa nīrām
ivaḷeṉak karutukiṉ ṟāyē
[[((10.9.8))]]

$$1940

__&&1940__$$10.9.9##__

nīraḻal vāṉāy neṭunilaṅ kālāy
niṉṟaniṉ nīrmiyi niṉintō?
cīrkkeḻu kōti yeṉṉala tilaḷeṉ
ṟaṉṉatōr tēṟṟaṉmi tāṉō?
pārkkeḻu pavvat tāramu taṉiya
pāviyip pāvamcey tēṉukku,
āraḻa lōmpum antaṇaṉ tōṭṭa
mākaniṉ maṉattuvit tāyē
[[((10.9.9))]]

$$1941

__&&1941__$$10.9.10##__

vēṭṭattik karutā taṭiyiṇi vaṇaṅki
meymminiṉ ṟemperu māṉi,
vāṭṭiṟal tāṉi maṅkiyar talivaṉ
māṉavēl kaliyaṉvā yolikaḷ,
tōṭṭalar pintārc cuṭarmuṭi yāṉip
paḻamoḻi yālpaṇin turitta,
pāṭṭivi pāṭap pattimi perukic
cittamum tiruvōṭu mikumē (2)
[[((10.9.10))]]
veṇṭuṟi

tiruttāy cempōttē.

collāy piṅkiḷiyē.

__&&1947__$$10.10.6##__

kōḻi kūveṉṉumāl,

kōḻi kūveṉṉumāl.

$$1948

__&&1948__$$10.10.7##__

kāmaṟ keṉkaṭavēṉ,

pūmē liṅkaṇi kōttup pukunteyyak,
kāmaṟ keṉkaṭavēṉ.

__&&1949__$$10.10.8##__

iṅkē pōtuṅkolō,

iṅkē pōtuṅkolō.

$$1950

__&&1950__$$10.10.9##__

iṉṉā reṉṟaṟiyēṉ,

__&&1951__$$10.10.10##__

toṇṭīr. pāṭumiṉō,
curumpārppoḻil maṅkiyarkōṉ,

toṇṭīr. pāṭumiṉō (2)

1952__((PTM_11.1))

$$1952

__&&1952__$$11.1.1##__

kuṉṟa moṉṟeṭut tēnti, māmaḻi
aṉṟu kāttavam māṉ,a rakkari
veṉṟa villiyār vīra mēkolō,
teṉṟal vantutī vīcu meṉceykēṉ. (2)
[[((11.1.1))]]

$$1953

__&&1953__$$11.1.2##__

kārum vārpaṉik kaṭalum aṉṉavaṉ,
tāru mārvamum kaṇṭa taṇṭamō,
cōru māmukil tuḷiyi ṉūṭuvantu
īra vāṭitāṉ īru meṉṉiyē.
[[((11.1.2))]]

$$1954

__&&1954__$$11.1.3##__

caṅku māmiyum taḷaru mēṉimēl,
tiṅkaḷ veṅkatir cīṟu meṉceykēṉ,
poṅku veṇṭirip puṇari vaṇṇaṉār,
koṅka lārntatār kūvu meṉṉiyē.
[[((11.1.3))]]

$$1955

__&&1955__$$11.1.4##__

aṅko rāykkulat tuḷva ḷarntuceṉṟu,
aṅkor tāyuru vāki vantavaḷ,
koṅki naṉcuṇṭa kōyiṉ mikolō,
tiṅkaḷ veṅkatir cīṟu kiṉṟatē
[[((11.1.4))]]

$$1956

__&&1956__$$11.1.5##__

aṅko rāḷari yāy,a vuṇaṉip
paṅka māviru kūṟu ceytavaṉ,
maṅkul māmati vāṅka vēkolō
poṅku mākaṭal pulampu kiṉṟatē.
[[((11.1.5))]]

$$1957

__&&1957__$$11.1.6##__

ceṉṟu vārcili vaḷittui laṅkiyi,
veṉṟa villiyār vīra mēkolō,
muṉṟil peṇṇimēl muḷarik kūṭṭakattu,
aṉṟi liṉkural aṭaru meṉṉiyē.
[[((11.1.6))]]

$$1958

__&&1958__$$11.1.7##__

pūvi vaṇṇaṉār puḷḷiṉ mēlvara,
mēvi niṉṟunāṉ kaṇṭa taṇṭamō,
vīvi liṅkaṇi villi yampukōttu,
āvi yēyilak kāka eyvatē.
[[((11.1.7))]]

$$1959

__&&1959__$$11.1.8##__

māli ṉantuḻāy varumeṉ ṉeñcakam,
māliṉ antuḻāy vanteṉ ṉuḷpuka,
kōla vāṭiyum koṇṭu vantatu,ōr
āli vantatāl aritu kāvalē.
[[((11.1.8))]]

$$1960

__&&1960__$$11.1.9##__

koṇṭi yoṇkaṇum tuyilum, eṉniṟam
paṇṭu paṇṭupō lokkum, mikkacīrt
toṇṭa riṭṭapūn tuḷaviṉ vācamē,
vaṇṭu koṇṭuvan tūtu mākilē
[[((11.1.9))]]

$$1961

__&&1961__$$11.1.10##__

aṉṟu pāratat tivar tūtaṉāy,
ceṉṟa māyaṉic ceṅkaṇ māliṉi,
maṉṟi lārppukaḻ maṅki vāḷkali
kaṉṟi, colvallārk kalla lilliyē (2)
[[((11.1.10))]]
kali nilittuṟi

1962__((PTM_11.2))

$$1962

__&&1962__$$11.2.1##__

kuṉṟa meṭuttu maḻita
ṭuttuiḷi yāroṭum
maṉṟil kuravi yiṇinta
māleṉṉi mālceytāṉ,
muṉṟil taṉiniṉṟa peṇṇi
mēlkiṭan tīrkiṉṟa
aṉṟiliṉ kūṭṭip pirikka
kiṟpava rārkolō. (2)
[[((11.2.1))]]

$$1963

__&&1963__$$11.2.2##__

pūṅku runtocittu āṉi
kāyntari māccekuttu,
āṅku vēḻattiṉ kompu
koṇṭuvaṉ pēymuli
vāṅki yuṇṭa,av vāyaṉ
niṟkiv vāyaṉvāy,
ēṅku vēyṅkuḻal eṉṉō
ṭāṭum iḷamiyē.
[[((11.2.2))]]

$$1964

__&&1964__$$11.2.3##__

mallōṭu kañcaṉum tuñca
veṉṟa maṇivaṇṇaṉ,
alli malarttaṇ ṭuḻāyni
ṉintirun tēṉiyē,
elli yiṉmā rutamvan
taṭumatu vaṉṟiyum,
kollival lēṟṟiṉ maṇiyum
kōyiṉmi ceyyumē.
[[((11.2.3))]]

$$1965

__&&1965__$$11.2.4##__

poruntu māmara mēḻu
ceyta puṉitaṉār
tiruntu cēvaṭi yeṉma
ṉattu niṉitoṟum,
karuntaṇ mākaṭal maṅku
lārkkum atuvaṉṟiyum,
varunta vāṭi varumi
taṟkiṉi yeṉceykēṉ.
[[((11.2.4))]]

$$1966

__&&1966__$$11.2.5##__

aṉṉi muṉivatum aṉṟi
liṉ kura līrvatum,
maṉṉu maṟikaṭa lārppa
tumvaḷi cōrvatum,
poṉṉaṅ kaliyalku laṉṉa
meṉṉaṭip pūṅkuḻal,
piṉṉi maṇāḷar tiṟatta
vāyiṉa piṉṉiyē
[[((11.2.5))]]

$$1967

__&&1967__$$11.2.6##__

āḻiyum caṅku muṭiya
naṅkaḷ aṭikaḷtām,
pāḻimi yāṉa kaṉavil
nammip pakarvittār,
tōḻiyum nāṉu moḻiya
viyam tuyiṉṟatu,
kōḻiyum kūkiṉṟa tillik
kūriru ḷāyiṟṟē.
[[((11.2.6))]]

$$1968

__&&1968__$$11.2.7##__

kāmaṉ ṟaṉakku muriyal
lēṉkaṭal vaṇṇaṉār,
māmaṇa vāḷa reṉakkut
tāṉum makaṉcollil,
yāmaṅkaḷ tōṟeri vīcu
meṉṉiḷaṅ koṅkikaḷ,
māmaṇi vaṇṇar tiṟatta
vāyvaḷar kiṉṟavē.
[[((11.2.7))]]

$$1969

__&&1969__$$11.2.8##__

maṉcuṟu māliruñ cōli
niṉṟa maṇāḷaṉār,
neñcam niṟikoṇṭu pōyi
ṉārniṉi kiṉṟilar,
veñcuṭar pōyviṭi yāmal
evviṭam pukkatō,
nañcu uṭalam tuyiṉṟāl
namakkiṉi nallatē.
[[((11.2.8))]]

$$1970

__&&1970__$$11.2.9##__

kāmaṉ kaṇikkō rilakka
māynalat tilmiku,
pūmaru kōlanam peṇmi
cintitti rātupōy
tūmalar nīrkoṭu tōḻi.
nāmtoḻu tēttiṉāl
kārmukil vaṇṇarik kaṇka
ḷālkāṇa lāṅkolō.
[[((11.2.9))]]

$$1971

__&&1971__$$11.2.10##__

veṉṟi viṭiyuṭa ṉēḻa
ṭartta aṭikaḷi,
maṉṟil malipukaḻ maṅki
maṉkali kaṉṟicol,
oṉṟu niṉṟavoṉ patumu
rippavar taṅkaḷmēl
eṉṟum nillāviṉi yoṉṟum
colli lulakilē (2)
[[((11.2.10))]]
taravu koccakak kalippā

1972__((PTM_11.3))

$$1972

__&&1972__$$11.3.1##__

maṉṉilaṅku pāratattut tērūrntu, māvaliyip
poṉṉilaṅku tiṇvilaṅkil vittup porukaṭalcūḻ
teṉṉilaṅki yīṭaḻitta tēvark kitukāṇīr
eṉṉilaṅku caṅkō ṭeḻiltōṟ ṟiruntēṉē. (2)
[[((11.3.1))]]

$$1973

__&&1973__$$11.3.2##__

iruntāṉe ṉuḷḷat tiṟivaṉ, kaṟicēr
paruntāḷ kaḷiṟṟuk karuḷceyta, ceṅkaṇ
peruntōḷ neṭumālip pērpāṭi yāṭa
varuntāteṉ koṅki yoḷimaṉṉum aṉṉē.
[[((11.3.2))]]

$$1974

__&&1974__$$11.3.3##__

aṉṉē. ivari yaṟivaṉ, maṟināṉkum
muṉṉē yuritta muṉiva rivarvantu
poṉṉēy vaḷikavarntu pōkār maṉampukuntu
eṉṉē yivareṇṇum eṇṇam aṟiyōmē.
[[((11.3.3))]]

$$1975

__&&1975__$$11.3.4##__

aṟiyōmē yeṉṟurikka lāmē emakku,
veṟiyār poḻilcūḻ viyaṉkuṭanti mēvi,
ciṟiyāṉōr piḷḷiyāy meḷḷa naṭantiṭṭu
uṟiyār naṟuveṇṇe yuṇṭukantār tammiyē?
[[((11.3.4))]]

$$1976

__&&1976__$$11.3.5##__

tammiyē nāḷum vaṇaṅkit toḻuvārkku,
tammiyē yokka aruḷceyva rātalāl,
tammiyē nāḷum vaṇaṅkit toḻutiṟiñci,
tammiyē paṟṟā maṉatteṉṟum vittōmē.
[[((11.3.5))]]

$$1977

__&&1977__$$11.3.6##__

vittā raṭiyār maṉattiṉil vittu, iṉpam
uṟṟā roḷivicumpi lōraṭivittu, ōraṭikkum
eyttātu maṇṇeṉ ṟimiyōr toḻutiṟiñci,
kittā marikuvikkum kaṇṇaṉeṉ kaṇṇaṉiyē
[[((11.3.6))]]

$$1978

__&&1978__$$11.3.7##__

kaṇṇaṉ maṉattuḷḷē niṟkavum, kivaḷikaḷ
eṉṉō kaḻaṉṟa? iviyeṉṉa māyaṅkaḷ?
peṇṇāṉōm peṇmiyōm niṟka, avaṉmēya,
aṇṇal maliyum araṅkamum pāṭōmē.
[[((11.3.7))]]

$$1979

__&&1979__$$11.3.8##__

pāṭōmē yenti perumāṉi? pāṭiniṉṟu
āṭōmē yāyiram pērāṉi? pērniṉintu
cūṭōmē cūṭum tuḻāyalaṅkal? cūṭi,nām
kūṭōmē kūṭak kuṟippākil? naṉṉeñcē.
[[((11.3.8))]]

$$1980

__&&1980__$$11.3.9##__

naṉṉeñcē. namperumāṉ nāḷum iṉitamarum,

muṉṉamcēr valviṉikaḷ pōka mukilvaṇṇaṉ,

peṟṟārār āyiram pērāṉip, pērpāṭap

luṟṟārkku, uṟutuya rilli yulakattē (2)

1982__((PTM_11.4))

$$1982

__&&1982__$$11.4.1##__

niliyiṭa meṅku miṉṟi neṭuveḷḷam
umpar vaḷanāṭu mūṭa imiyōr
taliyiṭa maṟṟe makkor caraṇilli
eṉṉa araṇāva ṉeṉṉu maruḷāl
alikaṭal nīrkku ḻampa akaṭāṭa
oṭi yakalvā ṉuriñca, mutukil
malikaḷi mītu koṇṭu varumīṉi
māli maṟavā tiṟiñceṉ maṉaṉē. (2)
[[((11.4.1))]]

$$1983

__&&1983__$$11.4.2##__

cerumiku vāḷe yiṟṟa aravoṉṟu
cuṟṟit ticimaṇṇum viṇṇu muṭaṉē
veruvara veḷḷi veḷḷam muḻutum
kuḻampa imiyōrkaḷ niṉṟu kaṭiya,
paruvari yoṉṟu niṉṟu mutukiṟ
parantu cuḻalak kiṭantu tuyilum,
aruvari yaṉṉa taṉmi aṭalāmi
yāṉa tirumāl namakko raraṇē.
[[((11.4.2))]]

$$1984

__&&1984__$$11.4.3##__

tītaṟu tiṅkaḷ poṅku cuṭarumpar
umpa rulakēḻi ṉōṭu muṭaṉē,
mātira maṇcu mantu vaṭakuṉṟu
niṉṟa maliyāṟum ēḻu kaṭalum
pātamar cūḻku ḷampi ṉakamaṇṭa
latti ṉorupā loṭuṅka vaḷarcēr,
ātimuṉ ēṉa māki araṇāya
mūrtti atuṉammi yāḷu maracē.
[[((11.4.3))]]

$$1985

__&&1985__$$11.4.4##__

taḷiyaviḻ kōti māli yirupāl
tayaṅka erikāṉ ṟiraṇṭu taṟukaṇ,
aḷaveḻa vemmi mikka ariyāki
aṉṟu pariyōṉ ciṉaṅka ḷaviḻa,
vaḷiyuki rāḷi moympil maṟavōṉa
tākam matiyātu ceṉṟo rukirāl
piḷaveḻa viṭṭa kuṭṭa matuviya
mūṭu perunīril mummi peritē.
[[((11.4.4))]]

$$1986

__&&1986__$$11.4.5##__

ventiṟal vāṇaṉ vēḷvi yiṭameyti
aṅkōr kuṟaḷāki meymmi yuṇara
centoḻil vēta nāviṉ muṉiyāki
viya muṭimūṉ ṟirantu peṟiṉum,
mantira mītu pōki matiniṉṟi
ṟiñca malarōṉ vaṇaṅka vaḷarcēr,
antara mēḻi ṉūṭu celavuytta
pātam atunammi yāḷu maracē.
[[((11.4.5))]]

$$1987

__&&1987__$$11.4.6##__

irunila maṉṉar tammi yirunālum
eṭṭu morunālu moṉṟu muṭaṉē,
ceruñuta lūṭu pōki yavarāvi
maṅka maḻuvāḷil veṉṟa tiṟalōṉ,
perunila maṅki maṉṉar malarmaṅki
nātar pulamaṅki kēḷvar pukaḻcēr,
perunila muṇṭu miḻnta peruvāya
rāki yavarnammi yāḷvar peritē.
[[((11.4.6))]]

$$1988

__&&1988__$$11.4.7##__

ilimali paḷḷi yeyti yitumāyam
eṉṉa iṉamāya māṉpiṉ eḻilcēr
alimali vēlka ṇāḷi yakalvippa
taṟko ruruvāya māṉi yāmiyā,
kolimali yeytu vitta koṭiyōṉ
ilaṅki poṭiyāka veṉṟi yamaruḷ,
cilimali ceñca raṅkaḷ celavuytta
naṅkaḷ tirumāl namakko raraṇē.
[[((11.4.7))]]

$$1989

__&&1989__$$11.4.8##__

muṉṉula kaṅka ḷēḻum iruḷmaṇṭi
yuṇṇa mutalōṭu vīṭu maṟiyātu,
eṉṉitu vanta teṉṉa imiyōr
tikippa eḻilvēta miṉṟi maṟiya,
piṉṉiyum vāṉa varkkum muṉivarkkum
nalki yiruḷtīrntiv viya makiḻa,
aṉṉama tāyi runtaṅ kaṟañūl
uritta atunammi yāḷu maracē.
[[((11.4.8))]]

$$1990

__&&1990__$$11.4.9##__

tuṇinili maṟṟe makko ruḷateṉ
ṟirātu toḻumiṉkaḷ toṇṭar toliya
uṇamuli muṅkoṭutta vuravōḷa
tāvi yukavuṇṭu veṇṇey maruvi,
paṇamuli yāyar māta ruralōṭu
kaṭṭa ataṉōṭu mōṭi aṭalcēr,
iṇimaru tiṟṟu vīḻa naṭikaṟṟa
teṟṟal viṉipaṟṟa ṟukkum vitiyē.
[[((11.4.9))]]

$$1991

__&&1991__$$11.4.10##__

kolikeḻu cemmu katta kaḷiṟoṉṟu
koṉṟu koṭiyōṉ ilaṅki poṭiyā
cilikeḻu ceñca raṅkaḷ celavuytta
naṅkaḷ tirumāli, vēli puṭicūḻ
kalikeḻu māṭa vīti vayalmaṅki
maṉṉu kalikaṉṟi coṉṉa paṉuval,
olikeḻu pāṭal pāṭi yuḻalkiṉṟa
toṇṭa ravarāḷva rumpa rulakē.
[[((11.4.10))]]

puḷḷāyōr ēṉamāyp pukkiṭantāṉ poṉṉaṭikkeṉṟu,
uḷḷātā ruḷḷatti yuḷḷamāk koḷḷōmē
[[((11.7.6))]]

$$2018

__&&2018__$$11.7.7##__

kaṉiyār kaṭalum karuviḷiyum kāyāvum
aṉiyāṉi, aṉpiṉāl ārvattāl, eṉṟum
cuṉiyār malariṭṭut toṇṭarāy niṉṟu,
niṉiyātār neñceṉṟum ceñcalla kaṇṭāmē
[[((11.7.7))]]

$$2019

__&&2019__$$11.7.8##__

veṟiyār karuṅkūntal āycciyar vitta
uṟiyār naṟuveṇṇey tāṉukan tuṇṭa
ciṟiyāṉi, ceṅka ṇeṭiyāṉic cintit
taṟiyātār, eṉṟum aṟiyātār kaṇṭāmē
[[((11.7.8))]]

$$2020

__&&2020__$$11.7.9##__

tēṉōṭu vaṇṭālum tirumā liruñcōli,
tāṉiṭamāk koṇṭāṉ taṭamalark kaṇṇikkāy,
āṉviṭiyē ḻaṉṟaṭarttāṟ kāḷāṉā rallātār,
māṉiṭavar allareṉ ṟeṉmaṉattē vittēṉē (2)
[[((11.7.9))]]

$$2021

__&&2021__$$11.7.10##__

meynniṉṟa pāvam akala, tirumālik
kinniṉṟa āḻiyāṉ cūḻum kaḻalcūṭi,
kinniṉṟa vēṟkik kaliya ṉolimāli,
aiyoṉṟu mintum ivipāṭi yāṭumiṉē (2)
[[((11.7.10))]]
kaliviruttam

2022__((PTM_11.8))

$$2022

__&&2022__$$11.8.1##__

māṟṟamuḷa vākilum colluvaṉ, makkaḷ
tōṟṟak kuḻitōṟṟu vippāyko leṉṟiṉṉam,
āṟṟaṅ karivāḻ marampōla añcukiṉṟēṉ,
nāṟṟañ cuviyū ṟoliyā kiyanampī. (2)
[[((11.8.1))]]

$$2023

__&&2023__$$11.8.2##__

cīṟṟamuḷa vākilum ceppuvaṉ, makkaḷ
tōṟṟak kuḻitōṟṟu vippāyko leṉṟañci,
kāṟṟat tiṭippaṭṭa kalavar maṉampōla,
āṟṟat tuḷaṅkā niṟpaṉā ḻivalavā.
[[((11.8.2))]]

$$2024

__&&2024__$$11.8.3##__

tūṅkār piṟavikka ḷiṉṉam pukappeytu,
vāṅkāyeṉṟu cintittu nāṉataṟ kañci,
pāmpō ṭorukū riyilē payiṉṟāṟpōl,
tāṅkātuḷ ḷamtaḷḷum eṉtā marikkaṇṇā.
[[((11.8.3))]]

$$2025

__&&2025__$$11.8.4##__

uruvār piṟavikka ḷiṉṉam pukappeytu,
tirivāyeṉṟu cintitti yeṉṟataṟ kañci,
irupā ṭerikoḷ ḷiyiṉuḷ eṟumpēpōl,
urukāniṟku meṉṉuḷḷam ūḻi mutalvā.
[[((11.8.4))]]

$$2026

__&&2026__$$11.8.5##__

koḷḷak kuṟiyāta iṭumpik kuḻiyil,
taḷḷi pukappeyti kolleṉ ṟataṟkañci,
veḷḷat tiṭippaṭṭa nariyiṉam pōlē,
uḷḷam tuḷaṅkāniṟpaṉ ūḻi mutalvā.
[[((11.8.5))]]

$$2027

__&&2027__$$11.8.6##__

paṭiniṉṟa pintā mariyōṭu aṇinīlam
maṭiniṉ ṟalarum vayalāli maṇāḷā,
iṭiyaṉ eṟinta maramēyot tirāmē,
aṭiya aruḷā yeṉakkuṉṟa ṉaruḷē
[[((11.8.6))]]

$$2028

__&&2028__$$11.8.7##__

vēmpiṉpuḻu vēmpiṉṟi yuṇṇātu, aṭiyēṉ
nāṉpiṉṉu muṉcē vaṭiyaṉṟi nayavēṉ,
tēmpaliḷan tiṅkaḷ ciṟiviṭuttu, aivāyp
pāmpiṉ aṇippaḷḷi koṇṭāy parañcōtī. (2)
[[((11.8.7))]]

$$2029

__&&2029__$$11.8.8##__

aṇiyār poḻilcūḻ araṅka nakarappā,
tuṇiyēṉ iṉiniṉ aruḷalla teṉakku,
maṇiyē. maṇimā ṇikkamē. matucūtā,
paṇiyā yeṉakkuy yumvaki, parañcōtī. (2)
[[((11.8.8))]]

$$2030

__&&2030__$$11.8.9##__

nantā narakat taḻuntā vaki,nāḷum
entāy. toṇṭarā ṉavarkkiṉ ṉaruḷceyvāy,
cantōkā. talivaṉē. tāmarik kaṇṇā,
antō. aṭiyēṟ karuḷāyuṉ ṉaruḷē (2)
[[((11.8.9))]]

$$2031

__&&2031__$$11.8.10##__

kuṉṟa meṭuttā nirikāt tavaṉṟaṉṉi,
maṉṟil pukaḻmaṅki maṉkali kaṉṟicol,
oṉṟu niṉṟavoṉ patumval lavarttammēl,
eṉṟum viṉiyāyiṉa cārakil lāvē (2)
[[((11.8.10))]]
